src/controllers/authController.js:

const User = require('../models/User');
const { generateToken, hashPassword, comparePassword } = require('../utils/auth');
const {
  generateResetCode,
  sendResetCodeEmail,
  sendEmailChangeCodeEmail
} = require('../utils/emailService');

// Register admin (untuk pertama kali)
const registerAdmin = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { username, password, email } = req.body;

    // Check if admin already exists
    const existingAdmin = await User.findOne({ role: 'admin' });
    if (existingAdmin) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Admin already registered'
      });
    }

    // Create new admin
    const hashedPassword = await hashPassword(password);
    
    const admin = new User({
      username,
      password: hashedPassword,
      email,
      role: 'admin'
    });

    await admin.save();

    // Generate token
    const token = generateToken(admin._id, admin.username, admin.role);

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Admin registered successfully',
      token,
      user: {
        id: admin._id,
        username: admin.username,
        role: admin.role,
        email: admin.email
      }
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Register error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Login
const login = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { username, password, role } = req.body;

    // Find user
    const user = await User.findOne({ username });
    if (!user) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid credentials'
      });
    }

    // Check if user is active
    if (!user.isActive) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Account is disabled'
      });
    }

    // Check role (admin harus login sebagai admin, user sebagai user)
    if (role && user.role !== role) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid role selection'
      });
    }

    // Verify password
    const isPasswordValid = await comparePassword(password, user.password);
    if (!isPasswordValid) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid credentials'
      });
    }

    // Update last login
    user.lastLogin = new Date();
    await user.save();

    // Generate token
    const token = generateToken(user._id, user.username, user.role);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Login successful',
      token,
      user: {
        id: user._id,
        username: user.username,
        role: user.role,
        email: user.email
      }
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Login error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Get current user profile
const getProfile = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id).select('-password');
    
    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      user
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Get profile error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error'
    });
  }
};

// Check if admin is registered (untuk frontend)
const checkAdminRegistered = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const admin = await User.findOne({ role: 'admin' });
    
    res.json({
      success: true,
      isRegistered: !!admin
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Check admin error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error'
    });
  }
};

const requestEmailChangeCode = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { newEmail } = req.body;

    if (!newEmail || !newEmail.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email baru wajib diisi'
      });
    }

    const email = newEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
    const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

    if (!emailRegex.test(email)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Format email tidak valid'
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    const currentEmail = (user.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();

    if (currentEmail === email) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email baru sama dengan email saat ini'
      });
    }

    const existingUser = await User.findOne({
      email,
      _id: { $ne: user._id }
    });

    if (existingUser) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email sudah digunakan akun lain'
      });
    }

    const code = generateResetCode();
    const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

    user.pendingEmail = email;
    user.emailChangeCode = code;
    user.emailChangeCodeExpiry = expiry;
    await user.save();

    try {
      await sendEmailChangeCodeEmail(email, code, user.username);
      console.log(`✅ Email change code sent to ${email}`);
    } catch (emailError) {
      console.error('❌ Email send error:', emailError);

      // FIXED: rollback — hapus semua field verifikasi agar user bisa
      // request ulang tanpa status "pendingEmail" yang menggantung.
      try {
        user.pendingEmail = undefined;
        user.emailChangeCode = undefined;
        user.emailChangeCodeExpiry = undefined;
        await user.save();
        console.log('↩️  Email change state rolled back');
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        console.error('❌ Rollback email change failed:', rollbackError.message);
      }

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gagal mengirim email. Periksa koneksi server atau coba lagi.'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Kode verifikasi telah dikirim ke email baru',
      email
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Request email change error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Update profile (username & email)
const updateProfile = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { username, email, emailChangeCode } = req.body;

    if (!username || !username.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Username is required'
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    const currentEmail = (user.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const requestedEmail = email !== undefined
      ? (email ? email.trim().toLowerCase() : '')
      : currentEmail;

    const emailChanged = requestedEmail !== currentEmail;

    // Validasi format email jika diisi
    if (requestedEmail) {
      const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
      if (!emailRegex.test(requestedEmail)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Invalid email format'
        });
      }
    }

    // Jika email berubah, wajib verifikasi kode
    if (emailChanged) {
      if (requestedEmail === '') {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Email baru wajib diisi'
        });
      }

      if (!emailChangeCode || !emailChangeCode.trim()) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi email wajib diisi'
        });
      }

      if (!user.pendingEmail || user.pendingEmail !== requestedEmail) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Silakan minta kode verifikasi untuk email baru terlebih dahulu'
        });
      }

      if (!user.emailChangeCode || !user.emailChangeCodeExpiry) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi tidak ditemukan. Silakan minta kode baru.'
        });
      }

      if (new Date() > new Date(user.emailChangeCodeExpiry)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi sudah kedaluwarsa. Silakan minta kode baru.'
        });
      }

      if (user.emailChangeCode !== emailChangeCode.trim()) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi salah'
        });
      }
    }

    // Cek username unik
    if (username.trim() !== user.username) {
      const existing = await User.findOne({
        username: username.trim(),
        _id: { $ne: user._id }
      });

      if (existing) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Username already taken'
        });
      }
    }

    user.username = username.trim();

    if (emailChanged) {
      user.email = requestedEmail;

      // Bersihkan data verifikasi
      user.pendingEmail = undefined;
      user.emailChangeCode = undefined;
      user.emailChangeCodeExpiry = undefined;
    } else if (email !== undefined) {
      user.email = requestedEmail;
    }

    await user.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Profile updated successfully',
      user: {
        id: user._id,
        username: user.username,
        email: user.email,
        role: user.role
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Update profile error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Change password
const changePassword = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { oldPassword, newPassword } = req.body;

    if (!oldPassword || !newPassword) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Old and new password are required'
      });
    }

    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password must be at least 8 characters'
      });
    }

    if (!/^(?=.*[a-zA-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(newPassword)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password must contain both letters and numbers'
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);
    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    const isValid = await comparePassword(oldPassword, user.password);
    if (!isValid) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Old password is incorrect'
      });
    }

    if (oldPassword === newPassword) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'New password must be different from old password'
      });
    }

    user.password = await hashPassword(newPassword);
    await user.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Password changed successfully'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Change password error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// ============ FITUR LUPA PASSWORD ============

// 1. Request reset password (kirim kode ke email)
const requestPasswordReset = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email } = req.body;

    if (!email || !email.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email wajib diisi',
      });
    }

    // Cari admin dengan email tersebut
    const user = await User.findOne({
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      role: 'admin',
    });

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email tidak terdaftar sebagai admin',
      });
    }

    // Generate kode 6-digit
    const code = generateResetCode();
    const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000); // 15 menit

    user.resetPasswordCode = code;
    user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = expiry;
    await user.save();

    // Kirim email
    try {
      await sendResetCodeEmail(user.email, code, user.username);
      console.log(`✅ Reset code sent to ${user.email}`);
    } catch (emailError) {
      console.error('❌ Email send error:', emailError);

      // FIXED: rollback kode reset agar tidak menggantung saat email gagal.
      try {
        user.resetPasswordCode = undefined;
        user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = undefined;
        await user.save();
        console.log('↩️  Reset password state rolled back');
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        console.error('❌ Rollback reset password failed:', rollbackError.message);
      }

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gagal mengirim email. Periksa koneksi server atau coba lagi.',
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Kode verifikasi telah dikirim ke email Anda',
      email: user.email,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Request password reset error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
};

// 2. Reset password dengan kode verifikasi
const resetPasswordWithCode = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email, code, newPassword } = req.body;

    if (!email || !code || !newPassword) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email, kode, dan password baru wajib diisi',
      });
    }

    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password minimal 8 karakter',
      });
    }

    if (!/^(?=.*[a-zA-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(newPassword)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password harus mengandung huruf dan angka',
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findOne({
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      role: 'admin',
    });

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email tidak terdaftar sebagai admin',
      });
    }

    if (!user.resetPasswordCode || !user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Kode verifikasi tidak ditemukan. Silakan minta kode baru.',
      });
    }

    if (new Date() > new Date(user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Kode sudah kedaluwarsa. Silakan minta kode baru.',
      });
    }

    if (user.resetPasswordCode !== code.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Kode verifikasi salah',
      });
    }

    // Update password
    user.password = await hashPassword(newPassword);
    user.resetPasswordCode = undefined;
    user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = undefined;
    await user.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Password berhasil direset',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Reset password error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
};

// 3. Kirim ulang kode
const resendResetCode = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email } = req.body;

    if (!email || !email.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email wajib diisi',
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findOne({
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      role: 'admin',
    });

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email tidak terdaftar sebagai admin',
      });
    }

    // Generate kode baru
    const code = generateResetCode();
    const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

    user.resetPasswordCode = code;
    user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = expiry;
    await user.save();

    try {
      await sendResetCodeEmail(user.email, code, user.username);
      console.log(`✅ Reset code resent to ${user.email}`);
    } catch (emailError) {
      console.error('❌ Email send error:', emailError);

      // FIXED: rollback kode reset agar tidak menggantung saat email gagal.
      try {
        user.resetPasswordCode = undefined;
        user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = undefined;
        await user.save();
        console.log('↩️  Resend reset code state rolled back');
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        console.error('❌ Rollback resend failed:', rollbackError.message);
      }

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gagal mengirim email. Coba lagi nanti.',
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Kode verifikasi baru telah dikirim ke email',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Resend code error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
};



module.exports = {
  registerAdmin,
  login,
  getProfile,
  checkAdminRegistered,
  updateProfile,
  changePassword,
  requestPasswordReset,
  resetPasswordWithCode,
  resendResetCode,
  requestEmailChangeCode,
};

src/middleware/authMiddleware.js:

const { verifyToken } = require('../utils/auth');

const authMiddleware = (req, res, next) => {
  // Get token from header
  const token = req.header('Authorization')?.replace('Bearer ', '');

  if (!token) {
    return res.status(401).json({ 
      success: false, 
      message: 'Access denied. No token provided.' 
    });
  }

  try {
    // Verify token
    const decoded = verifyToken(token);
    
    if (!decoded) {
      return res.status(401).json({ 
        success: false, 
        message: 'Invalid or expired token.' 
      });
    }

    // Add user info to request
    req.user = decoded;
    next();
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(401).json({ 
      success: false, 
      message: 'Invalid token.' 
    });
  }
};

const adminMiddleware = (req, res, next) => {
  if (req.user.role !== 'admin') {
    return res.status(403).json({ 
      success: false, 
      message: 'Access denied. Admin only.' 
    });
  }
  next();
};

module.exports = { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware };

  name: {
    type: String,
    required: true,
    trim: true
  },
  code: {
    type: String,
    unique: true,
    sparse: true, // ✅ FIX: izinkan banyak dokumen tanpa `code`
    trim: true,
    uppercase: true
  },
  description: {
    type: String,
    trim: true
  },
  roomCount: {
    type: Number,
    default: 0
  },
  createdAt: {
    type: Date,
    default: Date.now,
    immutable: true
  },
  updatedAt: {
    type: Date,
    default: Date.now
  }
});

src/models/Room.js:

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const roomSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  name: {
    type: String,
    required: true,
    trim: true
  },
  building: {
    type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
    ref: 'Building',
    required: true
  },
  buildingName: {
    type: String,
    required: true
  },
  dataSource: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['simulation', 'iot'],
    default: 'simulation'
  },
  iotDeviceId: {
    type: String,
    default: null
  },
  // FIXED: currentAQI nullable — null berarti "belum ada data valid".
  //   - null  → UI tampilkan card abu-abu "Tidak Ada Data"
  //   - 0..500 → nilai AQI normal
  currentAQI: {
    type: Number,
    default: null
  },
  // FIXED: semua field nullable. null berarti "tidak dikirim device".
  currentData: {
    pm25: { type: Number, default: null },
    pm10: { type: Number, default: null },
    co: { type: Number, default: null },
    so2: { type: Number, default: null },
    o3: { type: Number, default: null },
    no2: { type: Number, default: null },
    temperature: { type: Number, default: null },
    humidity: { type: Number, default: null },
    updatedAt: { type: Date }
  },
  demoMode: {
    type: Boolean,
    default: false
  },
  createdAt: {
    type: Date,
    default: Date.now
  },
  updatedAt: {
    type: Date,
    default: Date.now
  }
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('Room', roomSchema);

src/models/SensorData.js:

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const sensorDataSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  roomId: {
    type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
    ref: 'Room',
    required: true
  },
  roomName: { type: String, required: true },
  buildingName: { type: String, required: true },
  aqi: { type: Number, default: null },
  pm25: { type: Number, default: null },
  pm10: { type: Number, default: null },
  co: { type: Number, default: null },
  so2: { type: Number, default: null },
  o3: { type: Number, default: null },
  no2: { type: Number, default: null },
  temperature: { type: Number, default: null },
  humidity: { type: Number, default: null },
  category: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['baik', 'sedang', 'tidak_sehat', 'sangat_tidak_sehat', 'berbahaya', 'error', 'no_data'],
    required: true
  },
  
  isAnomaly: {
    type: Boolean,
    default: false,
    index: true,
  },
  anomalyType: {
    type: String,
    default: null,
  },

  timestamp: {
    type: Date,
    default: Date.now,
    index: true
  }
});

sensorDataSchema.index({ roomId: 1, timestamp: -1 });
sensorDataSchema.index({ timestamp: -1 });
sensorDataSchema.index({ roomId: 1, timestamp: 1, isAnomaly: 1 });

module.exports = mongoose.model('SensorData', sensorDataSchema);

src/models/User.js:

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  username: {
    type: String,
    required: true,
    unique: true,
    trim: true
  },
  password: {
    type: String,
    required: true
  },
  role: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['admin', 'user'],
    default: 'user'
  },
  email: {
    type: String,
    trim: true,
    lowercase: true
  },
  pendingEmail: {
  type: String,
  trim: true,
  lowercase: true
},
emailChangeCode: {
  type: String
},
emailChangeCodeExpiry: {
  type: Date
},
  isActive: {
    type: Boolean,
    default: true
  },
  createdAt: {
    type: Date,
    default: Date.now
  },
  lastLogin: {
    type: Date
  },

  resetPasswordCode: { type: String },
  resetPasswordCodeExpiry: { type: Date },

});

module.exports = mongoose.model('User', userSchema);

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();

const {
  registerAdmin,
  login,
  getProfile,
  checkAdminRegistered,
  updateProfile,
  changePassword,
  requestPasswordReset,
  resetPasswordWithCode,
  resendResetCode,
  requestEmailChangeCode,
} = require('../controllers/authController');

const { authMiddleware } = require('../middleware/authMiddleware');

// Public routes
router.get('/check-admin', checkAdminRegistered);
router.post('/register-admin', registerAdmin);
router.post('/login', login);

router.post('/forgot-password', requestPasswordReset);
router.post('/reset-password', resetPasswordWithCode);
router.post('/resend-reset-code', resendResetCode);

// Protected routes
router.get('/profile', authMiddleware, getProfile);
router.put('/profile', authMiddleware, updateProfile);
router.put('/change-password', authMiddleware, changePassword);

// Verifikasi email baru (protected)
router.post('/request-email-change', authMiddleware, requestEmailChangeCode);

src/routes/buildingRoutes.js

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const Building = require('../models/Building');
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware } = require('../middleware/authMiddleware');

// GET all buildings (public)
router.get('/', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const buildings = await Building.find().sort({ name: 1 });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      count: buildings.length,
      data: buildings
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching buildings:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memuat data gedung'
    });
  }
});

// GET single building (public)
router.get('/:id', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const building = await Building.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gedung tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: building
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching building:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memuat data gedung'
    });
  }
});

// POST create building (admin only)
router.post('/', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, code, description } = req.body;

    if (!name || !name.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama gedung wajib diisi'
      });
    }

    // Cek duplikat code HANYA jika code diisi
    if (code && code.trim()) {
      const existingCode = await Building.findOne({
        code: code.toUpperCase().trim()
      });
      if (existingCode) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
        });
      }
    }

    // ✅ FIX: jangan set field `code` sama sekali jika kosong
    const buildingData = {
      name: name.trim(),
      roomCount: 0,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date()
    };

    if (code && code.trim()) {
      buildingData.code = code.toUpperCase().trim();
    }
    if (description && description.trim()) {
      buildingData.description = description.trim();
    }

    const building = new Building(buildingData);
    await building.save();

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Gedung berhasil dibuat',
      data: building
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating building:', error);

    if (error.code === 11000) {
      const dupField = Object.keys(error.keyPattern || {})[0];
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: dupField === 'code'
          ? 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
          : 'Data gedung sudah ada'
      });
    }

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal membuat gedung. Silakan coba lagi.'
    });
  }
});

// PUT update building (admin only)
router.put('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, code, description } = req.body;

    const building = await Building.findById(req.params.id);
    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gedung tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    const oldName = building.name;

    // Cek duplikat code jika berubah
    if (code && code.trim() && code.toUpperCase().trim() !== building.code) {
      const existing = await Building.findOne({
        code: code.toUpperCase().trim(),
        _id: { $ne: building._id }
      });
      if (existing) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name && name.trim()) building.name = name.trim();

    if (code !== undefined) {
      if (code && code.trim()) {
        building.code = code.toUpperCase().trim();
      } else {
        // ✅ FIX: benar-benar unset field agar tidak tersimpan sebagai `null`
        building.set('code', undefined);
      }
    }

    if (description !== undefined) {
      building.description = description && description.trim()
        ? description.trim()
        : undefined;
    }
    building.updatedAt = new Date();

    await building.save();

    // Sinkronkan buildingName di semua Room terkait saat nama berubah
    if (oldName !== building.name) {
      await Room.updateMany(
        { building: building._id },
        { $set: { buildingName: building.name } }
      );
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Gedung berhasil diperbarui',
      data: building
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating building:', error);

    if (error.code === 11000) {
      const dupField = Object.keys(error.keyPattern || {})[0];
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: dupField === 'code'
          ? 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
          : 'Data gedung sudah ada'
      });
    }

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memperbarui gedung. Silakan coba lagi.'
    });
  }
});

// DELETE building (admin only)
router.delete('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const building = await Building.findById(req.params.id);
    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gedung tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    // Cegah hapus gedung yang masih memiliki ruangan
    const roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: building._id });
    if (roomCount > 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Gedung tidak dapat dihapus karena masih memiliki ${roomCount} ruangan`
      });
    }

    await building.deleteOne();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Gedung berhasil dihapus'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting building:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal menghapus gedung. Silakan coba lagi.'
    });
  }
});

module.exports = router;

C:\develop\UNILA-AQI\unila-aqi-backend\src\routes\iotDeviceRoutes.js

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const IoTDevice = require('../models/IoTDevice');
const { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware } = require('../middleware/authMiddleware');

// GET all IoT devices
router.get('/', authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const live = req.query.live === 'true';

    let devices = await IoTDevice.find().sort({ name: 1 });

    // ✅ Bila ?live=true → lakukan quick-check status online/offline
    // sebelum mengembalikan data, sehingga status di frontend selalu fresh.
    if (live && devices.length > 0) {
      try {
        const iotService = require('../services/iotService');
        await iotService.checkDeviceStatus(devices);

        // Re-fetch untuk dapat status yang sudah di-update
        devices = await IoTDevice.find().sort({ name: 1 });
      } catch (checkErr) {
        console.warn('⚠️ Live status check failed:', checkErr.message);
        // Tetap lanjut dengan data cached bila check gagal
      }
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      count: devices.length,
      data: devices
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching IoT devices',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// GET single IoT device
router.get('/:id', authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT device not found'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching IoT device',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// POST create IoT device (admin only)
router.post('/', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, description, apiEndpoint } = req.body;

    if (!name) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Device name is required'
      });
    }

    if (!apiEndpoint) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'API endpoint is required'
      });
    }

    try {
      new URL(apiEndpoint);
    } catch (error) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid API endpoint URL format'
      });
    }

    const existingDevice = await IoTDevice.findOne({ name: name.trim() });
    if (existingDevice) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama Device ini sudah ada, silahkan ganti nama lain'
      });
    }

    const device = new IoTDevice({
      name: name.trim(),
      description: description?.trim(),
      apiEndpoint: apiEndpoint.trim(),
      status: 'offline',
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date()
    });

    await device.save();

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'IoT device created successfully',
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error creating IoT device',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// PUT update IoT device (admin only)
router.put('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, description, apiEndpoint } = req.body;

    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT device not found'
      });
    }

    if (apiEndpoint) {
      try {
        new URL(apiEndpoint);
      } catch (error) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Invalid API endpoint URL format'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name && name !== device.name) {
      const existingDevice = await IoTDevice.findOne({
        name: name.trim(),
        _id: { $ne: device._id }
      });

      if (existingDevice) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Nama Device ini sudah ada, silahkan ganti nama lain'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name) device.name = name.trim();
    if (description !== undefined) device.description = description?.trim();
    if (apiEndpoint) device.apiEndpoint = apiEndpoint.trim();
    device.updatedAt = new Date();

    await device.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'IoT device updated successfully',
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error updating IoT device',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// DELETE IoT device (admin only)
router.delete('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT device not found'
      });
    }

    const Room = require('../models/Room');
    const roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ iotDeviceId: device._id.toString() });

    if (roomCount > 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Cannot delete device that is being used by rooms',
        roomCount: roomCount
      });
    }

    await device.deleteOne();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'IoT device deleted successfully'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error deleting IoT device',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// PATCH update device status
router.patch('/:id/status', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { status, lastUpdate } = req.body;

    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT device not found'
      });
    }

    const validStatuses = ['online', 'offline'];
    if (status && !validStatuses.includes(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid status. Must be online or offline'
      });
    }

    if (status) device.status = status;
    if (lastUpdate) device.lastUpdate = new Date(lastUpdate);
    device.updatedAt = new Date();

    await device.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Device status updated successfully',
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error updating device status',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});


router.post('/force-poll', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const iotService = require('../services/iotService');
    const result = await iotService.forcePollAll();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Force polling initiated',
      ...result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error forcing poll',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

router.get('/service/status', authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const iotService = require('../services/iotService');
    const simulationService = require('../services/simulationService');

    const iotStatus = iotService.getStatus();
    const simulationStatus = simulationService.getStatus();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        iot: iotStatus,
        simulation: simulationStatus,
        summary: {
          iotRunning: iotStatus.isRunning,
          simulationRunning: simulationStatus.isRunning,
          totalServices: (iotStatus.isRunning ? 1 : 0) + (simulationStatus.isRunning ? 1 : 0)
        }
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error getting service status',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

module.exports = router;

src/routes/roomRoutes.js

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const Building = require('../models/Building');
const SensorData = require('../models/SensorData');
const { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware } = require('../middleware/authMiddleware');
const {
  getAQICategory,
  aqiToPm25,
  aqiToSO2,
  aqiToO3,
  aqiToNO2,
} = require('../utils/aqiCalculator');

const broadcastRoomUpdate = (io, room, action, oldData = null) => {
  if (io) {
    const updateData = {
      action: action,
      room: {
        id: room._id,
        name: room.name,
        buildingId: room.building,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        dataSource: room.dataSource,
        iotDeviceId: room.iotDeviceId,
        currentAQI: room.currentAQI,
        currentData: room.currentData,
        createdAt: room.createdAt,
        updatedAt: room.updatedAt
      },
      oldData: oldData,
      timestamp: new Date()
    };

    io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-updated', updateData);
    io.emit('dashboard-room-updated', updateData);

    if (oldData) {
      if (oldData.name !== room.name) {
        console.log(`🔄 Broadcasting room name change: ${oldData.name} -> ${room.name}`);

        io.emit('room-name-changed', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldName: oldData.name,
          newName: room.name,
          buildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });

        io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-name-updated', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldName: oldData.name,
          newName: room.name,
          buildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }

      if (oldData.buildingId !== room.building.toString()) {
        io.emit('room-building-changed', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldBuildingId: oldData.buildingId,
          newBuildingId: room.building,
          oldBuildingName: oldData.buildingName,
          newBuildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }
    }

    console.log(`📢 Broadcast room ${action}: ${room.name} (${room._id})`);
  }
};

// GET all rooms
router.get('/', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const rooms = await Room.find()
      .populate('building', 'name code')
      .sort({ name: 1 });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      count: rooms.length,
      data: rooms
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching rooms',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// ============================================================
// DEMO ENDPOINTS — untuk sidang/demo agar dosen bisa melihat
// semua kategori AQI (termasuk "Tidak Sehat" s/d "Berbahaya")
// secara deterministik tanpa menunggu random.
// ============================================================

// POST /demo-cycle — set beberapa ruangan ke kategori berbeda sekaligus.
// Berguna untuk menunjukkan semua warna AQI dalam 1 dashboard.
router.post('/demo-cycle', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const targets = [
      { aqi: 25,  label: 'Baik' },
      { aqi: 75,  label: 'Sedang' },
      { aqi: 150, label: 'Tidak Sehat' },
      { aqi: 250, label: 'Sangat Tidak Sehat' },
      { aqi: 400, label: 'Berbahaya' },
    ];

    const rooms = await Room.find().sort({ name: 1 }).limit(targets.length);

    if (rooms.length === 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Belum ada ruangan. Tambahkan ruangan terlebih dahulu.'
      });
    }

    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const results = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < rooms.length && i < targets.length; i++) {
      const target = targets[i];
      const room = rooms[i];

       const pm25 = aqiToPm25(target.aqi);
      const pm10 = Math.round(pm25 * 1.5);
      const co = target.aqi > 200 ? 20000 : (target.aqi > 100 ? 12000 : 2000);
      const so2 = aqiToSO2(target.aqi);
      const o3 = aqiToO3(target.aqi);
      const no2 = aqiToNO2(target.aqi);
      const temperature = target.aqi > 200 ? 33 : (target.aqi > 100 ? 30 : 26);
      const humidity = target.aqi > 200 ? 80 : (target.aqi > 100 ? 70 : 55);

      room.demoMode = true;
      room.currentAQI = target.aqi;
      room.currentData = {
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      };
      room.updatedAt = new Date();
      await room.save();

      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: target.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: getAQICategory(target.aqi).category,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      }).save();

      if (io) {
        io.emit('dashboard-update', {
          type: 'room-data-updated',
          roomId: room._id,
          aqi: target.aqi,
          building: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }

      results.push({
        roomId: room._id,
        room: room.name,
        building: room.buildingName,
        aqi: target.aqi,
        category: getAQICategory(target.aqi).category,
        label: target.label,
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Demo cycle diterapkan ke ${results.length} ruangan`,
      results
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Demo cycle error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: error.message });
  }
});

// POST /demo-aqi — set 1 ruangan ke AQI tertentu (persist sampai reset)
router.post('/:id/demo-aqi', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { targetAqi } = req.body;
    const target = parseInt(targetAqi, 10);

    if (isNaN(target) || target < 0 || target > 500) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'targetAqi harus bilangan 0-500'
      });
    }

    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id);
    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

        const pm25 = aqiToPm25(target);
    const pm10 = Math.round(pm25 * 1.5);
    const co = target > 200 ? 20000 : (target > 100 ? 12000 : 2000);
    const so2 = aqiToSO2(target);
    const o3 = aqiToO3(target);
    const no2 = aqiToNO2(target);
    const temperature = target > 200 ? 33 : (target > 100 ? 30 : 26);
    const humidity = target > 200 ? 80 : (target > 100 ? 70 : 55);

    room.demoMode = true;
    room.currentAQI = target;
    room.currentData = {
      pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
      temperature, humidity,
      updatedAt: new Date()
    };
    room.updatedAt = new Date();
    await room.save();

    await new SensorData({
      roomId: room._id,
      roomName: room.name,
      buildingName: room.buildingName,
      aqi: target,
      pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
      temperature, humidity,
      category: getAQICategory(target).category,
      timestamp: new Date(),
    }).save();

    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    if (io) {
      io.emit('dashboard-update', {
        type: 'room-data-updated',
        roomId: room._id,
        aqi: target,
        building: room.buildingName,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Demo AQI diset ke ${target} untuk ${room.name}`,
      data: {
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        aqi: target,
        category: getAQICategory(target).category,
        pm25, pm10, co, temperature, humidity
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Demo AQI error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: error.message });
  }
});

// POST /:id/reset-demo — matikan demoMode, room akan di-update normal
// oleh simulationService / iotService pada tick berikutnya
router.post('/:id/reset-demo', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id);
    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    room.demoMode = false;
    room.updatedAt = new Date();
    await room.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Demo mode untuk ${room.name} dimatikan. Data akan update otomatis.`,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: error.message });
  }
});

// GET single room
router.get('/:id', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id)
      .populate('building', 'name code');

    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Room not found'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: room
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching room',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// POST create room (admin only)
router.post('/', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, buildingId, dataSource, iotDeviceId } = req.body;
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');

    if (!name) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Room name is required' });
    }

    if (!buildingId) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Building ID is required' });
    }

    const building = await Building.findById(buildingId);
    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Building not found' });
    }

    const validDataSources = ['simulation', 'iot'];
    if (dataSource && !validDataSources.includes(dataSource)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid data source. Must be "simulation" or "iot"'
      });
    }

    if (dataSource === 'iot' && !iotDeviceId) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT Device ID is required for IoT data source'
      });
    }

    const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({ name: name.trim(), building: buildingId });
    if (existingRoom) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama ruangan sudah ada di gedung ini, silahkan ganti nama atau pilih gedung lain'
      });
    }

    const room = new Room({
      name: name.trim(),
      building: buildingId,
      buildingName: building.name,
      dataSource: dataSource || 'simulation',
      iotDeviceId: dataSource === 'iot' ? iotDeviceId : null,
      currentAQI: null,        // ✅ no_data sampai poll pertama
      currentData: {
        pm25: null, pm10: null, co: null,
        so2: null, o3: null, no2: null,
        temperature: null, humidity: null,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      },
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date()
    });

    await room.save();

    building.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: buildingId });
    await building.save();

    if (io) broadcastRoomUpdate(io, room, 'created');

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Room created successfully',
      data: room
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error creating room',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// PUT update room (admin only)
router.put('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, buildingId, dataSource, iotDeviceId } = req.body;
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');

    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id).populate('building', 'name');
    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    const oldData = {
      name: room.name,
      buildingId: room.building._id ? room.building._id.toString() : room.building.toString(),
      buildingName: room.buildingName,
      dataSource: room.dataSource,
      iotDeviceId: room.iotDeviceId || null
    };

    console.log(`🔄 Room update request for: ${room.name}`);

    const validDataSources = ['simulation', 'iot'];
    if (dataSource && !validDataSources.includes(dataSource)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid data source. Must be "simulation" or "iot"'
      });
    }

    if (dataSource === 'iot' && !iotDeviceId) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT Device ID is required for IoT data source'
      });
    }

    let buildingChanged = false;
    let oldBuildingId = null;
    let newBuilding = null;

    if (buildingId && buildingId !== room.building.toString()) {
      newBuilding = await Building.findById(buildingId);
      if (!newBuilding) {
        return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Building not found' });
      }

      const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({
        name: name || room.name,
        building: buildingId,
        _id: { $ne: room._id }
      });

      if (existingRoom) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Room name already exists in the new building'
        });
      }

      oldBuildingId = room.building;
      room.building = buildingId;
      room.buildingName = newBuilding.name;
      buildingChanged = true;
    } else if (name && name !== room.name) {
      const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({
        name: name,
        building: room.building,
        _id: { $ne: room._id }
      });

      if (existingRoom) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Nama ruangan sudah ada di gedung ini, silahkan ganti nama atau pilih gedung lain'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name) {
      room.name = name.trim();
    }

    const prevDataSource = oldData.dataSource;
    const prevIotDeviceId = oldData.iotDeviceId || null;

    if (dataSource !== undefined) {
      room.dataSource = dataSource;
      room.iotDeviceId = dataSource === 'iot' ? iotDeviceId : null;
    }
    if (iotDeviceId !== undefined && dataSource === 'iot') {
      room.iotDeviceId = iotDeviceId;
    }
    room.updatedAt = new Date();

    const deviceChanged =
      (prevDataSource !== room.dataSource) ||
      (prevIotDeviceId !== (room.iotDeviceId || null));

    if (deviceChanged) {
      room.currentAQI = null;
      room.currentData = {
        pm25: null, pm10: null, co: null,
        so2: null, o3: null, no2: null,
        temperature: null, humidity: null,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      };
      console.log(
        `🔄 Room "${room.name}" device changed (${prevIotDeviceId} → ${room.iotDeviceId}). Data direset.`
      );
    }

    if (!buildingChanged) {
      const currentBuilding = await Building.findById(room.building);
      if (currentBuilding && room.buildingName !== currentBuilding.name) {
        room.buildingName = currentBuilding.name;
      }
    }

    await room.save();

    let deviceFetchStatus = null;

    if (deviceChanged && room.dataSource === 'iot' && room.iotDeviceId) {
      try {
        const iotService = require('../services/iotService');
        const fetchResult = await iotService.forceUpdateRoom(room._id.toString());

        if (fetchResult.success) {
          deviceFetchStatus = 'success';

          const refreshed = await Room.findById(room._id).populate('building', 'name');
          if (refreshed) {
            room.currentAQI = refreshed.currentAQI;
            room.currentData = refreshed.currentData;
            room.updatedAt = refreshed.updatedAt;
          }

          console.log(`✅ Device baru langsung fetch: AQI ${room.currentAQI}`);
        } else {
          deviceFetchStatus = 'failed';
          console.warn(
            `⚠️  Force-fetch device baru gagal: ${fetchResult.message}. Data akan diupdate pada poll berikutnya.`
          );
        }
      } catch (iotError) {
        deviceFetchStatus = 'error';
        console.error('⚠️  Error force-fetch device baru:', iotError.message);
      }
    }

    if (buildingChanged) {
      if (oldBuildingId) {
        const oldBuilding = await Building.findById(oldBuildingId);
        if (oldBuilding) {
          oldBuilding.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: oldBuildingId });
          await oldBuilding.save();

          if (io) {
            io.emit('building-updated', {
              action: 'room-removed',
              buildingId: oldBuildingId,
              roomId: room._id,
              roomName: room.name,
              timestamp: new Date()
            });
          }
        }
      }

      if (newBuilding) {
        newBuilding.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: buildingId });
        await newBuilding.save();

        if (io) {
          io.emit('building-updated', {
            action: 'room-added',
            buildingId: buildingId,
            roomId: room._id,
            roomName: room.name,
            timestamp: new Date()
          });
        }
      }
    } else {
      const currentBuilding = await Building.findById(room.building);
      if (currentBuilding) {
        currentBuilding.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: room.building });
        await currentBuilding.save();
      }
    }

    if (io) {
      broadcastRoomUpdate(io, room, 'updated', oldData);

      if (name && name !== oldData.name) {
        io.emit('room-name-changed-immediate', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldName: oldData.name,
          newName: room.name,
          buildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }
    }

    const changes = {
      nameChanged: name && name !== oldData.name,
      buildingChanged: buildingChanged,
      dataSourceChanged: dataSource && dataSource !== oldData.dataSource,
      deviceChanged: deviceChanged,
      deviceFetchStatus: deviceFetchStatus
    };

    let responseMessage = 'Room updated successfully';
    if (deviceChanged && room.dataSource === 'iot') {
      if (deviceFetchStatus === 'success') {
        responseMessage = 'Room updated & data partikel device baru berhasil dimuat.';
      } else if (deviceFetchStatus === 'failed' || deviceFetchStatus === 'error') {
        responseMessage = 'Room updated. Data partikel akan dimuat pada poll berikutnya karena device baru tidak merespons.';
      } else {
        responseMessage = 'Room updated.';
      }
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: responseMessage,
      data: room,
      changes: changes
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error updating room:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error updating room',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// DELETE room (admin only)
router.delete('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    const buildingId = room.building;
    const roomData = {
      id: room._id,
      name: room.name,
      buildingName: room.buildingName
    };

    await room.deleteOne();

    const building = await Building.findById(buildingId);
    if (building) {
      building.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: buildingId });
      await building.save();

      if (io) {
        io.emit('building-updated', {
          action: 'room-deleted',
          buildingId: buildingId,
          room: roomData,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }
    }

    if (io) {
      io.emit('room-deleted', {
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });

      io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-updated', {
        action: 'deleted',
        room: roomData,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });
    }

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Room deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error deleting room',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// Force refresh
router.post('/:id/force-refresh', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    if (io) {
      io.emit('room-force-refresh', {
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });

      io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-refresh-requested', {
        timestamp: new Date()
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Force refresh broadcasted',
      roomId: room._id,
      roomName: room.name
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error forcing refresh',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// Sync building name
router.post('/:id/sync-building', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id).populate('building', 'name');

    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    const oldBuildingName = room.buildingName;
    const newBuildingName = room.building.name;

    if (oldBuildingName !== newBuildingName) {
      room.buildingName = newBuildingName;
      room.updatedAt = new Date();
      await room.save();

      if (io) {
        io.emit('room-building-synced', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldBuildingName: oldBuildingName,
          newBuildingName: newBuildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }

      res.json({
        success: true,
        message: 'Building name synced',
        oldBuildingName,
        newBuildingName
      });
    } else {
      res.json({
        success: true,
        message: 'Building name already synced',
        buildingName: newBuildingName
      });
    }
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error syncing building name',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

module.exports = router;

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
sensorDataRoutes.js

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const SensorData = require('../models/SensorData');

// Helper function untuk convert string ke ObjectId
const toObjectId = (id) => {
  try {
    return new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id);
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
};

// GET sensor data for a room dengan data aggregation
// FIXED: route dibuat PUBLIC (authMiddleware dihapus).
// Alasan:
//  - Data historis 24 jam ini identik dengan data live yang sudah tampil
//    di dashboard publik (endpoint /rooms juga public).
//  - Visitor (tanpa login) harus bisa melihat grafik historis ruangan,
//    sama seperti admin.
//  - Sebelumnya endpoint butuh token → visitor mendapat 401 → grafik
//    kosong padahal admin normal.
router.get('/:roomId', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { roomId } = req.params;

    // Validasi roomId
    if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(roomId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid room ID format'
      });
    }

    // Ambil data 24 jam terakhir
    const now = new Date();
    const dateFilter = { timestamp: { $gte: new Date(now - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) } };

    // Ambil SEMUA data 24 jam (tanpa limit)
    const sensorData = await SensorData.find({
      roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
      ...dateFilter
    })
    .sort({ timestamp: 1 });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: sensorData,
      count: sensorData.length,
      message: '24 hours data loaded'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching sensor data:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching sensor data',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// GET sensor data by date untuk grafik history
// FIXED (2026): route ini sekarang mengambil data TEPAT di kelipatan
// interval (default 30 menit) dengan menggunakan $first setelah $sort,
// sehingga tiap titik merepresentasikan nilai pada awal slot
// (14:00, 14:30, 15:00, 15:30, dst) — bukan nilai di akhir slot
// (yang lebih dekat ke 14:29 / 14:59).
//
// Pipeline:
//   1. $match      — filter roomId + rentang tanggal
//   2. $addFields  — hitung slot interval (0 atau 30 untuk interval=30)
//   3. $sort       — urutkan ascending (WAJIB untuk $first yang deterministik)
//   4. $group      — per (hour, interval), ambil $first (= data di awal slot)
//   5. $sort       — urutkan hasil akhir secara kronologis
//   6. $project    — format timeLabel "HH:MM" + field yang dibutuhkan chart
router.get('/:roomId/history', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { roomId } = req.params;
    const { date, interval = 30 } = req.query; // interval dalam menit

    // Validasi roomId
    if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(roomId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid room ID format'
      });
    }

    // Parse tanggal
    let startDate, endDate;
    if (date) {
      // Jika ada tanggal spesifik
      const selectedDate = new Date(date);
      startDate = new Date(selectedDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0));
      endDate = new Date(selectedDate.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999));
    } else {
      // Default: hari ini
      const today = new Date();
      startDate = new Date(today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0));
      endDate = new Date(today.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999));
    }

    const intervalNum = parseInt(interval);

    console.log(
      `📊 Fetching history data for room ${roomId} from ${startDate} to ${endDate} (interval: ${intervalNum}m)`
    );

    // Aggregation pipeline untuk data per interval
    const aggregationPipeline = [
      {
        $match: {
          roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
          timestamp: { $gte: startDate, $lte: endDate }
        }
      },
      {
        $addFields: {
          // Buat interval waktu (misal: 30 menit)
          // minute 0-29 → 0, minute 30-59 → 30 (untuk interval=30)
          timeInterval: {
            $subtract: [
              { $minute: "$timestamp" },
              { $mod: [{ $minute: "$timestamp" }, intervalNum] }
            ]
          }
        }
      },
      // FIXED: sort ascending SEBELUM $group, agar $first di bawah
      // menghasilkan nilai TEPAT di awal slot (menit :00 / :30).
      // Tanpa sort, $first mengembalikan dokumen non-deterministik.
      {
        $sort: { "timestamp": 1 }
      },
      {
        $group: {
          _id: {
            hour: { $hour: "$timestamp" },
            interval: "$timeInterval"
          },
          timestamp: { $first: "$timestamp" },
          aqi: { $first: "$aqi" },
          pm25: { $first: "$pm25" },
          pm10: { $first: "$pm10" },
          co: { $first: "$co" },
          so2: { $first: "$so2" },
          o3: { $first: "$o3" },
          no2: { $first: "$no2" },
          temperature: { $first: "$temperature" },
          humidity: { $first: "$humidity" },
          category: { $first: "$category" },
          rawData: { $push: "$$ROOT" }
        }
      },
      {
        $sort: { "timestamp": 1 }
      },
      {
        $project: {
          _id: 0,
          timestamp: 1,
          hour: "$_id.hour",
          interval: "$_id.interval",
          timeLabel: {
            $concat: [
              { $toString: "$_id.hour" },
              ":",
              {
                $cond: {
                  if: { $lt: ["$_id.interval", 10] },
                  then: "0",
                  else: ""
                }
              },
              { $toString: "$_id.interval" }
            ]
          },
          aqi: 1,
          pm25: 1,
          pm10: 1,
          co: 1,
          so2: 1,
          o3: 1,
          no2: 1,
          temperature: 1,
          humidity: 1,
          category: 1,
        }
      }
    ];

    const aggregatedData = await SensorData.aggregate(aggregationPipeline);

    // Jika tidak ada data, coba ambil data mentah
    if (aggregatedData.length === 0) {
      const rawData = await SensorData.find({
        roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
        timestamp: { $gte: startDate, $lte: endDate }
      })
      .sort({ timestamp: 1 });

      return res.json({
        success: true,
        data: rawData.map(item => ({
          ...item.toObject(),
          timeLabel: `${item.timestamp.getHours()}:${item.timestamp.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`
        })),
        aggregated: false,
        count: rawData.length,
        message: 'Raw data loaded'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: aggregatedData,
      aggregated: true,
      count: aggregatedData.length,
      startDate: startDate,
      endDate: endDate,
      message: 'Aggregated history data loaded'
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching history data:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching history data',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

router.get('/:roomId/monthly', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { roomId } = req.params;
    const year  = parseInt(req.query.year, 10);
    const month = parseInt(req.query.month, 10);

    if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(roomId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid room ID format',
      });
    }

    if (isNaN(year) || year < 2000 || year > 2100) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Parameter year tidak valid',
      });
    }

    if (isNaN(month) || month < 1 || month > 12) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Parameter month tidak valid (1-12)',
      });
    }

    const startOfMonth = new Date(year, month - 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    const endOfMonth   = new Date(year, month, 0, 23, 59, 59, 999);

    console.log(
      `📊 Monthly trend → room ${roomId} | ${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')} | ` +
      `${startOfMonth.toISOString()} → ${endOfMonth.toISOString()}`
    );

    const allParams = [
      'aqi', 'pm25', 'pm10', 'co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2',
      'temperature', 'humidity',
    ];

    // Accumulator tahap 1: per tanggal
    const dailyAcc = {};
    allParams.forEach((p) => {
      dailyAcc[`daily_max_${p}`] = { $max: `$${p}` };
      dailyAcc[`daily_avg_${p}`] = { $avg: `$${p}` };
      dailyAcc[`daily_min_${p}`] = { $min: `$${p}` };
    });

    // Accumulator tahap 2: per hari-dalam-minggu
    const monthlyAcc = { weekCount: { $sum: 1 } };
    allParams.forEach((p) => {
      monthlyAcc[`max_${p}`] = { $max: `$daily_max_${p}` };
      monthlyAcc[`avg_${p}`] = { $avg: `$daily_avg_${p}` };
      monthlyAcc[`min_${p}`] = { $min: `$daily_min_${p}` };
    });

    const pipeline = [
      // 1. Filter: room + rentang bulan + BUKAN anomali + BUKAN error (-1)
      {
        $match: {
          roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
          timestamp: { $gte: startOfMonth, $lte: endOfMonth },
          isAnomaly: { $ne: true },   // exclude spike & error simulasi
          aqi: { $gte: 0 },           // exclude error (-1), aman untuk legacy
        },
      },
      // 2. Hitung index hari dalam minggu (Senin=1 .. Minggu=7)
      //    MongoDB $dayOfWeek: 1=Sun, 2=Mon, ..., 7=Sat
      //    Formula: ((dow + 5) % 7) + 1
      {
        $addFields: {
          dayOfWeekMonSun: {
            $add: [
              { $mod: [{ $add: [{ $dayOfWeek: '$timestamp' }, 5] }, 7] },
              1,
            ],
          },
        },
      },
      // 3. Group per tanggal (hari kalender)
      {
        $group: {
          _id: {
            day: { $dayOfMonth: '$timestamp' },
            dow: '$dayOfWeekMonSun',
          },
          ...dailyAcc,
        },
      },
      // 4. Group per hari-dalam-minggu
      {
        $group: {
          _id: '$_id.dow',
          ...monthlyAcc,
        },
      },
      // 5. Urutkan Senin..Minggu
      { $sort: { _id: 1 } },
    ];

    const rawResult = await SensorData.aggregate(pipeline);

    const dayNames = [
      'Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu', 'Minggu',
    ];

    const round1 = (v) =>
      typeof v === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(v)
        ? Math.round(v * 10) / 10
        : null;

    const days = [];
    for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {
      const found = rawResult.find((r) => r._id === i);

      const values = {};
      allParams.forEach((p) => {
        values[p] = {
          max: found ? round1(found[`max_${p}`]) : null,
          avg: found ? round1(found[`avg_${p}`]) : null,
          min: found ? round1(found[`min_${p}`]) : null,
        };
      });

      // Hari dianggap punya data bila minimal AQI punya nilai valid
      const hasData = values.aqi.max !== null;

      days.push({
        day: i,
        dayName: dayNames[i - 1],
        hasData,
        weekCount: found?.weekCount ?? 0,
        values,
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      year,
      month,
      roomId,
      startDate: startOfMonth,
      endDate: endOfMonth,
      days,
      message: 'Monthly trend data loaded',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching monthly trend:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching monthly trend',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
});

module.exports = router;

const express = require('express');
