src/services/iotService.js

const axios = require('axios');
const cron = require('node-cron');
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const IoTDevice = require('../models/IoTDevice');
const SensorData = require('../models/SensorData');
const { getAQICategory, calculateAQI } = require('../utils/aqiCalculator');

class IoTService {
  constructor() {
    this.isRunning = false;
    this.task = null;
    this.io = null;
    this.deviceLastData = new Map();
    this.pollingInterval = 30000;
  }

  start(io) {
    if (this.isRunning) {
      console.log('⚠️ IoT Service is already running');
      return;
    }

    this.io = io;
    console.log(`🚀 Starting IoT Service (polling every ${this.pollingInterval/1000}s)...`);

    this.task = cron.schedule('*/30 * * * * *', async () => {
      await this.pollAllIoTDevices();
    });

    this.isRunning = true;
    console.log('✅ IoT Service started');
    this.pollAllIoTDevices();
  }

  stop() {
    if (this.task) {
      this.task.stop();
      this.isRunning = false;
      console.log('⏹️ IoT Service stopped');
    }
  }

    async pollAllIoTDevices() {
    // ✅ Serialize: hanya satu service yang mencetak log pada satu waktu.
    const { runExclusive } = require('../utils/serviceLock');

    return runExclusive(async () => {
      try {
        const devices = await IoTDevice.find({});

        if (devices.length === 0) {
          console.log('ℹ️ No IoT devices to poll');
          return;
        }

        // 🔄 Header di dalam lock → selalu tercetak utuh sebelum
        // log room/device dari service lain masuk.
        console.log(`🔄 Polling ${devices.length} IoT devices...`);

        const pollPromises = devices.map(device =>
          this.pollDevice(device).catch(error => {
            console.error(`❌ Error polling device ${device.name}:`, error.message);
            return null;
          })
        );

        const results = await Promise.allSettled(pollPromises);

        const successfulPolls = results.filter(
          r => r.status === 'fulfilled' && r.value && r.value.success
        ).length;

        console.log(`\n✅ IoT polling completed: ${successfulPolls}/${devices.length} successful`);
        console.log(`🕒 Updated at ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`);
        console.log ('\n=====================================================================================')
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Error in pollAllIoTDevices:', error.message);
      }
    });
  }

  async pollDevice(device) {
    try {
      console.log(`📡 Polling device: ${device.name} (${device.apiEndpoint})`);
      const response = await axios.get(device.apiEndpoint, { timeout: 5000 });

      if (response.data?.success === true) {
        const iotData = response.data.data;

        if (!this.isValidIoTData(iotData)) {
          throw new Error('Invalid IoT data structure');
        }

        const lastData = this.deviceLastData.get(device._id.toString());
        const hasChanged = this._hasDataChanged(lastData, iotData);

        this.deviceLastData.set(device._id.toString(), iotData);

        device.status = 'online';
        device.lastUpdate = new Date();
        await device.save();

        if (hasChanged || !lastData) {

          const rooms = await Room.find({
            iotDeviceId: device._id.toString()
          });

          for (const room of rooms) {
            await this.updateRoomFromIoT(room, iotData, device.name);
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`ℹ️ No data change for device ${device.name}, skipping broadcast`);
        }

        return { success: true, changed: hasChanged };
      }

      return { success: false, error: 'Device did not return success' };
    } catch (error) {
      device.status = 'offline';
      await device.save();
      console.log('┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
      console.error(`❌ Error polling device ${device.name}:`, error.message);
      console.log('└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
      return { success: false, error: error.message };
    }
  }

  async checkDeviceStatus(devices) {
    if (!devices || devices.length === 0) return [];

    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
      devices.map(async (device) => {
        try {
          await axios.get(device.apiEndpoint, { timeout: 3000 });
          return { id: device._id.toString(), status: 'online' };
        } catch (_) {
          return { id: device._id.toString(), status: 'offline' };
        }
      })
    );

    const updates = results
      .filter((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled' && r.value)
      .map((r) => r.value);

    if (updates.length > 0) {
      const bulkOps = updates.map((u) => ({
        updateOne: {
          filter: { _id: u.id },
          update: { $set: { status: u.status } },
        },
      }));

      try {
        await IoTDevice.bulkWrite(bulkOps);
      } catch (bulkErr) {
        console.error('❌ bulkWrite status check failed:', bulkErr.message);
      }
    }

    return updates;
  }

     isValidIoTData(data) {
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return false;
    
    const knownFields = [
      'aqi', 'pm25', 'pm10', 'co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2',
      'temperature', 'humidity'
    ];
    return knownFields.some((k) => k in data);
  }

   _hasDataChanged(oldData, newData) {
    if (!oldData || !newData) return true;

    const changed = (oldV, newV) => {
      if (oldV == null && newV == null) return false;
      if (oldV == null || newV == null) return true;
      return Math.abs(Number(newV) - Number(oldV)) > 0;
    };

    return (
      changed(oldData.aqi, newData.aqi) ||
      changed(oldData.pm25, newData.pm25) ||
      changed(oldData.pm10, newData.pm10) ||
      changed(oldData.co, newData.co) ||
      changed(oldData.so2, newData.so2) ||
      changed(oldData.o3, newData.o3) ||
      changed(oldData.no2, newData.no2) ||
      changed(oldData.temperature, newData.temperature) ||
      changed(oldData.humidity, newData.humidity)
    );
  }

    async updateRoomFromIoT(room, iotData, deviceName) {
    try {
      if (room.demoMode) {
        console.log(`ℹ️ Skipping room ${room.name} (demoMode active)`);
        return;
      }

      const optNum = (v) =>
        typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && !Number.isNaN(v)
          ? v
          : null;

      const pm25 = optNum(iotData.pm25);
      const pm10 = optNum(iotData.pm10);
      const co   = optNum(iotData.co);
      const so2  = optNum(iotData.so2);
      const o3   = optNum(iotData.o3);
      const no2  = optNum(iotData.no2);
      const temperature = optNum(iotData.temperature);
      const humidity    = optNum(iotData.humidity);

      // Deteksi error hanya untuk field yang ADA dan negatif.
      const hasError = [pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity]
        .some((v) => v !== null && v < 0);

      if (hasError) {
        await this.handleDeviceError(room, deviceName);
        return;
      }

      const aqiResult = calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2);

      const normalizedData = {
        aqi: aqiResult.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: aqiResult.category,
        dominant: aqiResult.dominant,
      };

      const currentData = room.currentData;
      const hasRoomDataChanged = this._hasDataChanged(
        {
          aqi: room.currentAQI,
          pm25: currentData.pm25,
          pm10: currentData.pm10,
          co: currentData.co,
          so2: currentData.so2,
          o3: currentData.o3,
          no2: currentData.no2,
          temperature: currentData.temperature,
          humidity: currentData.humidity,
        },
        normalizedData
      );

      if (!hasRoomDataChanged) {
        console.log(
          `ℹ️ Room ${room.name} data unchanged (exact match), skipping update`
        );
        return;
      }

      room.currentAQI = normalizedData.aqi;
      room.currentData = {
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      };
      room.updatedAt = new Date();
      await room.save();

       await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: normalizedData.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: aqiResult.category,
        timestamp: new Date(),
        isAnomaly: iotData.isAnomaly === true,
        anomalyType: iotData.anomalyType || null,
      }).save();

      this.broadcastRoomUpdate(room, normalizedData, 'iot', deviceName);

      if (normalizedData.aqi == null) {
        console.log(
          `ℹ️ IoT room ${room.name} (device ${deviceName}): tidak ada data valid — status no_data`
        );
      } else if (normalizedData.aqi > 100) {
        console.log(
          `🚨 HIGH AQI in ${room.name} (IoT ${deviceName}): ${normalizedData.aqi} (${aqiResult.category})`
        );
      } else {
        console.log(
          `✅ IoT room ${room.name}: AQI ${normalizedData.aqi} (${aqiResult.dominant}), ` +
          `PM2.5=${pm25}, PM10=${pm10}, CO=${co}, SO2=${so2}, O3=${o3}, NO2=${no2}, ` +
          `Suhu=${temperature}, RH=${humidity}`
        );
      }

      if (aqiResult.dominant) {
        const dominantLabel = {
          pm25: `PM2.5 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuPM25})`,
          pm10: `PM10 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuPM10})`,
          co:   `CO (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuCO})`,
          so2:  `SO2 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuSO2})`,
          o3:   `O3 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuO3})`,
          no2:  `NO2 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuNO2})`,
        }[aqiResult.dominant] || aqiResult.dominant;

        console.log(
          `⚠️  DOMINAN ${aqiResult.dominant.toUpperCase()} in ${room.name}: AQI ${aqiResult.aqi} (${dominantLabel})`
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`❌ Error updating IoT room ${room.name}:`, error.message);
    }
  }

  

      async updateRoomFromIoTForce(room, iotData, deviceName) {
    try {
      if (room.demoMode) {
        console.log(`ℹ️ Skipping force update ${room.name} (demoMode active)`);
        return;
      }

      const optNum = (v) =>
        typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && !Number.isNaN(v)
          ? v
          : null;

      const pm25 = optNum(iotData.pm25);
      const pm10 = optNum(iotData.pm10);
      const co   = optNum(iotData.co);
      const so2  = optNum(iotData.so2);
      const o3   = optNum(iotData.o3);
      const no2  = optNum(iotData.no2);
      const temperature = optNum(iotData.temperature);
      const humidity    = optNum(iotData.humidity);

      const hasError = [pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity]
        .some((v) => v !== null && v < 0);

      if (hasError) {
        await this.handleDeviceError(room, deviceName);
        return;
      }

      const aqiResult = calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2);

      room.currentAQI = aqiResult.aqi;
      room.currentData = {
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      };
      room.updatedAt = new Date();
      await room.save();

      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: aqiResult.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: aqiResult.category,
        timestamp: new Date(),

        isAnomaly: iotData.isAnomaly === true,
        anomalyType: iotData.anomalyType || null,
      }).save();

      this.broadcastRoomUpdate(room, iotData, 'iot', deviceName);

      console.log(
        `✅ FORCE-updated IoT room ${room.name}: AQI ${aqiResult.aqi ?? 'null'} (${aqiResult.dominant ?? 'none'})`
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`❌ Error force-updating room ${room.name}:`, error.message);
    }
  }

  async handleDeviceError(room, deviceName) {
    try {
      // Catat sebagai error (-1) untuk history
      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: -1,
        pm25: -1, pm10: -1, co: -1, so2: -1, o3: -1, no2: -1,
        temperature: -1, humidity: -1,
        category: 'error',
        timestamp: new Date(),
        isAnomaly: true,
        anomalyType: 'error',
      }).save();

      console.log(
        `⚠️ IoT SENSOR ERROR in ${room.name} (device: ${deviceName}) — room retained last good value`
      );

    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`❌ Error handling device error for ${room.name}:`, err.message);
    }
  }

  async forceUpdateRoom(roomId) {
    try {
      const room = await Room.findById(roomId);
      if (!room) return { success: false, message: 'Room not found' };

      if (room.dataSource !== 'iot' || !room.iotDeviceId) {
        return { success: false, message: 'Room is not configured for IoT' };
      }

      const device = await IoTDevice.findById(room.iotDeviceId);
      if (!device) return { success: false, message: 'IoT device not found' };

      const response = await axios.get(device.apiEndpoint, { timeout: 5000 });

      if (response.data?.success !== true) {
        return { success: false, message: 'Device did not return success' };
      }

      const iotData = response.data.data;
      if (!this.isValidIoTData(iotData)) {
        return { success: false, message: 'Invalid IoT data structure' };
      }

      device.status = 'online';
      device.lastUpdate = new Date();
      await device.save();

      this.deviceLastData.set(device._id.toString(), iotData);

      await this.updateRoomFromIoTForce(room, iotData, device.name);

      return { success: true, data: iotData, deviceName: device.name };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`❌ forceUpdateRoom(${roomId}) error:`, error.message);
      return { success: false, error: error.message };
    }
  }

  async pollDeviceForce(device) {
    try {
      const response = await axios.get(device.apiEndpoint, { timeout: 5000 });

      if (response.data?.success !== true) {
        return { success: false, message: 'Device did not return success' };
      }

      const iotData = response.data.data;
      if (!this.isValidIoTData(iotData)) {
        return { success: false, message: 'Invalid IoT data' };
      }

      this.deviceLastData.set(device._id.toString(), iotData);

      device.status = 'online';
      device.lastUpdate = new Date();
      await device.save();

      const rooms = await Room.find({ iotDeviceId: device._id.toString() });
      for (const room of rooms) {
        await this.updateRoomFromIoTForce(room, iotData, device.name);
      }

      return { success: true, changed: true };
    } catch (error) {
      device.status = 'offline';
      await device.save().catch(() => {});
      return { success: false, error: error.message };
    }
  }

  broadcastRoomUpdate(room, data, source, deviceName = null) {
    if (this.io) {
      const updateData = {
        roomId: room._id,
        data: {
          currentAQI: room.currentAQI,
          currentData: room.currentData,
          updatedAt: room.updatedAt
        },
        timestamp: new Date(),
        source: source,
        deviceName: deviceName
      };

      this.io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-update', updateData);

      this.io.emit('dashboard-update', {
        type: 'room-data-updated',
        roomId: room._id,
        aqi: room.currentAQI,
        building: room.buildingName,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });

      console.log(`📢 Broadcast IoT update for room ${room.name}: AQI ${room.currentAQI}`);
    }
  }

  async forcePollAll() {
    console.log('🔄 FORCE polling all IoT devices...');
    try {
      const devices = await IoTDevice.find({});
      if (devices.length === 0) {
        return { success: true, message: 'No IoT devices to poll', count: 0 };
      }

      const results = await Promise.allSettled(
        devices.map(d => this.pollDeviceForce(d).catch(e => null))
      );

      const successCount = results.filter(
        r => r.status === 'fulfilled' && r.value?.success
      ).length;

      console.log(`✅ FORCE poll completed: ${successCount}/${devices.length}`);
      return {
        success: true,
        message: 'Force poll completed',
        polled: devices.length,
        successful: successCount
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ forcePollAll error:', error.message);
      return { success: false, error: error.message };
    }
  }

  getStatus() {
    return {
      isRunning: this.isRunning,
      lastUpdate: new Date(),
      nextUpdate: new Date(Date.now() + this.pollingInterval),
      socketConnected: this.io !== null,
      pollingInterval: this.pollingInterval
    };
  }
}

const iotService = new IoTService();
module.exports = iotService;

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
src/services/simulationService.js:

const cron = require('node-cron');
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const SensorData = require('../models/SensorData');
const { getAQICategory, calculateAQI } = require('../utils/aqiCalculator');
const {
  generateSensorData,
  simulateAnomaly,
} = require('../utils/dataGenerator');

class SimulationService {
  constructor() {
    this.isRunning = false;
    this.task = null;
    this.io = null;
    this.updateInterval = 30000;
  }

  start(io) {
    if (this.isRunning) {
      console.log('⚠️ Simulation Service is already running');
      return;
    }

    this.io = io;
    console.log(`🚀 Starting Simulation Service (update every ${this.updateInterval/1000}s)...`);

    this.task = cron.schedule(`*/${this.updateInterval/1000} * * * * *`, async () => {
      await this.updateAllRooms();
    });

    this.isRunning = true;
    console.log('✅ Simulation Service started');
    this.updateAllRooms();
  }

  stop() {
    if (this.task) {
      this.task.stop();
      this.isRunning = false;
      console.log('⏹️ Simulation Service stopped');
    }
  }

    async updateAllRooms() {
    // ✅ Serialize: pastikan siklus simulasi ini selesai dulu
    // sebelum IoT service boleh mencetak log-nya (dan sebaliknya).
    const { runExclusive } = require('../utils/serviceLock');

    return runExclusive(async () => {
      try {
        const rooms = await Room.find({
          dataSource: 'simulation',
          demoMode: { $ne: true }
        });

        if (rooms.length === 0) {
          console.log('ℹ️ No active simulation rooms to update');
          return;
        }

        // 🔄 Header di dalam lock → selalu tercetak pertama
        // sebelum log room manapun (simulation atau IoT).
        console.log(`\n🔄 Updating ${rooms.length} simulation rooms...`);

        for (const room of rooms) {
          await this.updateRoom(room);
        }

        console.log(`\n🕒 Updated ${rooms.length} rooms at ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()} \n`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Error in updateAllRooms:', error.message);
      }
    });
  }

  async updateRoom(room) {
    try {
      const roomType = this.getRoomType(room.name);

      const normalData = generateSensorData(roomType);
      const sensorData = simulateAnomaly(normalData);

      if (sensorData.isAnomaly && sensorData.anomalyType === 'error') {
        await this.handleSensorError(room, sensorData);
        return;
      }

      // ✅ AQI dihitung dari PM2.5, PM10, dan CO — ambil yang dominan
       const aqiResult = calculateAQI(
        sensorData.pm25,
        sensorData.pm10,
        sensorData.co,
        sensorData.so2,
        sensorData.o3,
        sensorData.no2
      );

      sensorData.aqi = aqiResult.aqi;
      sensorData.category = aqiResult.category;
      sensorData.dominant = aqiResult.dominant;
      sensorData.ispuPM25 = aqiResult.ispuPM25;
      sensorData.ispuPM10 = aqiResult.ispuPM10;
      sensorData.ispuCO = aqiResult.ispuCO;
      sensorData.ispuSO2 = aqiResult.ispuSO2;
      sensorData.ispuO3 = aqiResult.ispuO3;
      sensorData.ispuNO2 = aqiResult.ispuNO2;

      const hasChanged = this._hasDataChanged(room, sensorData);

      if (!hasChanged) {
        console.log(`ℹ️ Room ${room.name} data unchanged (exact match), skipping update`);
        return;
      }

      room.currentAQI = sensorData.aqi;
       room.currentData = {
        pm25: sensorData.pm25,
        pm10: sensorData.pm10,
        co: sensorData.co,
        so2: sensorData.so2,
        o3: sensorData.o3,
        no2: sensorData.no2,
        temperature: sensorData.temperature,
        humidity: sensorData.humidity,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      };
      room.updatedAt = new Date();

      await room.save();

       const historicalData = new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: sensorData.aqi,
        pm25: sensorData.pm25,
        pm10: sensorData.pm10,
        co: sensorData.co,
        so2: sensorData.so2,
        o3: sensorData.o3,
        no2: sensorData.no2,
        temperature: sensorData.temperature,
        humidity: sensorData.humidity,
        category: sensorData.category,
        timestamp: new Date(),
        isAnomaly: sensorData.isAnomaly === true,
        anomalyType: sensorData.anomalyType || null,
      });

      await historicalData.save();

      console.log('┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');

      this.broadcastRoomUpdate(room, sensorData);

      // Log ringkasan data room (simulasi) — format sama dengan IoT
      // Contoh: ✅ Simulation room H17: AQI 28, PM2.5=..., PM10=..., CO=..., Suhu=..., RH=...
      if (sensorData.isAnomaly) {
        console.log(
          `🚨 ANOMALY in ${room.name}: AQI ${sensorData.aqi} (${sensorData.category}) — type: ${sensorData.anomalyType}`
        );
      } else {
        console.log(
          `✅ Simulation room ${room.name}: AQI ${sensorData.aqi}, ` +
          `PM2.5=${sensorData.pm25}, PM10=${sensorData.pm10}, ` +
          `CO=${sensorData.co}, Suhu=${sensorData.temperature}, RH=${sensorData.humidity}`
        );
      }

      // Log parameter dominan (setelah ringkasan partikel)
      const dominantLabel = {
        pm25: `PM2.5 (ISPU=${sensorData.ispuPM25})`,
        pm10: `PM10 (ISPU=${sensorData.ispuPM10})`,
        co:   `CO (ISPU=${sensorData.ispuCO})`,
      }[sensorData.dominant] || sensorData.dominant;

      console.log(
        `⚠️  DOMINAN ${sensorData.dominant.toUpperCase()} in ${room.name}: AQI ${sensorData.aqi} (${dominantLabel})`
      );
      console.log('└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
      console.error(`❌ Error updating room ${room.name}:`, error.message);
      console.log('└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
    }
  }

  async handleSensorError(room, sensorData) {
    try {
      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: -1,
        pm25: -1, pm10: -1, co: -1, so2: -1, o3: -1, no2: -1,
        temperature: -1, humidity: -1,
        category: 'error',
        timestamp: new Date(),
        isAnomaly: true,
        anomalyType: 'error',
      }).save();

      console.log('┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
      console.log(
        `⚠️ SENSOR ERROR in ${room.name} (data invalid, room retained last good value)`
      );
      console.log('└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
    } catch (err) {
      console.log('┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
      console.error(`❌ Error handling sensor error for ${room.name}:`, err.message);
       console.log('└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────');
    }
  }

  _hasDataChanged(room, newData) {
    const currentData = room.currentData;

    const changed = (oldV, newV) => {
      if (oldV == null && newV == null) return false;
      if (oldV == null || newV == null) return true;
      return Math.abs(Number(newV) - Number(oldV)) > 0;
    };

    return (
      changed(room.currentAQI, newData.aqi) ||
      changed(currentData.pm25, newData.pm25) ||
      changed(currentData.pm10, newData.pm10) ||
      changed(currentData.co, newData.co) ||
      changed(currentData.so2, newData.so2) ||
      changed(currentData.o3, newData.o3) ||
      changed(currentData.no2, newData.no2) ||
      changed(currentData.temperature, newData.temperature) ||
      changed(currentData.humidity, newData.humidity)
    );
  }

  broadcastRoomUpdate(room, data) {
    if (this.io) {
      const updateData = {
        roomId: room._id,
        data: {
          currentAQI: room.currentAQI,
          currentData: room.currentData,
          updatedAt: room.updatedAt
        },
        timestamp: new Date(),
        source: 'simulation'
      };

      this.io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-update', updateData);

      this.io.emit('dashboard-update', {
        type: 'room-data-updated',
        roomId: room._id,
        aqi: room.currentAQI,
        building: room.buildingName,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });

      console.log(`📢 Broadcast simulation update for room ${room.name}: AQI ${room.currentAQI}`);
    }
  }

  getRoomType(roomName) {
    const name = roomName.toLowerCase();

    if (name.includes('lab') || name.includes('praktikum')) {
      return 'laboratory';
    } else if (name.includes('kelas') || name.includes('ruang') || name.includes('r.') || name.includes('gd.')) {
      return 'classroom';
    } else if (name.includes('perpustakaan') || name.includes('library')) {
      return 'library';
    } else if (name.includes('aula') || name.includes('auditorium') || name.includes('hall')) {
      return 'crowded';
    }

    return 'normal';
  }

  async forceUpdate() {
    console.log('🔄 Force updating all simulation rooms...');
    await this.updateAllRooms();
    return { success: true, message: 'Force simulation update completed' };
  }

  getStatus() {
    return {
      isRunning: this.isRunning,
      lastUpdate: new Date(),
      nextUpdate: new Date(Date.now() + this.updateInterval),
      socketConnected: this.io !== null,
      updateInterval: this.updateInterval
    };
  }
}

const simulationService = new SimulationService();
module.exports = simulationService;

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
unila-aqi-backend\src\utils\__tests__\aqiCalculator.test.js

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * UNIT TEST — src/utils/aqiCalculator.js
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Menguji 4 fungsi publik dari aqiCalculator.js:
 *   1. calculateISPUFromPM25(conc)       → ISPU 0-500 (dari PM2.5)
 *   2. calculateISPUFromPM10(conc)       → ISPU 0-500 (dari PM10)
 *   3. calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co)      → { aqi, dominant, ispuPM25, ... }
 *   4. getAQICategory(aqi)               → { category, label, color }
 *
 * Standar acuan: PP No. 22 Tahun 2021 (Indonesia).
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * RUMUS INTERPOLASI LINEAR
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   ISPU = ((ISPU_high − ISPU_low) / (C_high − C_low)) × (C − C_low) + ISPU_low
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * KATEGORI ISPU (PP 22/2021)
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   0   –  50   : Baik
 *   51  – 100   : Sedang
 *   101 – 200   : Tidak Sehat
 *   201 – 300   : Sangat Tidak Sehat
 *   301 – 500   : Berbahaya
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * POLA PENGUJIAN TIAP FUNGSI
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   A. NILAI DI TENGAH SEGMEN  → verifikasi rumus interpolasi benar
 *   B. NILAI TEPAT DI BOUNDARY → pastikan tidak "bocor" ke segmen sebelah
 *   C. PERPINDAHAN SEGMEN      → pastikan titik "naik kelas" benar (+1 unit)
 *   D. NILAI EKSTREM / INVALID → pastikan clamp & sanitasi input bekerja
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * STRUKTUR FILE
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   BAGIAN 1 — calculateISPUFromPM25
 *   BAGIAN 2 — calculateISPUFromPM10
 *   BAGIAN 3 — calculateAQI  (MAX dari ketiga parameter)
 *   BAGIAN 4 — getAQICategory (batas antar kategori)
 * ============================================================================
 */

const { test, describe } = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert');

const {
  calculateISPUFromPM25,
  calculateISPUFromPM10,
  calculateAQI,
  getAQICategory,
} = require('../aqiCalculator');


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 1 — calculateISPUFromPM25
// ============================================================================
// Tabel breakpoint PM2.5 (µg/m³) → ISPU:
//
//   ISPU          Konsentrasi (µg/m³)
//   ─────         ───────────────────
//     0 –  50        0 –  55
//    51 – 100       56 – 150
//   101 – 200      151 – 250
//   201 – 300      251 – 350
//   301 – 500      351 – 500
// ============================================================================
describe('calculateISPUFromPM25 — konversi PM2.5 (µg/m³) → ISPU', () => {

  // ── A. Nilai di tengah segmen (verifikasi rumus interpolasi) ─────────────

  test('PM2.5 = 0 → ISPU 0 (titik awal, tanpa interpolasi)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(0), 0);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 27.5 → ISPU 25 (tengah segmen 0–55)', () => {
    // (50 − 0)/(55 − 0) × (27.5 − 0) + 0 = 25
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(27.5), 25);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 100 → ISPU 74 (tengah segmen 56–150)', () => {
    // (100 − 51)/(150 − 56) × (100 − 56) + 51
    //   = (49/94) × 44 + 51
    //   = 22.94 + 51
    //   ≈ 73.93  → dibulatkan 74
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(100), 74);
  });

  // ── B. Nilai tepat di boundary (tidak boleh "bocor" ke segmen sebelah) ──

  test('PM2.5 = 55 → ISPU 50 (batas atas segmen Baik, BUKAN 51)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(55), 50);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 150 → ISPU 100 (batas atas segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(150), 100);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 250 → ISPU 200 (batas atas segmen Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(250), 200);
  });

  // ── C. Perpindahan segmen (1 unit di atas boundary) ──────────────────────

  test('PM2.5 = 56 → ISPU 51 (naik 1 tingkat ke segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(56), 51);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 151 → ISPU 101 (naik 1 tingkat ke segmen Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(151), 101);
  });

  // ── D. Nilai ekstrem & input tidak valid (clamp + sanitasi) ──────────────

  test('PM2.5 = −5 (negatif) → ISPU 0 (di-clamp ke batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(-5), 0);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 1000 (di atas 500) → ISPU 500 (di-clamp ke batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(1000), 500);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = NaN → ISPU 0 (input tidak valid disanitasi)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(NaN), 0);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = null → ISPU 0 (input null disanitasi)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(null), 0);
  });
});


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 2 — calculateISPUFromPM10
// ============================================================================
// Tabel breakpoint PM10 (µg/m³) → ISPU:
//
//   ISPU          Konsentrasi (µg/m³)
//   ─────         ───────────────────
//     0 –  50        0 –  50
//    51 – 100       51 – 150
//   101 – 200      151 – 350
//   201 – 300      351 – 420
//   301 – 500      421 – 500
//
// CATATAN: seluruh test di bagian ini fokus pada BOUNDARY karena breakpoint
// PM10 bersinggungan tepat di integer (0/50/51/150/…). Rumus interpolasi
// sudah divalidasi lengkap di BAGIAN 1, jadi di sini cukup memastikan setiap
// batas segmen jatuh di ISPU yang benar.
// ============================================================================
describe('calculateISPUFromPM10 — konversi PM10 (µg/m³) → ISPU', () => {

  test('PM10 = 50 → ISPU 50 (batas atas segmen Baik)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(50), 50);
  });

  test('PM10 = 51 → ISPU 51 (batas bawah segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(51), 51);
  });

  test('PM10 = 150 → ISPU 100 (batas atas segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(150), 100);
  });

  test('PM10 = 350 → ISPU 200 (batas atas segmen Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(350), 200);
  });

  test('PM10 = 420 → ISPU 300 (batas atas segmen Sangat Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(420), 300);
  });
});


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 3 — calculateAQI
// ============================================================================
// AQI = MAX(ISPU_PM25, ISPU_PM10, ISPU_CO).
//
// `dominant` = parameter penyumbang nilai MAX.
//
// ATURAN TIE (nilai ISPU sama di beberapa parameter):
//   Urutan pengecekan di implementasi adalah CO → PM2.5 → PM10.
//   Konsekuensi:
//     • CO tie dengan parameter lain   → CO menang
//     • PM2.5 tie dengan PM10          → PM2.5 menang
//
// Skenario yang diuji:
//   1. Masing-masing parameter menjadi dominan (PM10, PM2.5, CO)
//   2. Tie 2 arah & tie 3 arah
//   3. Konsistensi category/label mengikuti nilai MAX
// ============================================================================
describe('calculateAQI — AQI = MAX(ISPU PM2.5, ISPU PM10, ISPU CO)', () => {

  // ── Skenario 1: parameter dominan ────────────────────────────────────────

  test('PM10 dominan: PM2.5=20, PM10=60 → AQI 55, dominant "pm10"', () => {
    // PM2.5: (50/55) × 20                  = 18
    // PM10 : (49/99) × (60−51) + 51        = 55.45 → 55
    // CO   : 0                              = 0
    // MAX(18, 55, 0) = 55 → PM10 dominan
    const r = calculateAQI(20, 60);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 55);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'pm10');
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM25, 18);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM10, 55);
  });

  test('PM2.5 dominan: PM2.5=100, PM10=30 → AQI 74, dominant "pm25"', () => {
    // PM2.5: (49/94) × (100−56) + 51       = 73.93 → 74
    // PM10 : (50/50) × 30                  = 30
    // CO   : 0                              = 0
    // MAX(74, 30, 0) = 74 → PM2.5 dominan
    const r = calculateAQI(100, 30);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 74);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'pm25');
  });

  test('CO dominan: PM2.5=10, PM10=20, CO=20000 → AQI 234, dominant "co"', () => {
    // PM2.5: (50/55) × 10                  ≈ 9
    // PM10 : (50/50) × 20                  = 20
    // CO   : segmen 15001–30000 µg/m³      → (99/14999) × (20000−15001) + 201 ≈ 234
    // MAX(9, 20, 234) = 234 → CO dominan
    const r = calculateAQI(10, 20, 20000);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 234);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'co');
    assert.ok(r.ispuCO > r.ispuPM25);
    assert.ok(r.ispuCO > r.ispuPM10);
  });

  // ── Skenario 2: tie (nilai ISPU sama antar parameter) ────────────────────

  test('Tie PM2.5 vs PM10, CO rendah → dominant "pm25"', () => {
    // PM2.5 = 30 → ISPU (50/55) × 30 ≈ 27
    // PM10  = 27 → ISPU (50/50) × 27 = 27
    // CO    =  0 → ISPU = 0 (tidak ikut tie)
    // Tie PM2.5 vs PM10 → PM2.5 menang (dicek lebih dulu)
    const r = calculateAQI(30, 27, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM25, r.ispuPM10);
    assert.ok(r.ispuPM25 > r.ispuCO);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'pm25');
  });

  test('Tie 3 arah (semua ISPU = 0) → dominant "co"', () => {
    // PM2.5 = 0, PM10 = 0, CO = 0 → semua ISPU = 0
    // Tie 3 arah → CO menang (dicek lebih dulu)
    const r = calculateAQI(0, 0, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM25, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM10, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuCO, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'co');
  });

  // ── Skenario 3: konsistensi category & label ─────────────────────────────

  test('Kategori mengikuti nilai MAX: AQI 55 → category "sedang", label "Sedang"', () => {
    const r = calculateAQI(20, 60);
    assert.strictEqual(r.category, 'sedang');
    assert.strictEqual(r.label, 'Sedang');
  });

  test('Semua input nol → AQI 0, category "baik"', () => {
    const r = calculateAQI(0, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.category, 'baik');
  });

  test('Input sangat tinggi (600, 600) → AQI 500 (clamped), category "berbahaya"', () => {
    const r = calculateAQI(600, 600);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 500);
    assert.strictEqual(r.category, 'berbahaya');
  });
});


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 4 — getAQICategory
// ============================================================================
// Memastikan nilai AQI jatuh ke kategori yang benar tepat di batas:
//
//   Batas 50/51   : Baik             ↔ Sedang
//   Batas 100/101 : Sedang           ↔ Tidak Sehat
//   Batas 200/201 : Tidak Sehat      ↔ Sangat Tidak Sehat
//   Batas 300/301 : Sangat Tidak Sehat ↔ Berbahaya
//
// Pola pengujian: setiap transisi diuji dengan SEPASANG test — nilai tepat
// di batas atas kategori lama (n), dan n+1 untuk memastikan "naik kelas".
// ============================================================================
describe('getAQICategory — batas kategori sesuai PP 22/2021', () => {

  // ── Baik ↔ Sedang (batas 50/51) ──────────────────────────────────────────

  test('AQI = 50 → category "baik" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(50).category, 'baik');
  });

  test('AQI = 51 → category "sedang" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(51).category, 'sedang');
  });

  // ── Sedang ↔ Tidak Sehat (batas 100/101) ─────────────────────────────────

  test('AQI = 100 → category "sedang" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(100).category, 'sedang');
  });

  test('AQI = 101 → category "tidak_sehat" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(101).category, 'tidak_sehat');
  });

  // ── Tidak Sehat ↔ Sangat Tidak Sehat (batas 200/201) ─────────────────────

  test('AQI = 200 → category "tidak_sehat" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(200).category, 'tidak_sehat');
  });

  test('AQI = 201 → category "sangat_tidak_sehat" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(201).category, 'sangat_tidak_sehat');
  });

  // ── Sangat Tidak Sehat ↔ Berbahaya (batas 300/301) ───────────────────────

  test('AQI = 300 → category "sangat_tidak_sehat" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(300).category, 'sangat_tidak_sehat');
  });

  test('AQI = 301 → category "berbahaya" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(301).category, 'berbahaya');
  });

  // ── Batas atas global (500) ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  test('AQI = 500 → category "berbahaya" (batas atas skala)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(500).category, 'berbahaya');
  });
});

src/utils/aqiCalculator.js:

/**
 * ISPU Calculator — PP No. 22 Tahun 2021
 * AQI = MAX dari semua parameter yang TERSEDIA (non-null).
 * Jika semua null → aqi = null, category = 'no_data'.
 */

const PM25_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 55  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 56,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 250 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 251, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 351, concHigh: 500 },
];

  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 50  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 51,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 351, concHigh: 420 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 421, concHigh: 500 },
];

  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,     concHigh: 4000  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 4001,  concHigh: 8000  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 8001,  concHigh: 15000 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 15001, concHigh: 30000 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 30001, concHigh: 45000 },
];
const SO2_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,    concHigh: 80   },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 81,   concHigh: 365  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 366,  concHigh: 800  },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 801,  concHigh: 1600 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 1601, concHigh: 3000 },
];
const O3_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 120  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 121, concHigh: 235  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 236, concHigh: 400  },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 401, concHigh: 800  },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 801, concHigh: 1000 },
];
const NO2_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,    concHigh: 80   },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 81,   concHigh: 200  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 201,  concHigh: 1130 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 1131, concHigh: 2260 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 2261, concHigh: 3000 },
];

const isValidNumber = (v) =>
  typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && !Number.isNaN(v);

function calculateISPUFromConcentration(concentration, breakpoints) {
  if (!isValidNumber(concentration) || concentration < 0) return null;

  for (const bp of breakpoints) {
    if (concentration <= bp.concHigh) {
      const concRange = bp.concHigh - bp.concLow;
      if (concRange === 0) return bp.ispuHigh;
      const ispu =
        ((bp.ispuHigh - bp.ispuLow) / concRange) *
          (concentration - bp.concLow) +
        bp.ispuLow;
      return Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(500, ispu)));
    }
  }
  return 500;
}

const calculateISPUFromPM25 = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, PM25_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromPM10 = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, PM10_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromCO   = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, CO_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromSO2  = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, SO2_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromO3   = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, O3_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromNO2  = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, NO2_BREAKPOINTS);

/**
 * AQI = MAX dari parameter yang tersedia.
 * Semua parameter nullable. Bila SEMUA null → aqi: null, category: 'no_data'.
 * Tie-breaker: CO → SO2 → O3 → NO2 → PM2.5 → PM10
 */
function calculateAQI(pm25 = null, pm10 = null, co = null, so2 = null, o3 = null, no2 = null) {
  const hasPM25 = isValidNumber(pm25);
  const hasPM10 = isValidNumber(pm10);
  const hasCO   = isValidNumber(co);
  const hasSO2  = isValidNumber(so2);
  const hasO3   = isValidNumber(o3);
  const hasNO2  = isValidNumber(no2);

  if (!hasPM25 && !hasPM10 && !hasCO && !hasSO2 && !hasO3 && !hasNO2) {
    const cat = getAQICategory(null);
    return {
      aqi: null,
      ispuPM25: null, ispuPM10: null, ispuCO: null,
      ispuSO2: null, ispuO3: null, ispuNO2: null,
      dominant: null,
      category: cat.category,
      color: cat.color,
      label: cat.label,
    };
  }

  const ispuPM25 = hasPM25 ? calculateISPUFromPM25(pm25) : null;
  const ispuPM10 = hasPM10 ? calculateISPUFromPM10(pm10) : null;
  const ispuCO   = hasCO   ? calculateISPUFromCO(co)     : null;
  const ispuSO2  = hasSO2  ? calculateISPUFromSO2(so2)   : null;
  const ispuO3   = hasO3   ? calculateISPUFromO3(o3)     : null;
  const ispuNO2  = hasNO2  ? calculateISPUFromNO2(no2)   : null;

  const ordered = [
    ...(hasCO   ? [{ name: 'co',   value: ispuCO   }] : []),
    ...(hasSO2  ? [{ name: 'so2',  value: ispuSO2  }] : []),
    ...(hasO3   ? [{ name: 'o3',   value: ispuO3   }] : []),
    ...(hasNO2  ? [{ name: 'no2',  value: ispuNO2  }] : []),
    ...(hasPM25 ? [{ name: 'pm25', value: ispuPM25 }] : []),
    ...(hasPM10 ? [{ name: 'pm10', value: ispuPM10 }] : []),
  ];

  const aqi = Math.max(...ordered.map((c) => c.value));
  const dominant = ordered.find((c) => c.value === aqi)?.name || null;

  const cat = getAQICategory(aqi);

  return {
    aqi,
    ispuPM25, ispuPM10, ispuCO, ispuSO2, ispuO3, ispuNO2,
    dominant,
    category: cat.category,
    color: cat.color,
    label: cat.label,
  };
}

function getAQICategory(aqi) {
  if (aqi == null || Number.isNaN(aqi)) {
    return { category: 'no_data', color: '#BDBDBD', label: 'Tidak Ada Data' };
  }
  if (aqi <= 50)  return { category: 'baik',               color: '#00E400', label: 'Baik' };
  if (aqi <= 100) return { category: 'sedang',             color: '#0000FF', label: 'Sedang' };
  if (aqi <= 200) return { category: 'tidak_sehat',        color: '#FFFF00', label: 'Tidak Sehat' };
  if (aqi <= 300) return { category: 'sangat_tidak_sehat', color: '#FF0000', label: 'Sangat Tidak Sehat' };
  return { category: 'berbahaya', color: '#000000', label: 'Berbahaya' };
}

function aqiToConcentration(aqi, breakpoints) {
  if (!isValidNumber(aqi) || aqi <= 0) return 0;
  const target = Math.min(500, aqi);
  for (const bp of breakpoints) {
    if (target <= bp.ispuHigh) {
      const ispuRange = bp.ispuHigh - bp.ispuLow;
      if (ispuRange === 0) return bp.concLow;
      const ratio = (target - bp.ispuLow) / ispuRange;
      return Math.round(bp.concLow + ratio * (bp.concHigh - bp.concLow));
    }
  }
  return 500;
}

const aqiToPm25 = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, PM25_BREAKPOINTS);
const aqiToSO2  = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, SO2_BREAKPOINTS);
const aqiToO3   = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, O3_BREAKPOINTS);
const aqiToNO2  = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, NO2_BREAKPOINTS);

const evaluateParameter = (type, value) => {
  const catFor = (ispu) => getAQICategory(ispu);
  switch (type) {
    case 'pm25': { const i = calculateISPUFromPM25(value); const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'pm10': { const i = calculateISPUFromPM10(value); const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'co':   { const i = calculateISPUFromCO(value);   const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'so2':  { const i = calculateISPUFromSO2(value);  const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'o3':   { const i = calculateISPUFromO3(value);   const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'no2':  { const i = calculateISPUFromNO2(value);  const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'temperature':
      if (value == null) return { status: 'unknown', color: '#CCCCCC' };
      if (value >= 24 && value <= 26) return { status: 'ideal', color: '#0066CC' };
      if (value >= 20 && value <= 28) return { status: 'normal', color: '#00E400' };
      if (value >= 18 && value <= 30) return { status: 'sedang', color: '#FFFF00' };
      return { status: 'tidak_ideal', color: '#FF7E00' };
    case 'humidity':
      if (value == null) return { status: 'unknown', color: '#CCCCCC' };
      if (value >= 40 && value <= 60) return { status: 'ideal', color: '#0066CC' };
      if (value >= 30 && value <= 70) return { status: 'normal', color: '#00E400' };
      if (value >= 20 && value <= 80) return { status: 'sedang', color: '#FFFF00' };
      return { status: 'tidak_ideal', color: '#FF7E00' };
    default:
      return { status: 'unknown', color: '#CCCCCC' };
  }
};

module.exports = {
  calculateISPUFromPM25,
  calculateISPUFromPM10,
  calculateISPUFromCO,
  calculateISPUFromSO2,
  calculateISPUFromO3,
  calculateISPUFromNO2,
  calculateAQI,
  getAQICategory,
  evaluateParameter,
  aqiToPm25,
  aqiToSO2,
  aqiToO3,
  aqiToNO2,
  calculateAQIFromPM25: (pm25) => {
    const ispu = calculateISPUFromPM25(pm25);
    if (ispu == null) {
      const cat = getAQICategory(null);
      return { aqi: null, category: cat.category, color: cat.color };
    }
    const cat = getAQICategory(ispu);
    return { aqi: ispu, category: cat.category, color: cat.color };
  },
};

src/utils/auth.js:

const jwt = require('jsonwebtoken');
const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');

// Generate JWT token
const generateToken = (userId, username, role) => {
  return jwt.sign(
    { id: userId, username, role },
    process.env.JWT_SECRET,
    { expiresIn: '7d' }
  );
};

// Hash password
const hashPassword = async (password) => {
  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  return await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);
};

// Compare password
const comparePassword = async (password, hashedPassword) => {
  return await bcrypt.compare(password, hashedPassword);
};

// Verify JWT token
const verifyToken = (token) => {
  try {
    return jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
};

module.exports = {
  generateToken,
  hashPassword,
  comparePassword,
  verifyToken
};

const TIME_RANGES = {

function getTimeSlot(hour) {
  if (hour >= 6  && hour < 8)  return '06-08';
  if (hour >= 8  && hour < 12) return '08-12';
  if (hour >= 12 && hour < 16) return '12-16';
  if (hour >= 16 && hour < 18) return '16-18';
  if (hour >= 18 && hour < 20) return '18-20';
  return '20-06';
}

function randomInRange(min, max, mean) {
  const mode = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, mean ?? (min + max) / 2));
  const u = Math.random();
  const f = (mode - min) / (max - min);

  if (u < f) {
    return min + Math.sqrt(u * (max - min) * (mode - min));
  }
  return max - Math.sqrt((1 - u) * (max - min) * (max - mode));
}

const generateSensorData = (roomType = 'normal') => {
  const now = new Date();
  const slot = getTimeSlot(now.getHours());
  const r = TIME_RANGES[slot];

  const pm25 = randomInRange(r.pm25.min, r.pm25.max, r.pm25.mean);
  const pm10 = randomInRange(r.pm10.min, r.pm10.max, r.pm10.mean);
  const co   = randomInRange(r.co.min,   r.co.max,   r.co.mean);
  const so2  = randomInRange(r.so2.min,  r.so2.max,  r.so2.mean);
  const o3   = randomInRange(r.o3.min,   r.o3.max,   r.o3.mean);
  const no2  = randomInRange(r.no2.min,  r.no2.max,  r.no2.mean);
  const temperature = randomInRange(r.temperature.min, r.temperature.max, r.temperature.mean);
  const humidity    = randomInRange(r.humidity.min,    r.humidity.max,    r.humidity.mean);

  const aqiResult = calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2);

  return {
    pm25: parseFloat(pm25.toFixed(1)),
    pm10: parseFloat(pm10.toFixed(1)),
    co: Math.round(co),
    so2: parseFloat(so2.toFixed(1)),
    o3: parseFloat(o3.toFixed(1)),
    no2: parseFloat(no2.toFixed(1)),
    temperature: parseFloat(temperature.toFixed(1)),
    humidity: Math.round(humidity),
    aqi: aqiResult.aqi,
    category: aqiResult.category,
    dominant: aqiResult.dominant,
    ispuPM25: aqiResult.ispuPM25,
    ispuPM10: aqiResult.ispuPM10,
    ispuCO: aqiResult.ispuCO,
    ispuSO2: aqiResult.ispuSO2,
    ispuO3: aqiResult.ispuO3,
    ispuNO2: aqiResult.ispuNO2,
    timeSlot: slot,
    timestamp: now,
    isAnomaly: false,
    anomalyType: null,
  };
};

const simulateAnomaly = (normalData) => {
  const roll = Math.random();

  // 5% SPIKE
  if (roll < 0.05) {
    const subRoll = Math.random();
    let multiplier;

    if (subRoll < 0.60) {
      multiplier = 8 + Math.random() * 2;
    } else if (subRoll < 0.90) {
      multiplier = 10 + Math.random() * 3;
    } else {
      multiplier = 13 + Math.random() * 4;
    }

    const pm25 = normalData.pm25 * multiplier;
    const pm10 = normalData.pm10 * multiplier;
    const co   = normalData.co * (1.5 + Math.random());
    const so2  = normalData.so2 * multiplier;
    const o3   = normalData.o3 * multiplier;
    const no2  = normalData.no2 * multiplier;

    const aqiResult = calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2);

    return {
      ...normalData,
      pm25: parseFloat(pm25.toFixed(1)),
      pm10: parseFloat(pm10.toFixed(1)),
      co: Math.round(co),
      so2: parseFloat(so2.toFixed(1)),
      o3: parseFloat(o3.toFixed(1)),
      no2: parseFloat(no2.toFixed(1)),
      aqi: aqiResult.aqi,
      category: aqiResult.category,
      dominant: aqiResult.dominant,
      ispuPM25: aqiResult.ispuPM25,
      ispuPM10: aqiResult.ispuPM10,
      ispuCO: aqiResult.ispuCO,
      ispuSO2: aqiResult.ispuSO2,
      ispuO3: aqiResult.ispuO3,
      ispuNO2: aqiResult.ispuNO2,
      isAnomaly: true,
      anomalyType: 'spike',
    };
  }

  // 3% SENSOR ERROR
  if (roll < 0.08) {
    return {
      ...normalData,
      pm25: -1,
      pm10: -1,
      co: -1,
      so2: -1,
      o3: -1,
      no2: -1,
      temperature: -1,
      humidity: -1,
      aqi: -1,
      category: 'error',
      isAnomaly: true,
      anomalyType: 'error',
    };
  }

  return normalData;
};

module.exports = {
  generateSensorData,
  simulateAnomaly,
  getTimeSlot,
  TIME_RANGES,
};

const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');

// ============================================================
// KONFIGURASI EMAIL (SENDCOREX / EMAILSBIT SMTP)
// ============================================================
const SMTP_HOST = process.env.SMTP_HOST || 'smtp.emailsbit.com';
const SMTP_PORT = parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT || '588', 10);
const SMTP_USER = process.env.SMTP_USERNAME;
const SMTP_PASS = process.env.SMTP_PASSWORD; // Diambil dari Environment Render
const SMTP_FROM = process.env.SMTP_FROM || 'unilaaqi@inboxtaken.com';

if (!SMTP_USER || !SMTP_PASS) {
  console.error('❌ FATAL: SMTP_USERNAME dan SMTP_PASSWORD wajib di-set di Render Environment Variables.');
  process.exit(1);
}

const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
  host: SMTP_HOST,
  port: SMTP_PORT,
  secure: false,       // Wajib false untuk STARTTLS (port 588)
  requireTLS: true,    // Memaksa koneksi dienkripsi via STARTTLS
  auth: {
    user: SMTP_USER,
    pass: SMTP_PASS,
  },
  connectionTimeout: 15000, // Toleransi 15 detik agar tidak langsung timeout
});

// Verifikasi koneksi saat server menyala
transporter.verify((error) => {
  if (error) {
    console.error('⚠️  SMTP transporter verification failed:', error.message);
  } else {
    console.log('✅ SMTP transporter ready (EmailSbit/Sendcorex)');
  }
});

const generateResetCode = () => {
  return Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
};

const Building = require('../models/Building');
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const IoTDevice = require('../models/IoTDevice');

const sampleBuildings = [
  {

const seedSampleData = async () => {
  try {
    console.log('🌱 Seeding sample data...');

    // CREATE BUILDINGS tanpa menggunakan save() yang trigger hooks
    const buildings = {};
    
    for (const buildingData of sampleBuildings) {
      try {
        // Cek apakah building sudah ada
        const existingBuilding = await Building.findOne({ code: buildingData.code });
        
        if (existingBuilding) {
          console.log(`⏩ Building ${buildingData.code} already exists, skipping`);
          buildings[buildingData.code] = existingBuilding;
          continue;
        }

        // Gunakan insertOne untuk bypass hooks
        const result = await Building.collection.insertOne({
          ...buildingData,
          roomCount: 0,
          createdAt: new Date(),
          updatedAt: new Date()
        });
        
        const building = await Building.findById(result.insertedId);
        buildings[buildingData.code] = building;
        console.log(`✅ Created building: ${building.name} (${building.code})`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error creating building ${buildingData.code}:`, error.message);
      }
    }

    // CREATE ROOMS dengan buildingName yang benar
    let roomCount = 0;
    for (const roomData of sampleRooms) {
      try {
        const building = buildings[roomData.buildingCode];
        if (building) {
          // Cek apakah room sudah ada
          const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({ 
            name: roomData.name, 
            building: building._id 
          });
          
          if (existingRoom) {
            console.log(`⏩ Room ${roomData.name} already exists, skipping`);
            continue;
          }

          // Gunakan insertOne untuk bypass hooks dengan buildingName yang benar
          const result = await Room.collection.insertOne({
            name: roomData.name,
            building: building._id,
            buildingName: building.name,
            dataSource: roomData.dataSource,
            currentAQI: 0,
            currentData: {
              pm25: 0,
              pm10: 0,
              co2: 0,
              temperature: 0,
              humidity: 0,
              updatedAt: new Date()
            },
            createdAt: new Date(),
            updatedAt: new Date()
          });
          
          // Update building room count secara manual
          await Building.collection.updateOne(
            { _id: building._id },
            { $inc: { roomCount: 1 } }
          );
          
          roomCount++;
          console.log(`✅ Created room: ${roomData.name} in ${building.name}`);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error creating room ${roomData.name}:`, error.message);
      }
    }

    console.log(`🎉 Sample data seeding completed! Created ${roomCount} rooms.`);
    return { success: true, message: `Created ${roomCount} rooms` };
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error seeding sample data:', error);
    return { success: false, message: error.message };
  }
};

const clearSampleData = async () => {
  try {
    await Room.deleteMany({});
    await Building.deleteMany({});
    console.log('🗑️  Sample data cleared');
    return { success: true, message: 'Data cleared' };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error clearing data:', error);
    return { success: false, message: error.message };
  }
};

// Fungsi untuk sync building names
const syncBuildingNames = async () => {
  try {
    console.log('🔄 Syncing building names in rooms...');
    
    const rooms = await Room.find().populate('building', 'name');
    let updatedCount = 0;
    
    for (const room of rooms) {
      if (room.building && room.buildingName !== room.building.name) {
        room.buildingName = room.building.name;
        await room.save();
        updatedCount++;
      }
    }
    
    console.log(`✅ Synced building names for ${updatedCount} rooms`);
    return { success: true, message: `Synced ${updatedCount} rooms` };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error syncing building names:', error);
    return { success: false, message: error.message };
  }
};

// PERHATIAN: Ekspor dengan nama yang benar!
module.exports = {
  seedSampleData,
  clearSampleData,
  syncBuildingNames  // Tambahkan fungsi sync
};

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
src/app.js:

const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const express = require('express');
const cors = require('cors');
const http = require('http');
const socketIo = require('socket.io');
const simulationService = require('./services/simulationService');
const iotService = require('./services/iotService');
const { seedSampleData } = require('./utils/seedData');
const seedRoutes = require('./routes/seedRoutes');
const testRoutes = require('./routes/testRoutes');
const buildingRoutes = require('./routes/buildingRoutes');
const roomRoutes = require('./routes/roomRoutes');
const iotDeviceRoutes = require('./routes/iotDeviceRoutes');
const sensorDataRoutes = require('./routes/sensorDataRoutes');

require('dotenv').config();

const User = require('./models/User');
const Building = require('./models/Building');
const Room = require('./models/Room');
const SensorData = require('./models/SensorData');
const IoTDevice = require('./models/IoTDevice');
const authRoutes = require('./routes/authRoutes');
const { authMiddleware } = require('./middleware/authMiddleware');

// ============================================================
// 1) VALIDASI ENVIRONMENT VARIABLE (FAIL FAST)
// ============================================================
// Di Render / production, kita tidak mau server jalan tanpa
// konfigurasi yang benar. Lebih baik crash di awal dengan pesan
// jelas, daripada 500 error yang membingungkan nanti.
if (!process.env.JWT_SECRET) {
  console.error('❌ FATAL: JWT_SECRET is not defined in environment variables');
  process.exit(1);
}

if (!process.env.MONGODB_URI) {
  console.error('❌ FATAL: MONGODB_URI is not defined in environment variables');
  process.exit(1);
}

const PORT = process.env.PORT || 5000;
const HOST = '0.0.0.0'; // ✅ WAJIB untuk Render / Docker / VPS

// ============================================================
// 2) SETUP EXPRESS + HTTP SERVER + SOCKET.IO
// ============================================================
const app = express();
const server = http.createServer(app);

// Handler error di level HTTP server (mis. port bentrok)
server.on('error', (err) => {
  console.error('❌ Server error:', err.message);
  if (err.code === 'EADDRINUSE') {
    console.error(`   Port ${PORT} sudah digunakan.`);
    console.error('   Hentikan proses lain di port tersebut atau ubah PORT di .env');
  }
  process.exit(1);
});

const io = socketIo(server, {
  cors: {
    origin: '*',
    methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'DELETE'],
    credentials: true,
  },
  // ✅ Timeout lebih longgar untuk koneksi mobile (jaringan tidak stabil)
  pingTimeout: 60000,
  pingInterval: 25000,
});

// ============================================================
// 3) MIDDLEWARE
// ============================================================
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json({ limit: '1mb' })); // batasi payload agar tidak disalahgunakan
app.set('socketio', io);

// ============================================================
// 4) ROUTES
// ============================================================
app.use('/api/auth', authRoutes);
app.use('/api/seed', seedRoutes);
app.use('/api/test', testRoutes);
app.use('/api/buildings', buildingRoutes);
app.use('/api/rooms', roomRoutes);
app.use('/api/iot-devices', iotDeviceRoutes);
app.use('/api/sensor-data', sensorDataRoutes);

// Basic route (health check)
app.get('/', (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    message: 'UNILA AQI Backend API is running',
    database: mongoose.connection.readyState === 1 ? 'Connected' : 'Disconnected',
    timestamp: new Date(),
    socketIO: 'Active',
    env: process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development',
  });
});

app.get('/api/simple-test', (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    message: 'Simple test endpoint working',
    timestamp: new Date(),
    status: 'OK',
    dbConnected: mongoose.connection.readyState === 1,
    socketConnected: io !== undefined,
  });
});

// ============================================================
// 5) SOCKET.IO CONNECTION HANDLER
// ============================================================
io.on('connection', (socket) => {
  console.log(`🔌 New client connected: ${socket.id}`);

  socket.on('join-room', (roomId) => {
    socket.join(roomId);
    console.log(`📡 Socket ${socket.id} joined room: ${roomId}`);
    socket.emit('room-joined', {
      roomId: roomId,
      message: `Connected to room ${roomId}`,
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
  });

  socket.on('leave-room', (roomId) => {
    socket.leave(roomId);
    console.log(`📡 Socket ${socket.id} left room: ${roomId}`);
  });

  socket.on('subscribe-dashboard', () => {
    socket.join('dashboard');
    console.log(`📊 Socket ${socket.id} subscribed to dashboard updates`);
    socket.emit('dashboard-subscribed', {
      message: 'Subscribed to dashboard updates',
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
  });

  socket.on('unsubscribe-dashboard', () => {
    socket.leave('dashboard');
    console.log(`📊 Socket ${socket.id} unsubscribed from dashboard`);
  });

  socket.on('subscribe-room-updates', (roomId) => {
    socket.join(`room-updates-${roomId}`);
    console.log(`📡 Socket ${socket.id} subscribed to room ${roomId} updates`);
  });

  socket.on('request-refresh', (data) => {
    const roomId = data?.roomId;
    console.log(`🔄 Socket ${socket.id} requested refresh${roomId ? ` for room ${roomId}` : ''}`);

    if (roomId) {
      socket.to(roomId).emit('room-refresh-requested', {
        requestedBy: socket.id,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
    } else {
      simulationService.updateAllRooms().catch((e) => {
        console.error('❌ simulationService.updateAllRooms error:', e.message);
      });
      iotService.pollAllIoTDevices().catch((e) => {
        console.error('❌ iotService.pollAllIoTDevices error:', e.message);
      });

      io.emit('refresh-started', {
        requestedBy: socket.id,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
    }

    socket.emit('refresh-acknowledged', {
      message: 'Refresh request received',
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
  });

  socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
    console.log(`🔌 Client disconnected: ${socket.id} (reason: ${reason})`);
    socket.to('dashboard').emit('client-disconnected', {
      socketId: socket.id,
      timestamp: new Date(),
      reason: reason,
    });
  });

  socket.on('ping', (data) => {
    const clientTime = data?.timestamp;
    socket.emit('pong', {
      serverTime: new Date(),
      clientTime: clientTime,
      latency: clientTime ? Date.now() - new Date(clientTime).getTime() : null,
    });
  });

  socket.emit('connected', {
    socketId: socket.id,
    message: 'Connected to UNILA AQI WebSocket server',
    timestamp: new Date(),
    serverInfo: {
      name: 'UNILA AQI Backend',
      version: '1.0.0',
      features: ['real-time-updates', 'room-monitoring', 'dashboard-updates'],
    },
  });
});

// ============================================================
// 6) HELPER BROADCAST (opsional — dipakai modul lain bila perlu)
// ============================================================
const broadcastRoomUpdate = (roomId, data) => {
  if (io) {
    io.to(roomId).emit('room-update', {
      roomId,
      data,
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
    console.log(`📢 Broadcast update to room ${roomId}: AQI ${data.aqi}`);
  }
};

const broadcastToAll = (event, data) => {
  if (io) {
    io.emit(event, data);
  }
};

module.exports = { broadcastRoomUpdate, broadcastToAll };

// ============================================================
// 7) SEED ADMIN & SYNC HELPERS

async function syncBuildingNames() {
  try {
    console.log('🔄 Syncing building names in rooms...');
    const rooms = await Room.find().populate('building', 'name');
    let updatedCount = 0;

    for (const room of rooms) {
      if (room.building && room.buildingName !== room.building.name) {
        room.buildingName = room.building.name;
        await room.save();
        updatedCount++;
      }
    }

    if (updatedCount > 0) {
      console.log(`✅ Synced building names for ${updatedCount} rooms`);
    } else {
      console.log('✅ All room building names are already synced');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error syncing building names:', error.message);
  }
}

const seedInitialAdmin = async () => {
  try {
    const { hashPassword } = require('./utils/auth');
    const adminExists = await User.findOne({ role: 'admin' });

    if (!adminExists) {
      const hashedPassword = await hashPassword('admin123');
      const admin = new User({
        username: 'admin',
        password: hashedPassword,
        email: 'admin@unila-aqi.ac.id',
        role: 'admin',
      });

      await admin.save();
      console.log('✅ Initial admin user created');
      console.log('👤 Username: admin');
      console.log('🔑 Password: admin123');
    } else {
      console.log('✅ Admin user already exists');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error seeding admin:', error);
  }
};

    await simulationService.forceUpdate();
    await iotService.forcePollAll();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Force refresh completed for both services',
      timestamp: new Date(),
      services: {
        simulation: 'Forced update completed',
        iot: 'Forced polling completed',
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Force refresh error:', error);

      message: 'Gagal melakukan refresh',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
});

app.use((req, res) => {
  res.status(404).json({
    success: false,
    message: 'Endpoint not found',
    path: req.originalUrl,
    method: req.method,
  });
});

app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
  console.error('❌ Unhandled error:', err.message);
  res.status(err.status || 500).json({
    success: false,
    message: err.message || 'Internal server error',
  });
});

// ============================================================
// 10) MONGODB CONNECT + START SERVER
// ============================================================
// ✅ KUNCI PERUBAHAN:
//   - Semua inisialisasi (syncIndexes, seed, start service)
//     dijalankan SETELAH MongoDB tersambung.
//   - server.listen dipanggil SETELAH semuanya siap.
//   - Kalau koneksi DB gagal, proses keluar (exit 1) sehingga
//     Render tahu deploy gagal dan tidak meneruskan trafik.

mongoose
  .connect(process.env.MONGODB_URI, {
    serverSelectionTimeoutMS: 15000, // toleransi cold start Atlas
  })
  .then(async () => {
    console.log('✅ Connected to MongoDB Atlas');
    console.log(`📊 Database: ${mongoose.connection.db.databaseName}`);
    console.log(`📍 Host: ${mongoose.connection.host}`);

    // Drop index lama & buat ulang yang sparse untuk Building.code
    try {
      await Building.syncIndexes();
      console.log('✅ Building indexes synced (code sparse)');
    } catch (syncErr) {
      console.error('⚠️  Building index sync warning:', syncErr.message);
    }

    // Seed admin & sync building names
    await seedInitialAdmin();
    await syncBuildingNames();

    // Seed sample buildings & rooms jika kosong
    const buildingCount = await Building.countDocuments();
    if (buildingCount === 0) {
      console.log('🌱 Seeding sample data...');
      await seedSampleData();
    } else {
      console.log(`📊 Database already has ${buildingCount} buildings, skipping sample seeding`);
    }

    // Start background services
    simulationService.start(io);
    iotService.start(io);

    console.log('✅ All services started');
    console.log('   - Simulation Service: ACTIVE (30 second intervals)');
    console.log('   - IoT Service: ACTIVE (30 second intervals)');

    // ✅ START SERVER SETELAH SEMUA SIAP
    server.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
      console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
      console.log(`🚀 Server running on http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
      console.log(`🌐 API Base URL: /api`);
      console.log(`📡 WebSocket ready`);
      console.log(`🔌 Socket.IO ready for real-time updates`);
      console.log(`🌍 Environment: ${process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development'}`);
      console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
    });
  })
  .catch((err) => {
    console.error('❌ MongoDB connection error:', err.message);
    const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI || '';
    const maskedUri = uri.replace(/:[^:@]*@/, ':****@');
    console.error('Connection string:', maskedUri);
    console.error('⚠️  Server tidak akan dijalankan sampai DB tersambung.');
    process.exit(1); // ✅ Render akan tahu deploy gagal
  });

// ============================================================
// 11) GRACEFUL SHUTDOWN (penting untuk Render)
// ============================================================
// Render mengirim SIGTERM saat:
//   - deploy versi baru (container lama dimatikan)
//   - manual restart
//   - scale down
// Kita beri waktu 10 detik untuk menutup koneksi DB & socket,
// lalu exit. Tanpa ini, Render akan force-kill setelah 30 detik
// dan mungkin menyisakan koneksi "zombie" di MongoDB Atlas.

let isShuttingDown = false;

const shutdown = async (signal) => {
  if (isShuttingDown) return;
  isShuttingDown = true;

  console.log(`\n⚠️  Received ${signal}. Shutting down gracefully...`);

  // 1) Stop background services
  try {
    simulationService.stop();
    iotService.stop();
    console.log('⏹️  Background services stopped');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('⚠️  Error stopping services:', e.message);
  }

  // 2) Tutup Socket.IO
  try {
    io.close(() => console.log('🔌 Socket.IO closed'));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('⚠️  Error closing Socket.IO:', e.message);
  }

  // 3) Tutup HTTP server
  server.close(() => {
    console.log('🛑 HTTP server closed');
  });

  // 4) Disconnect MongoDB
  try {
    await mongoose.disconnect();
    console.log('🔌 MongoDB disconnected');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('⚠️  Error disconnecting MongoDB:', e.message);
  }

  // 5) Paksa exit setelah 10 detik jika masih menggantung
  setTimeout(() => {
    console.error('⏱️  Forcing exit after 10s');
    process.exit(1);
  }, 10000).unref();

  process.exit(0);
};

process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));

// Tangkap unhandled rejection agar tidak crash diam-diam
process.on('unhandledRejection', (reason) => {
  console.error('❌ Unhandled Rejection:', reason);
});

process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => {
  console.error('❌ Uncaught Exception:', err);
  // Jangan langsung exit — biarkan graceful shutdown jika ada
  shutdown('uncaughtException');
});
