lib/presentation/pages/auth/forgot_password_screen.dart:

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/helpers.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/validators.dart';
import '../../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import 'reset_password_screen.dart';

class ForgotPasswordScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ForgotPasswordScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<ForgotPasswordScreen> createState() => _ForgotPasswordScreenState();
}

class _ForgotPasswordScreenState extends State<ForgotPasswordScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();

  bool _isLoading = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleSendResetLink() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final response =
          await ApiService().forgotPassword(_emailController.text.trim());

      if (response['success'] == true && mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);

        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          'Kode verifikasi telah dikirim ke email Anda',
        );

        Navigator.push(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(
            builder: (context) => ResetPasswordScreen(
              email: _emailController.text.trim(),
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);

      // Server mati -> SnackBar khusus, jangan navigasi
      if (Helpers.isNetworkError(e)) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
        return;
      }

      final rawMsg = e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
      final message = rawMsg.contains('tidak terdaftar')
          ? 'Email tidak sesuai / tidak terdaftar sebagai admin'
          : rawMsg;

      Helpers.showSnackBar(context, message, isError: true);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
        title: const Text('UNILA Air Quality Index'),
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 80,
                height: 80,
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: const Icon(Icons.lock_reset,
                    size: 40, color: Colors.white),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Text(
                'LUPA PASSWORD',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                    ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Text(
                'Masukkan email admin yang terdaftar',
                style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 16, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 32),
              Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _emailController,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Email',
                        prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email),
                        border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                      ),
                      validator: Validators.validateEmail,
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                      textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: double.infinity,
                      height: 50,
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed:
                            _isLoading ? null : _handleSendResetLink,
                        child: const Text(
                          'KIRIM LINK RESET',
                          style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

lib/presentation/pages/auth/reset_password_screen.dart:

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/helpers.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/validators.dart';
import '../../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import 'admin_login_screen.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';


class ResetPasswordScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String email;

  const ResetPasswordScreen({super.key, required this.email});

  @override
  State<ResetPasswordScreen> createState() => _ResetPasswordScreenState();
}

class _ResetPasswordScreenState extends State<ResetPasswordScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _codeController = TextEditingController();   // ✅ ganti 6 controller
  final _codeFocusNode = FocusNode();    
  final _newPasswordController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmPasswordController = TextEditingController();

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _obscureNewPassword = true;
  bool _obscureConfirmPassword = true;
  int _countdown = 60;
  bool _canResend = false;

  @override
void initState() {
  super.initState();
  _codeController.addListener(() => setState(() {})); // update kotak visual
  _startCountdown();
}

  @override
void dispose() {
  _codeController.dispose();
  _codeFocusNode.dispose();
  _newPasswordController.dispose();
  _confirmPasswordController.dispose();
  super.dispose();
}

  // ============================================================
  // COUNTDOWN UNTUK KIRIM ULANG KODE
  // ============================================================
  void _startCountdown() {
    Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 1), () {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          if (_countdown > 0) {
            _countdown--;
            _startCountdown();
          } else {
            _canResend = true;
          }
        });
      }
    });
  }

  // ============================================================
  // RESET PASSWORD (VALIDASI KODE + SIMPAN PASSWORD BARU)
  // ============================================================
   Future<void> _handleResetPassword() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    final code = _codeController.text;
    if (code.length != 6) {
      Helpers.showSnackBar(
        context,
        'Masukkan 6 digit kode dengan lengkap',
        isError: true,
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final response = await ApiService().resetPasswordWithCode(
        email: widget.email,
        code: code,
        newPassword: _newPasswordController.text,
      );

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (response['success'] == true) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
        _showSuccessDialog();
      } else {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          response['message'] ?? 'Gagal mereset password',
          isError: true,
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);

      // Server mati -> SnackBar khusus, tidak reset password
      if (Helpers.isNetworkError(e)) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
        return;
      }

      final rawMsg = e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
      String message = rawMsg;
      if (rawMsg.toLowerCase().contains('kode') &&
          rawMsg.toLowerCase().contains('salah')) {
        message =
            'Kode verifikasi salah. Periksa kembali kode yang Anda masukkan.';
      } else if (rawMsg.toLowerCase().contains('kedaluwarsa') ||
          rawMsg.toLowerCase().contains('expired')) {
        message = 'Kode sudah kedaluwarsa. Silakan kirim ulang kode.';
      }

      Helpers.showSnackBar(context, message, isError: true);
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // KIRIM ULANG KODE (RESEND)
  // ============================================================
  Future<void> _resendCode() async {
    if (!_canResend) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final response = await ApiService().resendResetCode(widget.email);

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (response['success'] == true) {
        setState(() {
          _isLoading = false;
          _canResend = false;
          _countdown = 60;
        });

        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          'Kode verifikasi baru telah dikirim ke email Anda',
        );

        _startCountdown();
      } else {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          response['message'] ?? 'Gagal mengirim ulang kode',
          isError: true,
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);

      if (Helpers.isNetworkError(e)) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
        return;
      }

      final rawMsg = e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
      Helpers.showSnackBar(
        context,
        'Gagal mengirim ulang kode: $rawMsg',
        isError: true,
      );
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // DIALOG SUKSES
  // ============================================================
  void _showSuccessDialog() {
  showDialog(
    context: context,
    barrierDismissible: false,
    builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Berhasil'),
      content: const Text(
        'Password telah berhasil direset. '
        'Silakan login kembali menggunakan password baru Anda.',
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () {
            Navigator.pop(dialogContext); // Tutup dialog

            // 🔥 Kembali ke halaman login admin, bersihkan stack
            Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (context) => const AdminLoginScreen(),
              ),
              (route) => false,
            );
          },
          child: const Text('LOGIN SEKARANG'),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
}

  // ============================================================
  // HELPER: MASKED EMAIL
  // ============================================================
  String _maskedEmail(String email) {
    final parts = email.split('@');
    if (parts.length != 2) return email;
    final username = parts[0];
    final domain = parts[1];
    if (username.length <= 3) return email;
    final masked = username.substring(0, 3) + '*' * (username.length - 3);
    return '$masked@$domain';
  }

  // ============================================================
  // UI BUILD
  // ============================================================
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
        title: const Text('UNILA Air Quality Index'),
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              // Logo
              Container(
                width: 80,
                height: 80,
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: const Icon(
                  Icons.password,
                  size: 40,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Title
              Text(
                'RESET PASSWORD',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                    ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Instruction
              const Text(
                'Kode verifikasi telah dikirim ke email:',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 16,
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                _maskedEmail(widget.email),
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                ),
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 32),

              // Form
              Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    // Verification Code
                    const Text(
                      'Masukkan 6 digit kode verifikasi:',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    GestureDetector(
  onTap: () => _codeFocusNode.requestFocus(),
  child: Stack(
    children: [
      // TextField asli, disembunyikan (opacity 0) tapi tetap menerima input
      Opacity(
        opacity: 0,
        child: TextField(
          controller: _codeController,
          focusNode: _codeFocusNode,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
          maxLength: 6,
          inputFormatters: [
            FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly, // hanya angka
          ],
          decoration: const InputDecoration(counterText: ''),
        ),
      ),

      // Kotak visual 6 digit
      Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
        children: List.generate(6, (index) {
          final hasChar = _codeController.text.length > index;
          final isCursorHere =
              _codeFocusNode.hasFocus &&
              _codeController.text.length == index;
          final isFocused = isCursorHere;

          return Container(
            width: 50,
            height: 56,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              border: Border.all(
                color: isFocused
                    ? AppColors.primary
                    : AppColors.border,
                width: isFocused ? 2 : 1,
              ),
            ),
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: Text(
              hasChar ? _codeController.text[index] : '',
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 22,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              ),
            ),
          );
        }),
      ),
    ],
  ),
),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),

                    // New Password
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _newPasswordController,
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Password Baru*',
                        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock),
                        suffixIcon: IconButton(
                          icon: Icon(
                            _obscureNewPassword
                                ? Icons.visibility_off
                                : Icons.visibility,
                          ),
                          onPressed: () {
                            setState(() =>
                                _obscureNewPassword = !_obscureNewPassword);
                          },
                        ),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                      ),
                      obscureText: _obscureNewPassword,
                      validator: Validators.validatePassword,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),

                    // Confirm Password
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _confirmPasswordController,
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Konfirmasi Password Baru*',
                        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock),
                        suffixIcon: IconButton(
                          icon: Icon(
                            _obscureConfirmPassword
                                ? Icons.visibility_off
                                : Icons.visibility,
                          ),
                          onPressed: () {
                            setState(() => _obscureConfirmPassword =
                                !_obscureConfirmPassword);
                          },
                        ),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                      ),
                      obscureText: _obscureConfirmPassword,
                      validator: (value) => Validators.validateConfirmPassword(
                        value,
                        _newPasswordController.text,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),

                    // Reset Button
                    SizedBox(
                      width: double.infinity,
                      height: 50,
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleResetPassword,
                        child: const Text(
                          'RESET PASSWORD',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),

              // Resend Code
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: _canResend && !_isLoading ? _resendCode : null,
                    child: Text(
                      _canResend
                          ? 'Kirim ulang kode'
                          : 'Kirim ulang kode (${_countdown}s)',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: _canResend
                            ? AppColors.primary
                            : AppColors.textDisabled,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),

              // Password Requirements
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.surface,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
                ),
                child: const Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Ketentuan Password:',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                      ),
                    ),
                    SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      '• Minimal 8 karakter\n'
                      '• Kombinasi huruf dan angka\n'
                      '• Hindari penggunaan password yang mudah ditebak',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 14,
                        color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

lib/presentation/pages/dashboard/dashboard_screen.dart:

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/socket_service.dart';
import '../../../data/models/room.dart';
import '../../../presentation/providers/room_provider.dart';
import '../../../presentation/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../../presentation/widgets/common/drawer.dart';
import '../../../presentation/widgets/common/building_section.dart';
import '../../../presentation/pages/room/room_detail_screen.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/helpers.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../pages/info/aqi_info_screen.dart';
import '../../../presentation/widgets/common/server_status_indicator.dart';

class DashboardScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool isAdminMode;

  const DashboardScreen({
    super.key,
    this.isAdminMode = false,
  });

  @override
  State<DashboardScreen> createState() => _DashboardScreenState();
}

class _DashboardScreenState extends State<DashboardScreen> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();

  // Timer? _autoRefreshTimer;
  // int _autoRefreshCountdown = 10;
  bool _isMounted = false;
  bool _isRefreshing = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _isMounted = true;

    // Delay initialization to avoid setState during build
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
    if (!_isMounted) return;

    // 🔥 FIXED: sinkronkan search field dengan state provider.
    // Kalau provider masih menyimpan query lama (mis. dari sesi
    // sebelumnya), UI tidak akan menampilkan "Belum ada data"
    // tanpa filter yang terlihat.
    final roomProvider = Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);
    if (roomProvider.searchQuery.isNotEmpty) {
      _searchController.text = roomProvider.searchQuery;
    }

    _loadInitialData();
    _setupBuildingUpdateListener();
    _setupRoomNameUpdateListener();
  });
}

  // 🔥 BARU: Setup listener untuk update nama ruangan
  // FIXED: guard data agar tidak TypeError saat payload rusak.
  void _setupRoomNameUpdateListener() {
    Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);
    final socketService = SocketService();

    // Listen for room name updates
    socketService.on('room-name-changed', (data) {
      if (!_isMounted) return;
      final map = _asMap(data);
      if (map == null) return;

      final oldName = map['oldName']?.toString() ?? '-';
      final newName = map['newName']?.toString() ?? '-';

      print('🔄 Room name update received: $oldName -> $newName');

      // Refresh data untuk update tampilan
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (_isMounted) {
          _refreshData();

          // Show notification
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text('Nama ruangan diperbarui: $oldName -> $newName'),
              backgroundColor: Colors.blue,
              duration: Duration(seconds: 2),
            ),
          );
        }
      });
    });

    // Listen for dashboard-room-updated events
    // FIXED: guard data + nested access.
    socketService.on('dashboard-room-updated', (data) {
      if (!_isMounted) return;
      final map = _asMap(data);
      if (map == null) return;
      if (map['action'] != 'updated') return;

      print('📡 Dashboard room update received');

      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (_isMounted) {
          _refreshData();
        }
      });
    });
  }

  // FIXED: guard data agar tidak TypeError saat payload rusak.
  void _setupBuildingUpdateListener() {
    Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);
    final socketService = SocketService();

    // Listen for building updates
    socketService.on('building-updated', (data) {
      if (!_isMounted) return;
      final map = _asMap(data);
      if (map == null) return;

      print('🏢 Building update received: ${map['action']}');

      // Jika building diedit, refresh data
      if (map['action'] == 'updated') {
        WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
          if (_isMounted) {
            _refreshData();

            // Show notification
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              const SnackBar(
                content: Text('Data gedung diperbarui'),
                backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                duration: Duration(seconds: 2),
              ),
            );
          }
        });
      }
    });
  }

  // FIXED: helper untuk memvalidasi payload socket.
  Map<String, dynamic>? _asMap(dynamic data) {
    if (data == null) return null;
    if (data is Map<String, dynamic>) return data;
    if (data is Map) {
      try {
        return Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _isMounted = false;
    _searchController.dispose();
    // _autoRefreshTimer?.cancel();

    // Remove event listeners
    final socketService = SocketService();
    socketService.off('building-updated');
    socketService.off('room-name-changed'); // 🔥 BARU: Hapus listener nama ruangan
    socketService.off('dashboard-room-updated'); // 🔥 BARU: Hapus listener dashboard

    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadInitialData() async {
  if (!_isMounted) return;

  final roomProvider = Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);

  // 1) Tampilkan cache dulu (instant, tanpa spinner)
  await roomProvider.loadFromCache();
  if (!_isMounted) return;

  // 2) Fetch dari server
  try {
    await roomProvider.loadRooms();
  } catch (_) {
    // sudah ditangani di provider
  }
  if (!_isMounted) return;

  // 3) Tangani state pasca-fetch
  if (roomProvider.hasError) {
    final isNetwork = Helpers.isNetworkError(roomProvider.errorMessage);

    if (roomProvider.rooms.isEmpty) {
      // Kasus yang diminta user:
      // tidak ada cache SAMA SEKALI + server mati → build() akan
      // menampilkan error state layar penuh. Snackbar sebagai
      // pemberitahuan tambahan.
      if (isNetwork) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
      } else {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          'Gagal memuat data ruangan',
          isError: true,
        );
      }
    } else {
      // Ada cache, fetch gagal → tetap tampilkan cache,
      // cukup beri notifikasi bahwa data mungkin tidak terbaru.
      if (isNetwork) {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(
          context,
          'Server tidak dapat dihubungi. Menampilkan data terakhir.',
          isError: true,
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

  Future<void> _handleManualRefresh() async {
  if (_isRefreshing) return;
  setState(() => _isRefreshing = true);
  try {
    await _refreshData();
  } finally {
    if (mounted) setState(() => _isRefreshing = false);
  }
}

  Future<void> _refreshData() async {
    if (!_isMounted) return;

    final roomProvider = Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);

    try {
      await roomProvider.refresh();
    } catch (e) {
      if (_isMounted) {
        if (Helpers.isNetworkError(e)) {
          Helpers.showServerError(context);
        } else {
          Helpers.showSnackBar(context, 'Gagal refresh: $e', isError: true);
        }
      }
    }

    // Cek provider error juga (refresh menangkap internal)
    if (_isMounted && roomProvider.hasError) {
      final msg = roomProvider.errorMessage.toLowerCase();
      final isNet = msg.contains('socketexception') ||
          msg.contains('network') ||
          msg.contains('connection') ||
          msg.contains('timeout') ||
          msg.contains('failed host');
      if (isNet) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _refreshDataSilently() async {
  if (!_isMounted) return;

  final roomProvider = Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);

  try {
    // `refresh()` di provider sudah punya guard `if (_isRefreshing) return;`
    // sehingga aman dipanggil meskipun timer 60 detik sedang berjalan.
    await roomProvider.refresh();
  } catch (e) {
    // Silent — cukup log, jangan ganggu user dengan snackbar
    debugPrint('Auto-refresh on return failed: $e');
  }
}

  void _handleRoomTap(Room room) {
  Navigator.push(
    context,
    MaterialPageRoute(
      builder: (context) => RoomDetailScreen(room: room),
    ),
  ).then((_) {
    // FIXED: begitu user kembali dari detail ruangan, ambil data terbaru
    // dari server. Ini menangkap perubahan yang terjadi di detail
    // (manual refresh, auto-refresh 60s, atau notification socket).
    if (_isMounted) {
      _refreshDataSilently();
    }
  });
}

  // FIXED: sebelumnya tidak ada try-catch → unhandled exception saat server
  // mati. Sekarang: error di-log, navigasi tetap dijalankan di `finally`.
  void _handleLogout() async {
  try {
    final authProvider = Provider.of<AuthProvider>(context, listen: false);
    final roomProvider = Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);

    await authProvider.logout();

    // 🔥 FIXED: bersihkan state filter ruangan agar tidak "bocor"
    // ke sesi login berikutnya (search, filter gedung, sort).
    roomProvider.clearFilters();
  } catch (e) {
    debugPrint('❌ Logout error: $e');
  } finally {
    if (mounted) {
      Navigator.pushNamedAndRemoveUntil(context, '/', (route) => false);
    }
  }
}

  void _handleHelpTap() {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (context) => const AQIInfoScreen(),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final roomProvider = Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('UNILA Air Quality Index'),
        centerTitle: true,
        actions: [
          const ServerStatusIndicator(),
          IconButton(
            icon: _isRefreshing
                ? const SizedBox(
                    width: 18,
                    height: 18,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                      strokeWidth: 2,
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                    ),
                  )
                : const Icon(
                    Icons.refresh_rounded,
                    size: 22,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
            onPressed: () {
              ServerStatusIndicator.checkNow();
              _handleManualRefresh();
            },
            splashRadius: 20,
            tooltip: 'Refresh',
          ),
        ],
      ),
      drawer: AppDrawer(
      isAdmin: widget.isAdminMode,
      onDashboardTap: () => Navigator.pop(context),
      onBuildingsTap: () {
        Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/admin/buildings').then((_) {
          if (_isMounted) _refreshDataSilently();
        });
      },
      onRoomsTap: () {
        Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/admin/rooms').then((_) {
          if (_isMounted) _refreshDataSilently();
        });
      },
      onDevicesTap: () {
        Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/admin/iot-devices').then((_) {
          if (_isMounted) _refreshDataSilently();
        });
      },
      onProfileTap: () {
        Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/admin/profile').then((_) {
          if (_isMounted) _refreshDataSilently();
        });
  },
  onHelpTap: _handleHelpTap,
  onLogoutTap: _handleLogout,
),
      body: CustomScrollView(
        slivers: [
          // Search and Filter Section
          SliverToBoxAdapter(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  // Search Bar
                  TextField(
                    controller: _searchController,
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: '🔍 Search...',
                      filled: true,
                      fillColor: Colors.white,
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                      ),
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 16,
                        vertical: 12,
                      ),
                      suffixIcon: _searchController.text.isNotEmpty
                          ? IconButton(
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
                              onPressed: () {
                                _searchController.clear();
                                roomProvider.updateSearchQuery('');
                              },
                            )
                          : null,
                    ),
                    onChanged: (value) {
                      roomProvider.updateSearchQuery(value);
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  // Filter Row
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.white,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
                          ),
                          child: _buildBuildingDropdown(roomProvider),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.white,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
                          ),
                          child: _buildSortDropdown(roomProvider),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          // Loading State — hanya jika belum ada data
          if (roomProvider.isLoading && roomProvider.rooms.isEmpty)
            const SliverFillRemaining(
              child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
            )
          // Error State — hanya jika TIDAK ada data sama sekali
          else if (roomProvider.hasError && roomProvider.rooms.isEmpty)
  SliverFillRemaining(
    child: Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(
              Icons.cloud_off_rounded,
              size: 72,
              color: AppColors.textHint,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              'Tidak dapat memuat data ruangan',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              Helpers.friendlyErrorMessage(roomProvider.errorMessage),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 14,
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                height: 1.5,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            ElevatedButton.icon(
              onPressed: _refreshData,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 18),
              label: const Text('Coba Lagi'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ),
  )
          // Empty State
          else if (roomProvider.rooms.isEmpty)
  SliverFillRemaining(
    child: Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Builder(
            builder: (context) {
              // 🔥 FIXED: baca dari provider (sumber kebenaran filter),
              // bukan dari _searchController yang bisa saja belum disinkronkan.
              final hasSearch = roomProvider.searchQuery.trim().isNotEmpty;
              final hasBuildingFilter =
                  roomProvider.selectedBuilding != 'Semua Gedung';
              final hasFilter = hasSearch || hasBuildingFilter;

              final IconData icon;
              if (hasSearch) {
                icon = Icons.search_off;
              } else if (hasBuildingFilter) {
                icon = Icons.filter_alt_off_outlined;
              } else {
                icon = Icons.meeting_room_outlined;
              }

              final String message;
              if (hasSearch && hasBuildingFilter) {
                message =
                    'Tidak ada ruangan atau gedung yang cocok dengan\n'
                    '"${roomProvider.searchQuery.trim()}"\n'
                    'di ${roomProvider.selectedBuilding}';
              } else if (hasSearch) {
                message =
                    'Tidak ada ruangan atau gedung yang cocok dengan\n'
                    '"${roomProvider.searchQuery.trim()}"';
              } else if (hasBuildingFilter) {
                message =
                    'Tidak ada ruangan di gedung\n'
                    '"${roomProvider.selectedBuilding}"';
              } else {
                message = 'Belum ada data ruangan';
              }

              return Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Icon(icon, size: 64, color: Colors.grey),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    message,
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 16,
                      color: Colors.grey,
                      height: 1.5,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  if (hasFilter)
                    TextButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () {
                        roomProvider.clearFilters();
                        _searchController.clear();  // ← tetap clear controller
                      },
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 18),
                      label: const Text('Reset Filter & Pencarian'),
                    ),
                ],
              );
            },
          )
      ),
    ),
  )
          // Data State — tetap tampil walau refresh error
          else
            SliverList(
              delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                (context, index) {
                  final buildingEntries =
                      roomProvider.roomsByBuilding.entries.toList();
                  final buildingName = buildingEntries[index].key;
                  final rooms = buildingEntries[index].value;

                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                    child: BuildingSection(
                      buildingName: buildingName,
                      rooms: rooms,
                      onRoomTap: _handleRoomTap,
                    ),
                  );
                },
                childCount: roomProvider.roomsByBuilding.length,
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBuildingDropdown(RoomProvider roomProvider) {
    // Remove duplicates from building list
    final uniqueBuildings = roomProvider.buildings.toSet().toList();

    // ✅ Pastikan value selalu ada di items
    String selectedValue = roomProvider.selectedBuilding;
    if (!uniqueBuildings.contains(selectedValue)) {
      selectedValue = 'Semua Gedung';

      // Sinkronkan juga ke provider agar konsisten
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        roomProvider.updateBuildingFilter('Semua Gedung');
      });
    }

    return DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
      child: DropdownButton<String>(
        value: selectedValue, // ✅ gunakan selectedValue
        isExpanded: true,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_drop_down),
        onChanged: (value) {
          if (value != null) {
            roomProvider.updateBuildingFilter(value);
          }
        },
        items: uniqueBuildings.map((building) {
          return DropdownMenuItem(
            value: building,
            child: Text(building, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
          );
        }).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSortDropdown(RoomProvider roomProvider) {
    return DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
      child: DropdownButton<String>(
        value: roomProvider.sortBy,
        isExpanded: true,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_drop_down),
        onChanged: (value) {
          if (value != null) {
            roomProvider.updateSort(value);
          }
        },
        items: const [
          DropdownMenuItem(
            value: 'Terbaru',
            child: Text('Terbaru'),
          ),
          DropdownMenuItem(
            value: 'A-Z',
            child: Text('A-Z'),
          ),
          DropdownMenuItem(
            value: 'AQI Terbaik',
            child: Text('AQI Terbaik'),
          ),
          DropdownMenuItem(
            value: 'AQI Terburuk',
            child: Text('AQI Terburuk'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

lib/presentation/pages/info/aqi_info_screen.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../data/repositories/aqi_info_repository.dart';
import '../../../data/models/aqi_info.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/colors.dart';


class AQIInfoScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AQIInfoScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<AQIInfoScreen> createState() => _AQIInfoScreenState();
}

class _AQIInfoScreenState extends State<AQIInfoScreen> {
  final AQIInfoRepository _repository = AQIInfoRepository();
  AQIInfo? _aqiInfo;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final info = await _repository.getAQIInfo();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _aqiInfo = info;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Bantuan & Informasi'),
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _error != null
              ? _buildErrorState()
              : _buildContent(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildErrorState() {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 64, color: AppColors.textHint),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            const Text('Gagal memuat informasi', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ElevatedButton(onPressed: _loadData, child: const Text('Coba Lagi')),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildContent() {
    final info = _aqiInfo!;
    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 32),
      children: [
        _buildIntroBanner(),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: '📖',
          title: 'Apa itu AQI/ISPU?',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildWhatIsAQI(info),
        ),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: '📊',
          title: 'Parameter yang Dimonitor',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildParameters(info),
        ),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: '🎨',
          title: 'Kategori Kualitas Udara',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildCategories(info),
        ),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: '📏',
          title: 'Penjelasan Satuan',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildUnits(info),
        ),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: '🏥',
          title: 'Dampak Kesehatan & Rekomendasi',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildHealth(info),
        ),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: '🔄',
          title: 'Cara Membaca Data di Aplikasi',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildHowToRead(),
        ),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: '❓',
          title: 'FAQ / Troubleshooting',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildFAQ(),
        ),
        _ExpandableSection(
          emoji: 'ℹ️',
          title: 'Tentang Aplikasi',
          color: AppColors.primary,
          child: _buildAbout(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // INTRO BANNER
  // ============================================================
  Widget _buildIntroBanner() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.25),
            blurRadius: 12,
            offset: const Offset(0, 6),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 52,
            height: 52,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            ),
            child: const Icon(Icons.help_outline_rounded,
                color: Colors.white, size: 28),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          const Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Pusat Bantuan',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  'Ketuk tiap bagian di bawah untuk membuka penjelasan.',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12.5,
                    color: Colors.white,
                    height: 1.4,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 1. APA ITU AQI/ISPU?
  // ============================================================
  Widget _buildWhatIsAQI(AQIInfo info) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _paragraph(
          'Air Quality Index (AQI) atau ISPU (Indeks Standar Pencemar Udara) '
          'adalah angka tanpa satuan yang menunjukkan seberapa bersih atau '
          'tercemar udara pada suatu lokasi, serta dampak kesehatannya.',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        _paragraph(
          'Aplikasi ini mengikuti standar PP No. 22 Tahun 2021 tentang '
          'Penyelenggaraan Perlindungan dan Pengelolaan Lingkungan Hidup. '
          'Nilai ISPU dikelompokkan menjadi 5 kategori: Baik, Sedang, '
          'Tidak Sehat, Sangat Tidak Sehat, dan Berbahaya.',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        _paragraph(
          'ISPU dipengaruhi oleh konsentrasi partikel polusi di udara, yaitu PM2.5, PM10, '
          'serta gas CO, SO2, O3, dan NO2. Semakin tinggi konsentrasi parameter tersebut, '
          'semakin tinggi nilai ISPU, dan semakin besar risiko kesehatan bagi penghuni ruangan.',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        _paragraph(
          'Setiap parameter (PM2.5, PM10, CO, SO2, O3, dan NO2) dihitung '
          'terpisah menggunakan interpolasi linear terhadap tabel breakpoint '
          'PP 22/2021. Nilai ISPU ruangan adalah nilai MAX dari keenam parameter.',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Tabel Breakpoint PM2.5'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _breakpointTable(
          headers: const ['ISPU Bawah', 'ISPU Atas', 'Kons. Bawah', 'Kons. Atas'],
          rows: const [
            ['0', '50', '0', '55'],
            ['51', '100', '56', '150'],
            ['101', '200', '151', '250'],
            ['201', '300', '251', '350'],
            ['301', '500', '351', '500'],
          ],
          unit: 'μg/m³',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Tabel Breakpoint PM10'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _breakpointTable(
          headers: const ['ISPU Bawah', 'ISPU Atas', 'Kons. Bawah', 'Kons. Atas'],
          rows: const [
            ['0', '50', '0', '50'],
            ['51', '100', '51', '150'],
            ['101', '200', '151', '350'],
            ['201', '300', '351', '420'],
            ['301', '500', '421', '500'],
          ],
          unit: 'μg/m³',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Tabel Breakpoint CO'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _breakpointTable(
          headers: const ['ISPU Bawah', 'ISPU Atas', 'Kons. Bawah', 'Kons. Atas'],
          rows: const [
            ['0', '50', '0', '4.000'],
            ['51', '100', '4.001', '8.000'],
            ['101', '200', '8.001', '15.000'],
            ['201', '300', '15.001', '30.000'],
            ['301', '500', '30.001', '45.000'],
          ],
          unit: 'μg/m³',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Tabel Breakpoint SO2'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _breakpointTable(
          headers: const ['ISPU Bawah', 'ISPU Atas', 'Kons. Bawah', 'Kons. Atas'],
          rows: const [
            ['0', '50', '0', '80'],
            ['51', '100', '81', '365'],
            ['101', '200', '366', '800'],
            ['201', '300', '801', '1.600'],
            ['301', '500', '1.601', '3.000'],
          ],
          unit: 'μg/m³',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Tabel Breakpoint O3'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _breakpointTable(
          headers: const ['ISPU Bawah', 'ISPU Atas', 'Kons. Bawah', 'Kons. Atas'],
          rows: const [
            ['0', '50', '0', '120'],
            ['51', '100', '121', '235'],
            ['101', '200', '236', '400'],
            ['201', '300', '401', '800'],
            ['301', '500', '801', '1.000'],
          ],
          unit: 'μg/m³',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Tabel Breakpoint NO2'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _breakpointTable(
          headers: const ['ISPU Bawah', 'ISPU Atas', 'Kons. Bawah', 'Kons. Atas'],
          rows: const [
            ['0', '50', '0', '80'],
            ['51', '100', '81', '200'],
            ['101', '200', '201', '1.130'],
            ['201', '300', '1.131', '2.260'],
            ['301', '500', '2.261', '3.000'],
          ],
          unit: 'μg/m³',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Rumus ISPU'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _codeBox(
          'ISPU = ((ISPU_high − ISPU_low) / (C_high − C_low)) × (C − C_low) + ISPU_low'
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        _paragraph(
          'Keterangan:',
        ),
        _bulletList([
          'C = konsentrasi partikel (μg/m³)',
          'C_low =  batas bawah konsentrasi pada segmen',
          'C_high = batas atas konsentrasi pada segmen',
          'ISPU_low = batas bawah nilai ISPU pada segmen',
          'ISPU_high = batas atas nilai ISPU pada segmen',
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Contoh Perhitungan'),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _paragraph(
          'Diketahui PM2.5 ruangan = 100 μg/m³. Nilai ini berada pada '
          'segmen kedua, yaitu:',
        ),

        _bulletList([
          'C_low = 56',
          'C_high = 150',
          'C_high = batas atas konsentrasi pada segmen',
          'ISPU_low = 51',
          'ISPU_high = 100',
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        _codeBox(
          'ISPU = ((100 − 51) / (150 − 56)) × (100 − 56) + 51\n'
          '     = (49 / 94) × 44 + 51\n'
          '     = 22.94 + 51\n'
          '     ≈ 74',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
         _paragraph(
          'Diketahui PM10 ruangan = 30 μg/m³. Nilai ini berada pada '
          'segmen pertama, yaitu:',
        ),
        _bulletList([
          'C_low = 0',
          'C_high = 50',
          'ISPU_low = 0',
          'ISPU_high = 50',
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        _codeBox(
          'ISPU = ((30 − 0) / (50 − 0)) × (50 − 0) + 0\n'
          '     = (30 / 50) × 50\n'
          '     = 30',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12), 

        _paragraph(
          'Nilai ISPU dalam ruangan diambil dari partikel yang lebih dominan, yaitu PM2.5 yang memiliki nilai lebih tinggi dari PM10.\n'
          'Maka, ISPU ruangan tersbeut adalah 74.',
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 2. PARAMETER YANG DIMONITOR (konteks dalam ruangan)
  // ============================================================
  Widget _buildParameters(AQIInfo info) {
    final indoorParams = <AQIParameter>[
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'PM2.5',
        abbreviation: 'PM2.5',
        description:
            'Partikel halus berukuran ≤ 2.5 mikrometer yang melayang di udara '
            'ruangan. Di dalam ruangan, partikel ini berasal dari asap rokok, '
            'uap memasak, printer, lilin, dan debu halus yang tersuspensi. '
            'Ukurannya sangat kecil sehingga dapat menembus jauh ke dalam '
            'paru-paru dan masuk ke sistem peredaran darah.',
        unit: 'μg/m³',
        impact:
            'Iritasi saluran pernapasan, memperburuk asma, risiko penyakit '
            'kardiovaskular, penurunan fungsi paru-paru.',
        source:
            'Asap rokok, uap memasak, printer, lilin, debu furnitur, VOC '
            'dari cat/furnitur baru, partikel dari ventilasi.',
        safeRange: '0 – 55 μg/m³ (ISPU ≤ 50)',
        icon: '🏗️',
      ),
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'PM10',
        abbreviation: 'PM10',
        description:
            'Partikel berukuran ≤ 10 mikrometer yang tersuspensi di udara '
            'ruangan. Di dalam ruangan, partikel ini berasal dari debu lantai, '
            'serat kain/sofa, spora jamur, dan partikel dari luar yang masuk '
            'melalui pintu/jendela/ventilasi.',
        unit: 'μg/m³',
        impact:
            'Iritasi mata, hidung, tenggorokan, dan saluran pernapasan bagian '
            'atas; memperburuk alergi debu.',
        source:
            'Debu lantai/meja, serat kain & karpet, spora jamur, partikel '
            'masuk dari ventilasi.',
        safeRange: '0 – 50 μg/m³ (ISPU ≤ 50)',
        icon: '🏗️',
      ),
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'Karbon Monoksida',
        abbreviation: 'CO',
        description:
            'Gas tidak berbau dan tidak berwarna hasil pembakaran tidak sempurna. '
            'Di dalam ruangan, gas ini bisa berasal dari kompor, pemanas, atau masuknya asap kendaraan dari luar.',
        unit: 'µg/m³',
        impact:
            'Sakit kepala, pusing, mual, memperburuk kondisi jantung.',
        source:
            'Kompor gas, asap kendaraan masuk, sistem ventilasi yang buruk.',
        safeRange: '0 – 4.000 µg/m³ (ISPU ≤ 50)',
        icon: '☁️',
      ),
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'Sulfur Dioksida',
        abbreviation: 'SO2',
        description:
            'Gas tidak berwarna dengan bau menyengat yang dihasilkan dari '
            'pembakaran bahan bakar fosil yang mengandung sulfur (batu bara, '
            'minyak). Di dalam ruangan, SO2 dapat berasal dari asap kendaraan '
            'yang masuk melalui ventilasi, kompor gas, atau pembakaran lilin.',
        unit: 'μg/m³',
        impact:
            'Iritasi saluran pernapasan, memperburuk asma dan bronkitis, '
            'iritasi mata dan tenggorokan, penurunan fungsi paru-paru.',
        source:
            'Pembakaran bahan bakar fosil, asap kendaraan, kompor gas, '
            'pembakaran lilin, ventilasi dari luar.',
        safeRange: '0 – 80 μg/m³ (ISPU ≤ 50)',
        icon: '🏭',
      ),
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'Ozon',
        abbreviation: 'O3',
        description:
            'Gas yang terbentuk dari reaksi fotokimia antara nitrogen oksida '
            '(NOx) dan senyawa organik volatil (VOC) dengan bantuan sinar '
            'matahari. Di dalam ruangan, O3 dapat berasal dari peralatan '
            'elektronik seperti printer, mesin fotokopi, dan air purifier '
            'yang menghasilkan ozon, atau masuk dari luar.',
        unit: 'μg/m³',
        impact:
            'Iritasi saluran pernapasan, penurunan fungsi paru-paru, '
            'memperburuk asma, nyeri dada saat bernapas dalam.',
        source:
            'Reaksi fotokimia di luar ruangan, peralatan elektronik '
            '(printer, fotokopi), air purifier tipe ozon, ventilasi dari luar.',
        safeRange: '0 – 120 μg/m³ (ISPU ≤ 50)',
        icon: '⚡',
      ),
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'Nitrogen Dioksida',
        abbreviation: 'NO2',
        description:
            'Gas cokelat kemerahan dengan bau menyengat yang dihasilkan dari '
            'pembakaran pada suhu tinggi (mesin kendaraan, pembangkit listrik). '
            'Di dalam ruangan, NO2 dapat berasal dari kompor gas, pemanas '
            'ruangan, asap rokok, dan asap kendaraan yang masuk melalui '
            'ventilasi.',
        unit: 'μg/m³',
        impact:
            'Iritasi saluran pernapasan, memperburuk asma dan infeksi '
            'pernapasan, penurunan fungsi paru-paru pada paparan jangka panjang.',
        source:
            'Kompor gas, pemanas ruangan berbahan bakar, asap rokok, '
            'asap kendaraan, sistem ventilasi yang buruk.',
        safeRange: '0 – 80 μg/m³ (ISPU ≤ 50)',
        icon: '🚗',
      ),
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'Suhu',
        abbreviation: 'T',
        description:
            'Ukuran panas atau dinginnya udara dalam ruangan. Suhu yang tepat '
            'meningkatkan kenyamanan termal, konsentrasi, dan produktivitas '
            'saat belajar atau bekerja.',
        unit: '°C',
        impact:
            'Ketidaknyamanan, dehidrasi, penurunan konsentrasi, gangguan tidur.',
        source:
            'Cuaca eksternal, peralatan elektronik, jumlah orang dalam ruangan, '
            'AC/kipas/pemanas.',
        safeRange: '24 – 27 °C',
        icon: '🌡️',
      ),
      AQIParameter(
        name: 'Kelembaban',
        abbreviation: 'RH',
        description:
            'Jumlah uap air di udara ruangan. Kelembaban yang tepat penting '
            'untuk kesehatan pernapasan dan mencegah pertumbuhan jamur di '
            'dinding, plafon, atau unit AC.',
        unit: '%',
        impact:
            'Pertumbuhan jamur & tungau, alergi, iritasi pernapasan, '
            'ketidaknyamanan.',
        source:
            'Aktivitas manusia (bernafas, berkeringat), AC, ventilasi, cuaca.',
        safeRange: '40 – 60 %',
        icon: '💧',
      ),
    ];

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _paragraph(
          'Aplikasi memantau 8 parameter lingkungan di dalam ruangan. '
          'Enam parameter polutan (PM2.5, PM10, CO, SO2, O3, dan NO2) '
          'menentukan nilai AQI/ISPU ruangan (diambil nilai MAX dari '
          'keenam parameter), sedangkan dua parameter lainnya (suhu dan '
          'kelembaban) memberi konteks kenyamanan & ventilasi ruangan.',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ...indoorParams.map(_buildParameterCard),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildParameterCard(AQIParameter p) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Text(p.icon, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22)),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      p.name,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                    Text(
                      '${p.abbreviation} • ${p.unit}',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: AppColors.textHint,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Text(
            p.description,
            textAlign: TextAlign.justify,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 13,
              height: 1.5,
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              fontFamily: 'Inter',
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          _infoRow('🏥 Dampak', p.impact),
          _infoRow('📊 Sumber', p.source),
          _infoRow('✅ Aman', p.safeRange),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 3. KATEGORI KUALITAS UDARA
  // ============================================================
  Widget _buildCategories(AQIInfo info) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _paragraph(
          'Kategori kualitas udara menggunakan indikator warna menentukan cara membaca angka AQI. '
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Kategori AQI/ISPU'),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ...info.categories.map(_buildCategoryRow),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        _subheading('Kategori per Parameter (dengan legenda warna)'),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'PM2.5 (μg/m³)',
          rows: const [
            {'range': '0 – 55', 'label': 'Baik', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '56 – 150', 'label': 'Sedang', 'color': AppColors.aqiModerate},
            {'range': '151 – 250', 'label': 'Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthySensitive},
            {'range': '251 – 350', 'label': 'Sangat Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthy},
            {'range': '351 – 500', 'label': 'Berbahaya', 'color': AppColors.aqiHazardous},
          ],
        ),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'PM10 (μg/m³)',
          rows: const [
            {'range': '0 – 50', 'label': 'Baik', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '51 – 150', 'label': 'Sedang', 'color': AppColors.aqiModerate},
            {'range': '151 – 350', 'label': 'Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthySensitive},
            {'range': '351 – 420', 'label': 'Sangat Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthy},
            {'range': '421 – 500', 'label': 'Berbahaya', 'color': AppColors.aqiHazardous},
          ],
        ),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'CO (µg/m³)', 
          rows: const [
            {'range': '0 – 4.000', 'label': 'Baik', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '4.001 – 8.000', 'label': 'Sedang', 'color': AppColors.aqiModerate},
            {'range': '8.001 – 15.000', 'label': 'Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthySensitive},
            {'range': '15.001 – 30.000', 'label': 'Sangat Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthy},
            {'range': '> 30.000', 'label': 'Berbahaya', 'color': AppColors.aqiHazardous},
          ],
        ),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'SO2 (μg/m³)',
          rows: const [
            {'range': '0 – 80', 'label': 'Baik', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '81 – 365', 'label': 'Sedang', 'color': AppColors.aqiModerate},
            {'range': '366 – 800', 'label': 'Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthySensitive},
            {'range': '801 – 1.600', 'label': 'Sangat Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthy},
            {'range': '1.601 – 3.000', 'label': 'Berbahaya', 'color': AppColors.aqiHazardous},
          ],
        ),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'O3 (μg/m³)',
          rows: const [
            {'range': '0 – 120', 'label': 'Baik', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '121 – 235', 'label': 'Sedang', 'color': AppColors.aqiModerate},
            {'range': '236 – 400', 'label': 'Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthySensitive},
            {'range': '401 – 800', 'label': 'Sangat Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthy},
            {'range': '801 – 1.000', 'label': 'Berbahaya', 'color': AppColors.aqiHazardous},
          ],
        ),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'NO2 (μg/m³)',
          rows: const [
            {'range': '0 – 80', 'label': 'Baik', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '81 – 200', 'label': 'Sedang', 'color': AppColors.aqiModerate},
            {'range': '201 – 1.130', 'label': 'Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthySensitive},
            {'range': '1.131 – 2.260', 'label': 'Sangat Tidak Sehat', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthy},
            {'range': '2.261 – 3.000', 'label': 'Berbahaya', 'color': AppColors.aqiHazardous},
          ],
        ),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'Suhu (°C)',
          rows: const [
            {'range': '< 24', 'label': 'Dingin', 'color': Colors.blue},
            {'range': '24 – 27', 'label': 'Ideal', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '28 – 30', 'label': 'Hangat', 'color': AppColors.warning},
            {'range': '> 30', 'label': 'Panas', 'color': AppColors.error},
          ],
        ),
        _parameterCategoryTable(
          title: 'Kelembaban (%)',
          rows: const [
            {'range': '< 40', 'label': 'Kering', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthySensitive},
            {'range': '40 – 60', 'label': 'Ideal', 'color': AppColors.aqiGood},
            {'range': '61 – 70', 'label': 'Lembab', 'color': AppColors.aqiModerate},
            {'range': '> 70', 'label': 'Tidak Nyaman', 'color': AppColors.aqiUnhealthy},
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCategoryRow(AQICategory c) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: c.color.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: c.color.withOpacity(0.4)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 14,
            height: 14,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: c.color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              c.name,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                color: c.color,
                fontFamily: 'Inter',
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            '${c.min} – ${c.max}',
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 13,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              fontFamily: 'Inter',
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _parameterCategoryTable({
    required String title,
    required List<Map<String, dynamic>> rows,
  }) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            title,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 13.5,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              fontFamily: 'Inter',
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ...rows.map((r) {
            final color = r['color'] as Color;
            return Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 12,
                    height: 12,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: color,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      r['range'] as String,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12.5,
                        color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    r['label'] as String,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      color: color,
                      fontFamily: 'Inter',
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 4. PENJELASAN SATUAN
  // ============================================================
  Widget _buildUnits(AQIInfo info) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _paragraph(
          'Setiap parameter menggunakan satuan yang berbeda. Berikut penjelasan '
          'singkat tiap satuan agar Anda bisa membaca data dengan tepat.',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ...info.units.map(
          (u) => Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        u.symbol,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 14,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                          color: AppColors.primary,
                          fontFamily: 'Inter',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        u.name,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 14,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          fontFamily: 'Inter',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  u.description,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 13,
                    height: 1.5,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text(
                  'Contoh: ${u.example}',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                    color: AppColors.textHint,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 5. DAMPAK KESEHATAN & REKOMENDASI
  // ============================================================
  Widget _buildHealth(AQIInfo info) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _paragraph(
          'Semakin tinggi nilai AQI/ISPU, semakin besar risiko kesehatan bagi '
          'seluruh penghuni ruangan, terutama kelompok sensitif (anak-anak, '
          'lansia, ibu hamil, dan penderita gangguan pernapasan).',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Dampak AQI/ISPU pada Ruangan'),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ...info.categories.map(
          (c) => Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: c.color.withOpacity(0.06),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              border: Border.all(color: c.color.withOpacity(0.4)),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 12,
                      height: 12,
                      decoration:
                          BoxDecoration(color: c.color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text(
                      c.name,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 14,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        color: c.color,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Text(
                      '(${c.min}–${c.max})',

                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: AppColors.textHint,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',

                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),

                _infoRow('💡 Saran', _indoorAqiRecommendation(c.min, c.max)),

            ),

        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        _subheading('Rekomendasi per Parameter'),
