  String _indoorAqiRecommendation(int min, int max) {
    if (max <= 50) {
      return 'Aman untuk perkuliahan normal dengan durasi berapa pun. '
          'Tidak perlu tindakan khusus.';
    }
    if (max <= 100) {
      return 'Perkuliahan tatap muka tetap aman. Pastikan ruangan bebas debu '
          'berlebih. Kelompok sensitif sebaiknya tidak duduk dekat jendela.';
    }
    if (max <= 200) {
      return 'Kurangi aktivitas fisik berat di ruangan. Nyalakan air purifier '
          'bila tersedia. Masker disarankan untuk mahasiswa dengan riwayat '
          'pernapasan.';
    }
    if (max <= 300) {
      return 'Wajib memakai masker di dalam ruangan. Nyalakan air purifier '
          'kecepatan maksimum. Batasi durasi tatap muka.';
    }
    return 'Tidak ada aktivitas akademik tatap muka. Alihkan ke daring. '
        'Tim gedung pantau AQI setiap jam.';
  }

  Widget _buildParameterRecommendationCard({
    required String title,
    required List<Map<String, dynamic>> rows,
  }) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            title,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 13.5,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              fontFamily: 'Inter',
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ...rows.map((r) {
            final color = r['color'] as Color;
            return Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color.withOpacity(0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.3)),
              ),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 10,
                    height: 10,
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: color,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          r['range'] as String,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            color: color,
                            fontFamily: 'Inter',
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 2),
                        Text(
                          r['rec'] as String,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            height: 1.4,
                            color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                            fontFamily: 'Inter',
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

          'akan muncul warning berwarna kuning atau merah yang akan menyesuaikan dengan tingkat di luar rentang amannya.'
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),

        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        _miniHeading('Indikator Server'),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        _paragraph(
          'Di pojok kanan bilah status terdapat titik kecil berwarna hijau atau merah. '
          'Hijau berarti server dapat dihubungi, sedangkan merah berarti perangkat tidak memiliki data internet atau server sedang tidak tersedia. '
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 8. FAQ / TROUBLESHOOTING
  // ============================================================
  Widget _buildFAQ() {
    final faqs = <Map<String, String>>[
      {
        'q': 'Kenapa nilai AQI tidak berubah-ubah?',
        'a': 'Dahboard memperbarui data secara otomatis setiap 60 detik atau saat Anda menekan tombol refresh. '
            'Ketika tidak ada perubahan data yang terjadi, bisa jadi karena memang data belum berubah, atau disebabkan karena sumber pengambil data partikel ssedang tidak aktif, '
            'perangkat sedang tidak memiliki akses internet, atau server yang sedang tidak tersedia.'
      },
      {
        'q': 'Kenapa grafik kosong?',
        'a': 'Ada beberapa kemungkinan, (1) tanggal yang dipilih belum memiliki '
            'data historis, (2) server sedang offline, cek indikator titik di '
            'pojok kanan bilah status, (3) cache lokal belum terisi, coba refresh atau '
            'pilih tanggal hari ini.',
      },
      {
        'q': 'Kenapa muncul notifikasi "Server tidak dapat dihubungi"?',
        'a': 'Aplikasi gagal melakukan request ke backend. Periksa koneksi '
            'internet Anda, lalu cek indikator server (titik hijau/merah) di '
            'bilah status. Jika merah, kemungkinan backend sedang mati atau tidak '
            'dapat dijangkau.',
      },
      {
        'q': 'Kenapa data tidak muncul di dashboard meskipun server online?',
        'a': 'Periksa filter gedung dan kotak pencarian. Jika filter aktif dan '
            'tidak ada yang cocok, dashboard akan tampil kosong. Gunakan tombol '
            '"Reset Filter & Pencarian" untuk menampilkan semua ruangan kembali.',
      },
      {
        'q': 'Kenapa badge berubah dari "UPDATE" menjadi "KEMARIN"?',
        'a': 'Artinya data terakhir dari ruangan tersebut bukan dari hari ini. '
            'Silahkan laporkan ke kontak administrator jika terdapat ruangan yang tidak aktif lebih dari 24 jam.',
      },
    ];

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        ...faqs.map(
          (f) => Theme(
            data: Theme.of(context).copyWith(dividerColor: Colors.transparent),
            child: Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: ExpansionTile(
                tilePadding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 2),
                childrenPadding:
                    const EdgeInsets.only(left: 12, right: 12, bottom: 12),
                title: Text(
                  f['q']!,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 13.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
                children: [
                  Align(
                    alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                    child: Text(
                      f['a']!,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 13,
                        height: 1.55,
                        color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAbout() {

  Widget _breakpointTable({
    required List<String> headers,
    required List<List<String>> rows,
    required String unit,
  }) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: headers
                .map(
                  (h) => Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      h,
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 11.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                )
                .toList(),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          ...rows.map(
            (r) => Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
              child: Row(
                children: r
                    .map(
                      (v) => Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          v,
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                            fontFamily: 'Inter',
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                    .toList(),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
            child: Text(
              'Satuan konsentrasi: $unit',
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10.5,
                fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                color: AppColors.textHint,
                fontFamily: 'Inter',
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

   Widget _paragraph(String text) {
    return Text(
      text,
      textAlign: TextAlign.justify,
      style: const TextStyle(
        fontSize: 13.5,
        height: 1.6,
        color: AppColors.textSecondary,
        fontFamily: 'Inter',
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _subheading(String text) {
    return Text(
      text,
      style: const TextStyle(
        fontSize: 14,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
        color: AppColors.textPrimary,
        fontFamily: 'Inter',
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _miniHeading(String text) {
    return Text(
      text,
      style: const TextStyle(
        fontSize: 13.5,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
        color: AppColors.primary,
        fontFamily: 'Inter',
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _codeBox(String code) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
      ),
      child: Text(
        code,
        style: const TextStyle(
          fontSize: 12.5,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          fontFamily: 'monospace',
          color: AppColors.textPrimary,
          height: 1.55,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _infoRow(String label, String value) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 90,
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: AppColors.textHint,
                fontFamily: 'Inter',
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              value,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12.5,
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                height: 1.4,
                fontFamily: 'Inter',
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _bulletList(List<String> items) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: items
          .map((item) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    const Padding(
                      padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 6, right: 8),
                      child:
                          Icon(Icons.circle, size: 5, color: AppColors.textHint),
                    ),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        item,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 13,
                          height: 1.55,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                          fontFamily: 'Inter',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ))
          .toList(),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXPANDABLE SECTION WIDGET
// ============================================================
class _ExpandableSection extends StatefulWidget {
  final String emoji;
  final String title;
  final Color color;
  final Widget child;
  final bool initiallyExpanded;

  const _ExpandableSection({
    required this.emoji,
    required this.title,
    required this.color,
    required this.child,
  }) : initiallyExpanded = false;

  @override
  State<_ExpandableSection> createState() => _ExpandableSectionState();
}

class _ExpandableSectionState extends State<_ExpandableSection>
    with AutomaticKeepAliveClientMixin {
  late bool _expanded;

  @override
  bool get wantKeepAlive => true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _expanded = widget.initiallyExpanded;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    super.build(context);

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(
          color: _expanded ? widget.color.withOpacity(0.35) : AppColors.border,
          width: _expanded ? 1.5 : 1,
        ),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(_expanded ? 0.06 : 0.03),
            blurRadius: _expanded ? 10 : 6,
            offset: const Offset(0, 3),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          InkWell(
            onTap: () => setState(() => _expanded = !_expanded),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 40,
                    height: 40,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: widget.color.withOpacity(0.12),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    ),
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    child: Text(widget.emoji,
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20)),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      widget.title,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 14.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  AnimatedRotation(
                    turns: _expanded ? 0.5 : 0,
                    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
                    child: Icon(
                      Icons.expand_more_rounded,
                      color: widget.color,
                      size: 24,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          if (_expanded)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 0, 14, 16),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Divider(color: widget.color.withOpacity(0.2), height: 1),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  widget.child,
                ],
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/constants/colors.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/socket_service.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/utils/helpers.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/models/room.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/repositories/room_repository.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/presentation/widgets/chart/history_chart.dart';
import '../../widgets/common/server_status_indicator.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/presentation/widgets/chart/monthly_trend_chart.dart';

enum _RecSeverity { normal, warning, danger }

class RoomDetailScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final Room room;

  const RoomDetailScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.room,
  });

  @override
  State<RoomDetailScreen> createState() => _RoomDetailScreenState();
}

class _RoomDetailScreenState extends State<RoomDetailScreen> {
  final SocketService _socketService = SocketService();
  final RoomRepository _roomRepository = RoomRepository();

  final GlobalKey<HistoryChartState> _historyKey =
      GlobalKey<HistoryChartState>();

  String? _lastNotificationMessage;
  DateTime? _lastNotificationTime;

  // Auto-refresh 1 menit
  static const Duration _autoRefreshInterval = Duration(minutes: 1);

  bool _isRefreshing = false;
  bool _isMounted = false;
  late Room _currentRoomData;
  Timer? _autoRefreshTimer;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _isMounted = true;
    _currentRoomData = widget.room;

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      _joinRoomForUpdates();
      _setupRealtimeListener();
      _setupBuildingUpdateListener();
      _setupRoomNameUpdateListener();
      _ensureSocketConnected();
      _startAutoRefreshTimer();
    });
  }

  // FIXED: helper untuk validasi payload socket.
  Map<String, dynamic>? _asMap(dynamic data) {
    if (data == null) return null;
    if (data is Map<String, dynamic>) return data;
    if (data is Map) {
      try {
        return Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // Auto-refresh 60 detik — fetch ulang dari API.
  void _startAutoRefreshTimer() {
    _autoRefreshTimer?.cancel();
    _autoRefreshTimer = Timer.periodic(_autoRefreshInterval, (_) async {
      if (_isRefreshing || !_isMounted) return;
      print('🔄 Room detail auto-refresh (1 minute): fetching fresh data...');
      await _refreshData();
    });
  }

  void _ensureSocketConnected() {
    if (!_socketService.isConnected && _isMounted) {
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        _reconnectSocket();
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _reconnectSocket() async {
    try {
      await _socketService.connect();
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));
      if (_socketService.isConnected) {
        _joinRoomForUpdates();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Failed to reconnect: $e');
    }
  }

  // FIXED: guard data + nested access.
  void _setupRoomNameUpdateListener() {
    _socketService.on('room-name-updated', (data) {
      if (!_isMounted) return;
      final map = _asMap(data);
      if (map == null) return;
      if (map['roomId'] != widget.room.id) return;

      final newName = map['newName']?.toString();
      final oldName = map['oldName']?.toString();
      if (newName == null) return;

      setState(() {
        _currentRoomData = Room(
          id: _currentRoomData.id,
          name: newName,
          buildingId: _currentRoomData.buildingId,
          buildingName: _currentRoomData.buildingName,
          dataSource: _currentRoomData.dataSource,
          iotDeviceId: _currentRoomData.iotDeviceId,
          currentAQI: _currentRoomData.currentAQI,
          currentData: _currentRoomData.currentData,
          createdAt: _currentRoomData.createdAt,
          updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
        );
      });

      _showNotification(
          'Nama ruangan diperbarui: ${oldName ?? '-'} -> $newName', 'info');
    });

    _socketService.on('room-updated', (data) {
      if (!_isMounted) return;
      final map = _asMap(data);
      if (map == null) return;

      final roomData = map['room'];
      if (roomData is! Map) return;
      final roomMap = Map<String, dynamic>.from(roomData);

      if (roomMap['id'] != widget.room.id) return;

      if (map['action'] == 'updated' &&
          map['oldData'] != null &&
          map['oldData'] is Map) {
        final oldMap = Map<String, dynamic>.from(map['oldData'] as Map);
        final newName = roomMap['name'];
        final oldName = oldMap['name'];

        if (newName != oldName) {
          setState(() {
            _currentRoomData = Room(
              id: _currentRoomData.id,
              name: newName?.toString() ?? _currentRoomData.name,
              buildingId: _currentRoomData.buildingId,
              buildingName: _currentRoomData.buildingName,
              dataSource: _currentRoomData.dataSource,
              iotDeviceId: _currentRoomData.iotDeviceId,
              currentAQI: _currentRoomData.currentAQI,
              currentData: _currentRoomData.currentData,
              createdAt: _currentRoomData.createdAt,
              updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
            );
          });

          _showNotification(
              'Nama ruangan diperbarui: $oldName -> $newName', 'info');
        }
      }
    });
  }

  // FIXED: guard data.
  void _setupBuildingUpdateListener() {
    _socketService.on('room-building-updated', (data) {
      if (!_isMounted) return;
      final map = _asMap(data);
      if (map == null) return;
      if (map['buildingId'] != widget.room.buildingId) return;

      final newBuildingName = map['newBuildingName']?.toString();
      if (newBuildingName == null) return;

      setState(() {
        _currentRoomData = Room(
          id: _currentRoomData.id,
          name: _currentRoomData.name,
          buildingId: _currentRoomData.buildingId,
          buildingName: newBuildingName,
          dataSource: _currentRoomData.dataSource,
          iotDeviceId: _currentRoomData.iotDeviceId,
          currentAQI: _currentRoomData.currentAQI,
          currentData: _currentRoomData.currentData,
          createdAt: _currentRoomData.createdAt,
          updatedAt: _currentRoomData.updatedAt,
        );
      });

      _showNotification('Nama gedung diperbarui: $newBuildingName', 'info');
    });
  }

  void _joinRoomForUpdates() {
    _socketService.joinRoom(widget.room.id);
  }

  void _setupRealtimeListener() {
    _socketService.on('notification', (data) {
      if (!_isMounted) return;
      final map = _asMap(data);
      if (map == null) return;

      _showNotification(
        map['message']?.toString() ?? '',
        map['type']?.toString() ?? 'info',
      );
    });
  }

  void _showNotification(String message, String type) {
    if (!_isMounted) return;

    // Throttling: cegah pesan yang sama muncul berulang dalam 10 detik
    final now = DateTime.now();
    if (_lastNotificationMessage == message &&
        _lastNotificationTime != null &&
        now.difference(_lastNotificationTime!).inSeconds < 10) {
      return;
    }
    _lastNotificationMessage = message;
    _lastNotificationTime = now;

    Color backgroundColor;
    IconData icon;

    switch (type) {
      case 'warning':
        backgroundColor = Colors.orange.withOpacity(0.9);
        icon = Icons.warning_rounded;
        break;
      case 'error':
        backgroundColor = Colors.red.withOpacity(0.9);
        icon = Icons.error_rounded;
        break;
      case 'success':
        backgroundColor = Colors.green.withOpacity(0.9);
        icon = Icons.check_circle_rounded;
        break;
      default:
        backgroundColor = Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.9);
        icon = Icons.info_rounded;
    }

    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
    messenger.hideCurrentSnackBar();
    messenger.showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                message,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        backgroundColor: backgroundColor,
        duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Fetch data terbaru dari server — dipakai manual refresh & auto-refresh.
  Future<void> _refreshData() async {
    if (!_isMounted) return;

    setState(() => _isRefreshing = true);

    try {
      final freshRoom = await _roomRepository.getRoomById(widget.room.id);
      if (_isMounted) {
        setState(() {
          _currentRoomData = freshRoom;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (_isMounted) {
        if (Helpers.isNetworkError(e)) {
          Helpers.showServerError(context);
        } else {
          Helpers.showSnackBar(
            context,
            'Gagal refresh data ruangan',
            isError: true,
          );
        }
      }
    } finally {
      if (_isMounted) {
        setState(() => _isRefreshing = false);
      }
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------
  // UI BUILDING METHODS
  // ------------------------------------------------------------

  Widget _buildAQICard() {
    final aqi = _currentRoomData.currentAQI;
    final aqiColor = Helpers.getAQIColor(aqi);
    final aqiLabel = Helpers.getAQILabel(aqi);

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: aqiColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black12,
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'INDEKS KUALITAS UDARA',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 11,
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  Text(
                    'AQI',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 10,
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              Container(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      _currentRoomData.dataSource == 'iot'
                          ? Icons.sensors_rounded
                          : Icons.auto_awesome_mosaic_rounded,
                      size: 12,
                      color: Colors.white,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      _currentRoomData.dataSource == 'iot'
                          ? 'IoT'
                          : 'Simulasi',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        color: Colors.white,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Text(
                aqi?.toString() ?? '—',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 32,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 12, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        aqiLabel.toUpperCase(),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                          color: Colors.white,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildParameterCard(
      String label, String value, String status, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black12,
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 11,
              color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            value,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 22,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              color: Colors.white,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Text(
              status,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: Colors.white,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Baris parameter dinamis — hanya parameter yang tersedia (non-null)
  /// yang ditampilkan. Cell disusun 3-kolom, dengan filler untuk menjaga
  /// alignment tetap konsisten meski jumlahnya bukan kelipatan 3.
  Widget _buildParameterRows() {
    final data = _currentRoomData.currentData;
    final cells = <Widget>[];

    if (data.hasPM25) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'PM2.5',
        '${data.pm25!.toStringAsFixed(1)}\nμg/m³',
        Helpers.getPM25Status(data.pm25),
        Helpers.getPM25Color(data.pm25),
      ));
    }
    if (data.hasPM10) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'PM10',
        '${data.pm10!.toStringAsFixed(1)}\nμg/m³',
        Helpers.getPM10Status(data.pm10),
        Helpers.getPM10Color(data.pm10),
      ));
    }
    if (data.hasCO) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'CO',
        '${data.co!.round()}\nµg/m³',
        Helpers.getCOStatus(data.co),
        Helpers.getCOColor(data.co),
      ));
    }
    if (data.hasSO2) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'SO2',
        '${data.so2!.toStringAsFixed(1)}\nµg/m³',
        Helpers.getSO2Status(data.so2),
        Helpers.getSO2Color(data.so2),
      ));
    }
    if (data.hasO3) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'O3',
        '${data.o3!.toStringAsFixed(1)}\nµg/m³',
        Helpers.getO3Status(data.o3),
        Helpers.getO3Color(data.o3),
      ));
    }
    if (data.hasNO2) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'NO2',
        '${data.no2!.toStringAsFixed(1)}\nµg/m³',
        Helpers.getNO2Status(data.no2),
        Helpers.getNO2Color(data.no2),
      ));
    }
    if (data.hasTemperature) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'SUHU',
        '${data.temperature!.toStringAsFixed(1)}°C',
        Helpers.getTemperatureStatus(data.temperature),
        Helpers.getTemperatureColor(data.temperature),
      ));
    }
    if (data.hasHumidity) {
      cells.add(_buildParameterCard(
        'KELEMBABAN',
        '${data.humidity!.round()}%',
        Helpers.getHumidityStatus(data.humidity),
        Helpers.getHumidityColor(data.humidity),
      ));
    }

    if (cells.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    final rows = <Widget>[];
    for (int i = 0; i < cells.length; i += 3) {
      final rowChildren = <Widget>[];
      for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
        if (j > 0) rowChildren.add(const SizedBox(width: 12));
        if (i + j < cells.length) {
          rowChildren.add(Expanded(child: cells[i + j]));
        } else {
          rowChildren.add(const Expanded(child: SizedBox()));
        }
      }
      rows.add(Row(children: rowChildren));
      if (i + 3 < cells.length) rows.add(const SizedBox(height: 12));
    }
    return Column(children: rows);
  }

  Widget _buildHealthRecommendations() {
    final recommendations =
        Helpers.getDetailedRecommendations(_currentRoomData);

    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, 3),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Row(
            children: [
              Icon(
                Icons.health_and_safety_rounded,
                color: Colors.blue,
                size: 18,
              ),
              SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text(
                'REKOMENDASI KESEHATAN',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ...recommendations.map(_buildRecommendationItem),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRecommendationItem(String text) {
    _RecSeverity _recommendationSeverity(String text) {
      final data = _currentRoomData.currentData;
      final aqi = _currentRoomData.currentAQI;

      // --- NO DATA ---
      if (text.startsWith('BELUM ADA DATA') ||
          text.startsWith('Perangkat belum mengirimkan') ||
          text.startsWith('Jika kondisi ini berlangsung lama')) {
        return _RecSeverity.warning;
      }

      // --- SENSOR ERROR ---
      if (text.startsWith('SENSOR RUANGAN MELAPORKAN ERROR') ||
          text.startsWith('Nilai AQI yang ditampilkan') ||
          text.startsWith('Hubungi admin')) {
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- AQI ---
      if (text.startsWith('AQI:')) {
        if (aqi == null) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        if (aqi < 0) return _RecSeverity.danger;
        if (aqi <= 50) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (aqi <= 100) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- PM2.5 ---
      if (text.startsWith('PM2.5:')) {
        final v = data.pm25;
        if (v == null || v < 0) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 55) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 150) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- PM10 ---
      if (text.startsWith('PM10:')) {
        final v = data.pm10;
        if (v == null || v < 0) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 50) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 150) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- CO ---
      if (text.startsWith('CO:')) {
        final v = data.co;
        if (v == null || v < 0) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 4000) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 8000) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- SO2 ---
      if (text.startsWith('SO2:')) {
        final v = data.so2;
        if (v == null || v < 0) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 80) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 365) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- O3 ---
      if (text.startsWith('O3:')) {
        final v = data.o3;
        if (v == null || v < 0) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 120) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 235) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- NO2 ---
      if (text.startsWith('NO2:')) {
        final v = data.no2;
        if (v == null || v < 0) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 80) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (v <= 200) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- Suhu ---
      if (text.startsWith('Suhu:')) {
        final t = data.temperature;
        if (t == null) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (t < 24) return _RecSeverity.danger;
        if (t <= 27) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (t <= 30) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      // --- Kelembaban ---
      if (text.startsWith('Kelembaban:')) {
        final h = data.humidity;
        if (h == null) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (h < 40) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        if (h <= 60) return _RecSeverity.normal;
        if (h <= 70) return _RecSeverity.warning;
        return _RecSeverity.danger;
      }

      return _RecSeverity.normal;
    }

    final severity = _recommendationSeverity(text);

    final IconData icon;
    final Color iconColor;
    switch (severity) {
      case _RecSeverity.warning:
        icon = Icons.warning_amber_rounded;
        iconColor = Colors.amber.shade700;
        break;
      case _RecSeverity.danger:
        icon = Icons.warning_amber_rounded;
        iconColor = Colors.red;
        break;
      case _RecSeverity.normal:
        icon = Icons.arrow_right;
        iconColor = Colors.grey;
        break;
    }

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 16, color: iconColor),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              text,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontFamily: 'Inter',
                fontSize: 15,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                color: Colors.black,
                height: 1.4,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRoomInfoHeader() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          colors: [Colors.white, Colors.grey.shade50],
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade200, width: 1),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.03),
            blurRadius: 12,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
            spreadRadius: -2,
          ),
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 52,
            height: 52,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                colors: [Colors.blue.shade400, Colors.blue.shade600],
              ),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.3),
                  blurRadius: 8,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: const Icon(
              Icons.meeting_room_rounded,
              size: 28,

    _autoRefreshTimer?.cancel();

    _socketService.leaveRoom(widget.room.id);

    _socketService.off('notification');
    _socketService.off('room-building-updated');
    _socketService.off('room-name-updated');
    _socketService.off('room-updated');

    super.dispose();
  }

lib/presentation/providers/auth_provider.dart:

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/services/auth_service.dart';

class AuthProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  final AuthService _authService = AuthService();
  
  Map<String, dynamic>? _currentUser;
  bool _isLoggedIn = false;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  
  Map<String, dynamic>? get currentUser => _currentUser;
  bool get isLoggedIn => _isLoggedIn;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  bool get isAdmin => _currentUser?['role'] == 'admin';
  
  // Initialize auth state
  Future<void> initialize() async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      _isLoggedIn = await _authService.isLoggedIn();
      if (_isLoggedIn) {
        _currentUser = await _authService.getCurrentUser();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _isLoggedIn = false;
      _currentUser = null;
    } finally {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
  
  // Login
  Future<void> login(String username, String password, String role) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      final response = await _authService.loginAdmin(username, password);
      
      if (response['success'] == true) {
        _currentUser = response['user'];
        _isLoggedIn = true;
      } else {
        throw Exception(response['message'] ?? 'Login failed');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      rethrow;
    } finally {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
  
  // Logout
  // FIXED: sebelumnya `rethrow` menyebabkan state lokal tidak ter-update
  // saat server error. Sekarang: error hanya di-log, state lokal direset
  // di `finally` agar user tetap bisa keluar dari aplikasi.
  Future<void> logout() async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      await _authService.logout();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('❌ Logout error (server): $e');
    } finally {
      _currentUser = null;
      _isLoggedIn = false;
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
  
  // Change password
  Future<void> changePassword(String oldPassword, String newPassword) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      await _authService.changePassword(oldPassword, newPassword);
    } catch (e) {
      rethrow;
    } finally {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../data/models/building.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';

class BuildingProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();
  
  List<Building> _buildings = [];
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _hasError = false;
  String _errorMessage = '';
  
  // Getters
  List<Building> get buildings => _buildings;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  bool get hasError => _hasError;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;
  
  // Load buildings
  Future<void> loadBuildings() async {
  _isLoading = true;
  _hasError = false;
  notifyListeners();

  try {
    final response = await _apiService.getTestBuildings();
    
    if (response['success'] == true) {
      final List<dynamic> data = response['data'];
      _buildings = data.map((json) => Building.fromJson(json)).toList();
    } else {
      throw Exception('Failed to load buildings: ${response['message']}');
    }
  } catch (e) {
    _hasError = true;
    _errorMessage = e.toString();
    print('Error loading buildings: $e');
  } finally {
    _isLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }
}
  
  // Get building by ID
  Building? getBuildingById(String buildingId) {
    try {
      return _buildings.firstWhere((building) => building.id == buildingId);
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }
  
  // Add building (will be implemented in Phase 2)
  Future<void> addBuilding({
    required String name,
    String? code,
    String? description,
  }) async {
    // TODO: Implement in Phase 2
    notifyListeners();
  }
  
  // Update building (will be implemented in Phase 2)
  Future<void> updateBuilding({
    required String buildingId,
    required String name,
    String? code,
    String? description,
  }) async {
    // TODO: Implement in Phase 2
    notifyListeners();
  }
  
  // Delete building (will be implemented in Phase 2)
  Future<void> deleteBuilding(String buildingId) async {
    // TODO: Implement in Phase 2
    notifyListeners();
  }
  
  // Clear error
  void clearError() {
    _hasError = false;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

lib/presentation/providers/room_provider.dart:

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/repositories/room_repository.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/socket_service.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/storage_service.dart';
import '../../data/models/room.dart';

class RoomProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  final RoomRepository _roomRepository = RoomRepository();
  final SocketService _socketService = SocketService();
  final StorageService _storageService = StorageService();

  static const String _cacheKey = 'cache_dashboard_rooms';

  // FIXED: Auto-refresh UI tiap 1 menit.
  // Backend tetap update DB tiap 10 detik — frontend hanya fetch ulang
  // dari API setiap 60 detik, bukan live socket.
  static const Duration _autoRefreshInterval = Duration(minutes: 1);

  List<Room> _rooms = [];
  List<Room> _filteredRooms = [];
  String _selectedBuilding = 'Semua Gedung';
  String _sortBy = 'Terbaru';
  String _searchQuery = '';

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _hasError = false;
  String _errorMessage = '';
  bool _socketConnected = false;
  bool _isRefreshing = false;

  DateTime _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();

  StreamSubscription? _socketSubscription;
  // FIXED: timer baru untuk auto-refresh 60 detik.
  Timer? _autoRefreshTimer;

  List<Room> get rooms => _filteredRooms;
  List<Room> get allRooms => _rooms;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  bool get isRefreshing => _isRefreshing;
  bool get hasError => _hasError;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;
  String get selectedBuilding => _selectedBuilding;
  String get sortBy => _sortBy;
  String get searchQuery => _searchQuery;
  bool get socketConnected => _socketConnected;
  DateTime get lastUpdate => _lastUpdate;

  List<String> get buildings {
    final buildingSet = <String>{'Semua Gedung'};
    for (final room in _rooms) {
      if (room.buildingName.isNotEmpty) {
        buildingSet.add(room.buildingName);
      }
    }
    return buildingSet.toList();
  }

  Future<void> initSocket() async {
    try {
      final token = await _storageService.getString('auth_token');
      await _socketService.connect(token: token);

      _socketSubscription = _socketService.connectionStream.listen((connected) {
        _socketConnected = connected;
        notifyListeners();
      });

      // FIXED: Tidak subscribe ke dataStream lagi.
      // Data update lewat _autoRefreshTimer (60 detik) atau manual refresh.
      // Socket hanya untuk event struktural (room added/deleted/renamed).

      _setupSocketListeners();
      _startAutoRefreshTimer();

      _socketConnected = _socketService.isConnected;
      await _storageService.setSocketConnected(_socketConnected);

      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error initializing socket: $e');
      _socketConnected = false;
    }
  }

  /// FIXED: auto-refresh baru — 1 menit sekali, fetch ulang dari API.
  void _startAutoRefreshTimer() {
    _autoRefreshTimer?.cancel();
    _autoRefreshTimer = Timer.periodic(_autoRefreshInterval, (_) async {
      if (_isRefreshing) return;
      print('🔄 RoomProvider auto-refresh (1 minute): fetching fresh data...');
      await _refreshData();
    });
  }

  // FIXED: guard tipe data agar payload null/rusak tidak memicu TypeError.
  void _handleRoomDeleted(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final roomId = map['roomId'];
      final roomName = map['roomName'];

      print('🗑️ Processing room deletion: $roomName ($roomId)');

      final initialLength = _rooms.length;
      _rooms.removeWhere((room) => room.id == roomId);

      if (_rooms.length < initialLength) {
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        _applyFilters();
        notifyListeners();
        print('✅ Room removed from dashboard: $roomName');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling room deletion: $e');
    }
  }

  // FIXED: guard tipe data agar payload null/rusak tidak memicu TypeError.
  void _handleDashboardRoomUpdated(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final action = map['action'];
      final roomData = map['room'];

      if (action != 'created' || roomData == null || roomData is! Map) return;

      final roomMap = Map<String, dynamic>.from(roomData);
      final roomId = roomMap['id']?.toString();
      if (roomId == null) return;

      if (_rooms.any((r) => r.id == roomId)) {
        print('ℹ️ Room already exists in list, skipping: $roomId');
        return;
      }

      final newRoom = Room(
        id: roomId,
        name: roomMap['name']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown',
        buildingId: roomMap['buildingId']?.toString() ?? '',
        buildingName: roomMap['buildingName']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown',
        dataSource: roomMap['dataSource']?.toString() ?? 'simulation',
        iotDeviceId: roomMap['iotDeviceId']?.toString(),
        currentAQI: (roomMap['currentAQI'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
        currentData: RoomData.fromJson(
          roomMap['currentData'] is Map
              ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(roomMap['currentData'])
              : <String, dynamic>{},
        ),
        createdAt: roomMap['createdAt'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(roomMap['createdAt'].toString())
            : DateTime.now(),
        updatedAt: roomMap['updatedAt'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(roomMap['updatedAt'].toString())
            : DateTime.now(),
      );

      _rooms.add(newRoom);
      _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
      _applyFilters();
      notifyListeners();

      print('✅ Room added to dashboard: ${newRoom.name} (${newRoom.buildingName})');
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling dashboard-room-updated: $e');
    }
  }

  void _setupSocketListeners() {
    // FIXED: hanya event STRUKTURAL yang di-listen.
    // 'room-update' & 'dashboard-update' (data AQI tiap 10s) TIDAK di-listen
    // lagi — data update via _autoRefreshTimer (60s) atau manual refresh.
    _socketService.on('dashboard-room-updated', _handleDashboardRoomUpdated);
    _socketService.on('room-name-changed', (data) {
      handleRoomNameChanged(data);
    });
    _socketService.on('room-building-changed', (data) {
      handleBuildingNameChanged(data);
    });
    _socketService.on('room-deleted', _handleRoomDeleted);
  }

  void _validateSelectedBuilding() {
    final availableBuildings = buildings;
    if (!availableBuildings.contains(_selectedBuilding)) {
      _selectedBuilding = 'Semua Gedung';
    }
  }

  void joinRoom(String roomId) {
    _socketService.joinRoom(roomId);
    _storageService.addSubscribedRoom(roomId);
  }

  void leaveRoom(String roomId) {
    _socketService.leaveRoom(roomId);
    _storageService.removeSubscribedRoom(roomId);
  }

  Future<void> checkSocketConnection() async {
    final isConnected = await _socketService.checkConnection();
    _socketConnected = isConnected;
    await _storageService.setSocketConnected(isConnected);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> reconnectSocket() async {
    await initSocket();
  }

  Future<void> loadFromCache() async {
    try {
      final cached = _storageService.getJson(_cacheKey);
      if (cached != null && cached['data'] is List) {
        final list = (cached['data'] as List)
            .map((e) => Room.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e as Map)))
            .toList();
        if (list.isNotEmpty && _rooms.isEmpty) {
          _rooms = list;
          _applyFilters();
          _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
          notifyListeners();
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // cache rusak — abaikan, biarkan loadRooms yang isi
    }
  }

  Future<void> loadRooms() async {
    // Hanya tampilkan spinner jika belum ada data (cache kosong).
    _isLoading = _rooms.isEmpty;
    _hasError = false;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      if (!_socketConnected) {
        await initSocket();
      }

      _rooms = await _roomRepository.getRooms();
      _applyFilters();
      _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();

      // Simpan ke cache untuk cold-start berikutnya
      try {
        await _storageService.setJson(_cacheKey, {
          'data': _rooms.map((r) => r.toJson()).toList(),
        });
      } catch (_) {}
    } catch (e) {
      _hasError = true;
      _errorMessage = e.toString();
      print('❌ Error loading rooms: $e');
    } finally {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Manual refresh — dipakai tombol refresh di Dashboard.
  /// Menampilkan spinner (`_isRefreshing = true`).
  Future<void> refresh() async {
    if (_isRefreshing) return;

    _isRefreshing = true;
    _hasError = false;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      _rooms = await _roomRepository.getRooms();
      _applyFilters();
      _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();

      try {
        await _storageService.setJson(_cacheKey, {
          'data': _rooms.map((r) => r.toJson()).toList(),
        });
      } catch (_) {}
    } catch (e) {
      _hasError = true;
      _errorMessage = e.toString();
    } finally {
      _isRefreshing = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// FIXED: silent background refresh — dipanggil oleh _autoRefreshTimer.
  /// Tidak menampilkan spinner, hanya notify kalau ada perubahan.
  Future<void> _refreshData() async {
    try {
      final newRooms = await _roomRepository.getRooms();

      bool hasChanges = false;
      if (newRooms.length != _rooms.length) {
        hasChanges = true;
      } else {
        for (int i = 0; i < newRooms.length; i++) {
          if (newRooms[i].currentAQI != _rooms[i].currentAQI ||
              newRooms[i].updatedAt != _rooms[i].updatedAt) {
            hasChanges = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if (hasChanges) {
        _rooms = newRooms;
        _applyFilters();
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        notifyListeners();

        // Cache diperbarui supaya cold-start berikutnya tampil data terbaru
        try {
          await _storageService.setJson(_cacheKey, {
            'data': _rooms.map((r) => r.toJson()).toList(),
          });
        } catch (_) {}

        print('✅ RoomProvider auto-refresh updated ${_rooms.length} rooms');
      } else {
        print('ℹ️ RoomProvider auto-refresh: no changes');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error during background refresh: $e');
    }
  }

  void _applyFilters() {
    _validateSelectedBuilding();

    List<Room> result = List.from(_rooms);

    if (_selectedBuilding != 'Semua Gedung') {
      result = result.where((room) => room.buildingName == _selectedBuilding).toList();
    }

    if (_searchQuery.isNotEmpty) {
      final query = _searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      result = result.where((room) =>
        room.name.toLowerCase().contains(query) ||
        room.buildingName.toLowerCase().contains(query)
      ).toList();
    }

    switch (_sortBy) {
      case 'Terbaru':
        result.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt.compareTo(a.updatedAt));
        break;
      case 'A-Z':
        result.sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name));
        break;
      case 'AQI Terbaik':
        result.sort((a, b) {
          final aV = a.currentAQI;
          final bV = b.currentAQI;
          if (aV == null && bV == null) return 0;
          if (aV == null) return 1; // null ditaruh di akhir
          if (bV == null) return -1;
          return aV.compareTo(bV);
        });
        break;
      case 'AQI Terburuk':
        result.sort((a, b) {
          final aV = a.currentAQI;
          final bV = b.currentAQI;
          if (aV == null && bV == null) return 0;
          if (aV == null) return 1;
          if (bV == null) return -1;
          return bV.compareTo(aV);
        });
        break;
    }

    _filteredRooms = result;
  }

  void updateBuildingFilter(String building) {
    _selectedBuilding = building;
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void updateSort(String sortBy) {
    _sortBy = sortBy;
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void updateSearchQuery(String query) {
    _searchQuery = query;
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Map<String, List<Room>> get roomsByBuilding {
    final Map<String, List<Room>> result = {};

    for (final room in _filteredRooms) {
      final buildingName = room.buildingName;
      if (!result.containsKey(buildingName)) {
        result[buildingName] = [];
      }
      result[buildingName]!.add(room);
    }

    return result;
  }

  void clearFilters() {
    _selectedBuilding = 'Semua Gedung';
    _sortBy = 'Terbaru';
    _searchQuery = '';
    _applyFilters();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Room? getRoomById(String roomId) {
    try {
      return _rooms.firstWhere((room) => room.id == roomId);
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // FIXED: signature diubah ke dynamic + guard tipe.
  void handleBuildingNameChanged(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final buildingId = map['buildingId'];
      final newBuildingName = map['newBuildingName'];

      print('🔄 Processing building name change: $buildingId -> $newBuildingName');

      bool hasChanges = false;
      for (int i = 0; i < _rooms.length; i++) {
        if (_rooms[i].buildingId == buildingId) {
          final updatedRoom = Room(
            id: _rooms[i].id,
            name: _rooms[i].name,
            buildingId: _rooms[i].buildingId,
            buildingName: newBuildingName ?? _rooms[i].buildingName,
            dataSource: _rooms[i].dataSource,
            iotDeviceId: _rooms[i].iotDeviceId,
            currentAQI: _rooms[i].currentAQI,
            currentData: _rooms[i].currentData,
            createdAt: _rooms[i].createdAt,
            updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
          );

          _rooms[i] = updatedRoom;
          hasChanges = true;

          print('✅ Updated room ${_rooms[i].name}: building name -> $newBuildingName');
        }
      }

      if (hasChanges) {
        _applyFilters();
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        notifyListeners();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling building name change: $e');
    }
  }

  // FIXED: signature diubah ke dynamic + guard tipe.
  void handleRoomNameChanged(dynamic data) {
    if (data is! Map) return;
    try {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      final roomId = map['roomId'];
      final newName = map['newName'];
      final oldName = map['oldName'];
      final buildingName = map['buildingName'];

      print('🔄 Processing room name change: $oldName -> $newName ($roomId)');

      final roomIndex = _rooms.indexWhere((room) => room.id == roomId);

      if (roomIndex != -1) {
        final updatedRoom = Room(
          id: _rooms[roomIndex].id,
          name: newName ?? _rooms[roomIndex].name,
          buildingId: _rooms[roomIndex].buildingId,
          buildingName: buildingName ?? _rooms[roomIndex].buildingName,
          dataSource: _rooms[roomIndex].dataSource,
          iotDeviceId: _rooms[roomIndex].iotDeviceId,
          currentAQI: _rooms[roomIndex].currentAQI,
          currentData: _rooms[roomIndex].currentData,
          createdAt: _rooms[roomIndex].createdAt,
          updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
        );

        _rooms[roomIndex] = updatedRoom;
        _applyFilters();
        _lastUpdate = DateTime.now();
        notifyListeners();

        print('✅ Room name updated: $oldName -> $newName');
      } else {
        print('⚠️ Room not found in local list: $roomId');
        _refreshData();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print('❌ Error handling room name change: $e');
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _autoRefreshTimer?.cancel();
    _socketSubscription?.cancel();
    _socketService.disconnect();
    _socketService.off('dashboard-room-updated');
    _socketService.off('room-deleted');
    _socketService.off('room-name-changed');
    _socketService.off('room-building-changed');
    super.dispose();
  }
}
