lib/presentation/widgets/chart/history_chart.dart:

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/constants/colors.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/constants/parameter_colors.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/api_service.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/utils/helpers.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/models/sensor_data.dart';

class HistoryChart extends StatefulWidget {
  final String roomId;
  final String roomName;
  final String buildingName;

  const HistoryChart({
    super.key,
    required this.roomId,
    required this.roomName,
    required this.buildingName,
  });

  @override
  State<HistoryChart> createState() => HistoryChartState();
}

class HistoryChartState extends State<HistoryChart> {
  List<SensorData> _historyData = [];
  DateTime _selectedDate = DateTime.now();
  String _selectedParameter = 'aqi';
  bool _isLoading = false;
  String _error = '';
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  static const int _pointsInView = 8;
  static const double _containerPadding = 16.0;

  static const List<String> _allParameters = [
    'aqi', 'pm25', 'pm10', 'co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2', 'temperature', 'humidity',
  ];

  final Map<String, String> _parameterLabels = {
    'aqi': 'AQI',
    'pm25': 'PM2.5',
    'pm10': 'PM10',
    'co': 'CO',
    'so2': 'SO2',
    'o3': 'O3',
    'no2': 'NO2',
    'temperature': 'Suhu',
    'humidity': 'Kelembaban',
  };

  static const List<String> _dayNames = [
    'Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu', 'Minggu',
  ];
  static const List<String> _monthNames = [
    'Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
    'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember',
  ];

  String _formatSelectedDate(DateTime d) {
    final dayName = _dayNames[d.weekday - 1];
    final monthName = _monthNames[d.month - 1];
    return '$dayName, ${d.day} $monthName ${d.year}';
  }

  Future<void> refresh() async {
    await _loadHistoryData();
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadHistoryData();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300), () {
        if (_scrollController.hasClients) {
          _scrollController.animateTo(
            _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent,
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
            curve: Curves.easeOut,
          );
        }
      });
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  double? _valueFor(SensorData data, String parameter) {
    switch (parameter) {
      case 'aqi':         return data.aqi?.toDouble();
      case 'pm25':        return data.pm25;
      case 'pm10':        return data.pm10;
      case 'co':          return data.co;
      case 'so2':         return data.so2;
      case 'o3':          return data.o3;
      case 'no2':         return data.no2;
      case 'temperature': return data.temperature;
      case 'humidity':    return data.humidity;
      default:            return null;
    }
  }

  List<String> get _availableParameters {
    return _allParameters.where((p) {
      return _historyData.any((d) => _valueFor(d, p) != null);
    }).toList();
  }

  Future<void> _loadHistoryData() async {
    if (!mounted) return;

    final hadData = _historyData.isNotEmpty;

    setState(() {
      _isLoading = !hadData;
      _error = '';
    });

    bool failed = false;
    bool isNetworkFailure = false;

    try {
      final dateStr =
          '${_selectedDate.year}-${_selectedDate.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${_selectedDate.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

      final response =
          await ApiService().getSensorHistory(widget.roomId, dateStr);

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (response is Map && response['success'] == true) {
        final List<dynamic> data = response['data'] ?? [];
        final parsed = data
            .map((json) => SensorData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(json)))
            .toList();

        setState(() {
          _historyData = parsed;
          if (!_availableParameters.contains(_selectedParameter)) {
            _selectedParameter =
                _availableParameters.isNotEmpty ? _availableParameters.first : 'aqi';
          }
        });
      } else {
        failed = true;
        final errStr =
            (response is Map) ? (response['error']?.toString() ?? '') : '';
        isNetworkFailure = Helpers.isNetworkError(errStr);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      failed = true;
      isNetworkFailure = Helpers.isNetworkError(e);
    }

    if (!mounted) return;

    if (failed) {
      if (!hadData) {
        setState(() {
          _error = isNetworkFailure
              ? 'Tidak dapat memuat data grafik karena server tidak dapat dihubungi.'
              : 'Gagal memuat data grafik.';
        });
      }

      if (isNetworkFailure) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
      } else {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(context, 'Gagal memuat data grafik', isError: true);
      }
    }

    if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
  }

  Future<void> _selectDate(BuildContext context) async {
    try {
      final DateTime? picked = await showDatePicker(
        context: context,
        initialDate: _selectedDate,
        firstDate: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 30)),
        lastDate: DateTime.now(),
      );
      if (picked != null && picked != _selectedDate) {
        setState(() => _selectedDate = picked);
        await _loadHistoryData();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('❌ Date picker error: $e');
      if (mounted) {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(context, 'Gagal membuka pemilih tanggal',
            isError: true);
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _changeDay(int delta) async {
    final today = DateTime.now();
    final todayStart = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
    final minDate = todayStart.subtract(const Duration(days: 30));

    final candidate = DateTime(
      _selectedDate.year,
      _selectedDate.month,
      _selectedDate.day + delta,
    );

    if (candidate.isAfter(todayStart)) return;
    if (candidate.isBefore(minDate)) return;

    setState(() => _selectedDate = candidate);
    await _loadHistoryData();
  }

  List<FlSpot> _getDataPoints() {
    final spots = <FlSpot>[];
    for (int i = 0; i < _historyData.length; i++) {
      final y = _valueFor(_historyData[i], _selectedParameter);
      if (y == null) continue;
      spots.add(FlSpot(i.toDouble(), y));
    }
    return spots;
  }

  SideTitles _getBottomTitles() {
    return SideTitles(
      showTitles: true,
      reservedSize: 30,
      interval: 1,
      getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
        final index = value.toInt();
        if (value != index.toDouble()) return const SizedBox.shrink();
        if (index < 0 || index >= _historyData.length) {
          return const SizedBox.shrink();
        }
        final data = _historyData[index];
        if (data.timestamp.minute != 0) return const SizedBox.shrink();

        final hourLabel = data.timestamp.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8.0),
          child: Text(
            '$hourLabel:00',
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: Colors.grey),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildChart() {
    final spots = _getDataPoints();
    final color = ParameterColors.of(_selectedParameter);

    final screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(context).size.width;
    final availableViewport = screenWidth - (_containerPadding * 2);
    final pointSpacing = availableViewport / _pointsInView;
    final calculatedWidth = _historyData.length * pointSpacing;
    final actualChartWidth = calculatedWidth > availableViewport
        ? calculatedWidth
        : availableViewport;

    return Container(
      height: 350,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(_containerPadding),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.04),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.25)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Flexible(
                      child: Text(
                        'Grafik ${_parameterLabels[_selectedParameter]}',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            width: 12,
                            height: 12,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 4),
                          Text(
                            _parameterLabels[_selectedParameter]!,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 12,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                              color: color,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              controller: _scrollController,
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              child: Container(
                width: actualChartWidth,
                child: LineChart(
                  LineChartData(
                    gridData: FlGridData(
                      show: true,
                      drawVerticalLine: true,
                      drawHorizontalLine: false,
                      horizontalInterval: 20,
                      getDrawingHorizontalLine: (value) => const FlLine(
                        color: Colors.transparent,
                        strokeWidth: 0,
                      ),
                      getDrawingVerticalLine: (value) {
                        if (value % 2 == 0) {
                          return FlLine(
                            color: Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.1),
                            strokeWidth: 0.5,
                          );
                        }
                        return const FlLine(
                            color: Colors.transparent, strokeWidth: 0);
                      },
                    ),
                    titlesData: FlTitlesData(
                      show: true,
                      rightTitles: const AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                      ),
                      topTitles: const AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                      ),
                      bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: _getBottomTitles(),
                      ),
                      leftTitles: const AxisTitles(
                        sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                      ),
                    ),
                    borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
                    minX: 0,
                    maxX: _historyData.length > 0
                        ? (_historyData.length - 1).toDouble()
                        : 0,
                    minY: _getMinY(),
                    maxY: _getMaxY(),
                    lineBarsData: [
                      LineChartBarData(
                        spots: spots,
                        isCurved: true,
                        color: color,
                        barWidth: 2.5,
                        isStrokeCapRound: true,
                        dotData: FlDotData(
                          show: true,
                          getDotPainter: (spot, percent, barData, index) {
                            return FlDotCirclePainter(
                              radius: 3.5,
                              color: color,
                              strokeWidth: 2,
                              strokeColor: Colors.white,
                            );
                          },
                        ),
                        belowBarData: BarAreaData(
                          show: true,
                          gradient: LinearGradient(
                            begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                            end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                            colors: [
                              color.withOpacity(0.3),
                              color.withOpacity(0.1),
                              Colors.transparent,
                            ],
                            stops: const [0.0, 0.5, 1.0],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                    lineTouchData: LineTouchData(
                      enabled: true,
                      touchTooltipData: LineTouchTooltipData(
                        tooltipBgColor: Colors.white,
                        tooltipRoundedRadius: 8,
                        tooltipPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        fitInsideHorizontally: true,
                        fitInsideVertically: true,
                        maxContentWidth:
                            MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.7,
                        getTooltipItems: (List<LineBarSpot> touchedSpots) {
                          return touchedSpots.map((spot) {
                            final index = spot.spotIndex;
                            if (index < 0 || index >= _historyData.length) {
                              return null;
                            }
                            final data = _historyData[index];
                            final time =
                                '${data.timestamp.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${data.timestamp.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

                            final values = <String, String>{
                              if (data.aqi != null) 'AQI': '${data.aqi}',
                              if (data.pm25 != null)
                                'PM2.5': '${data.pm25!.toStringAsFixed(1)} μg/m³',
                              if (data.pm10 != null)
                                'PM10': '${data.pm10!.toStringAsFixed(1)} μg/m³',
                              if (data.co != null)
                                'CO': '${data.co!.round()} μg/m³',
                              if (data.so2 != null)
                                'SO2': '${data.so2!.toStringAsFixed(1)} μg/m³',
                              if (data.o3 != null)
                                'O3': '${data.o3!.toStringAsFixed(1)} μg/m³',
                              if (data.no2 != null)
                                'NO2': '${data.no2!.toStringAsFixed(1)} μg/m³',
                              if (data.temperature != null)
                                'Suhu': '${data.temperature!.toStringAsFixed(1)}°C',
                              if (data.humidity != null)
                                'Kelembaban': '${data.humidity!.round()}%',
                            };

                            return LineTooltipItem(
                              'Waktu: $time\n\n${values.entries.map((e) => '${e.key}: ${e.value}').join('\n')}',
                              const TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.black87,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                fontSize: 12,
                              ),
                            );
                          })
                              .where((e) => e != null)
                              .cast<LineTooltipItem>()
                              .toList();
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  double _getMinY() {
    if (_historyData.isEmpty) return 0.0;
    double minValue = double.infinity;
    for (var data in _historyData) {
      final value = _valueFor(data, _selectedParameter);
      if (value == null) continue;
      if (value < minValue) minValue = value;
    }
    if (minValue == double.infinity) return 0.0;
    return minValue - (minValue * 0.1);
  }

  double _getMaxY() {
    if (_historyData.isEmpty) return 100.0;
    double maxValue = double.negativeInfinity;
    for (var data in _historyData) {
      final value = _valueFor(data, _selectedParameter);
      if (value == null) continue;
      if (value > maxValue) maxValue = value;
    }
    if (maxValue == double.negativeInfinity) return 100.0;
    return maxValue + (maxValue * 0.1);
  }

  Widget _buildParameterSelector() {
    final available = _availableParameters;
    if (available.isEmpty) return const SizedBox(height: 40);

    return SizedBox(
      height: 40,
      child: ListView.builder(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: available.length,
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final parameter = available[index];
          final isSelected = _selectedParameter == parameter;
          final color = ParameterColors.of(parameter);

          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () => setState(() => _selectedParameter = parameter),
            child: Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6),
              padding:
                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                // ✅ Selected = warna parameter itu sendiri
                color: isSelected
                    ? color
                    : AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 8,
                    height: 8,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  Center(
                    child: Text(
                      _parameterLabels[parameter]!,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDateSelector() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_left),
            onPressed: () => _changeDay(-1),
            tooltip: 'Hari sebelumnya',
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: InkWell(
              onTap: () => _selectDate(context),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      _formatSelectedDate(_selectedDate),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
            onPressed: () => _changeDay(1),
            tooltip: 'Hari berikutnya',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _buildDateSelector(),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        _buildParameterSelector(),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        if (_isLoading)
          Container(
            height: 350,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: const CircularProgressIndicator(),
          )
        else if (_error.isNotEmpty && _historyData.isEmpty)
          Container(
            height: 350,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 48, color: Colors.red),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  _error,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: _loadHistoryData,
                  child: const Text('Coba Lagi'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        else if (_historyData.isEmpty || _availableParameters.isEmpty)
          Container(
            height: 350,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: const Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.bar_chart_outlined,
                size: 48, color: AppColors.textHint),
                SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(
                  'Tidak ada data history untuk tanggal ini',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textHint,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        else
          _buildChart(),
      ],
    );
  }
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
unila_aqi/lib/presentation/widgets/chart/monthly_trend_chart.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/constants/colors.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/constants/parameter_colors.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/api_service.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/utils/helpers.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/models/monthly_trend.dart';

class MonthlyTrendChart extends StatefulWidget {
  final String roomId;

  const MonthlyTrendChart({super.key, required this.roomId});

  @override
  State<MonthlyTrendChart> createState() => _MonthlyTrendChartState();
}

class _MonthlyTrendChartState extends State<MonthlyTrendChart> {
  late int _year;
  late int _month;
  String _parameter = 'aqi';

  bool _isLoading = false;
  String _error = '';
  MonthlyTrendResponse? _response;

  static const Map<String, String> _paramLabels = {
    'aqi': 'AQI',
    'pm25': 'PM2.5',
    'pm10': 'PM10',
    'co': 'CO',
    'so2': 'SO2',
    'o3': 'O3',
    'no2': 'NO2',
    'temperature': 'Suhu',
    'humidity': 'Kelembaban',
  };

  static const Map<String, String> _paramUnits = {
    'aqi': '',
    'pm25': 'μg/m³',
    'pm10': 'μg/m³',
    'co': 'μg/m³',
    'so2': 'μg/m³',
    'o3': 'μg/m³',
    'no2': 'μg/m³',
    'temperature': '°C',
    'humidity': '%',
  };

  static const List<String> _paramOrder = [
    'aqi', 'pm25', 'pm10', 'co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2',
    'temperature', 'humidity',
  ];

  static const List<String> _monthNames = [
    'Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
    'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember',
  ];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final now = DateTime.now();
    _year = now.year;
    _month = now.month;
    _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadData() async {
    if (!mounted) return;
    final hadData = _response != null && _response!.hasAnyData;

    setState(() {
      _isLoading = !hadData;
      _error = '';
    });

    final result = await ApiService()
        .getMonthlyTrend(widget.roomId, year: _year, month: _month);

    if (!mounted) return;

    if (result is Map && result['success'] == true) {
      setState(() {
        _response = MonthlyTrendResponse.fromJson(
          Map<String, dynamic>.from(result),
        );
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } else {
      final errStr = (result is Map) ? result['error']?.toString() ?? '' : '';
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = Helpers.isNetworkError(errStr)
            ? 'Tidak dapat memuat trend bulanan karena server tidak dapat dihubungi.'
            : 'Gagal memuat trend bulanan.';
      });
      if (Helpers.isNetworkError(errStr)) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
      } else {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(context, 'Gagal memuat trend bulanan',
            isError: true);
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _changeMonth(int delta) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    int newMonth = _month + delta;
    int newYear = _year;

    if (newMonth < 1) {
      newMonth = 12;
      newYear -= 1;
    } else if (newMonth > 12) {
      newMonth = 1;
      newYear += 1;
    }

    final candidate = DateTime(newYear, newMonth);
    final current = DateTime(now.year, now.month);
    if (candidate.isAfter(current)) return;

    setState(() {
      _year = newYear;
      _month = newMonth;
    });
    await _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _pickMonthYear() async {
    final picked = await showModalBottomSheet<DateTime>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => _MonthYearPickerSheet(
        initialYear: _year,
        initialMonth: _month,
      ),
    );

    if (picked != null) {
      setState(() {
        _year = picked.year;
        _month = picked.month;
      });
      await _loadData();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, 3),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.bar_chart_rounded,
                  color: Colors.blue, size: 18),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              const Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  'TREND HARIAN BULANAN',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              IconButton(
                onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _loadData,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 20),
                tooltip: 'Refresh trend bulanan',
                splashRadius: 20,
                padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _buildMonthYearSelector(),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          _buildParameterChips(),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          if (_isLoading)
            const SizedBox(
              height: 350,
              child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
            )
          else if (_error.isNotEmpty)
            _buildErrorState()
          else if (_response == null || !_response!.hasAnyData)
            _buildEmptyState()
          else
            ..._buildAllCharts(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMonthYearSelector() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_left),
            onPressed: () => _changeMonth(-1),
            tooltip: 'Bulan sebelumnya',
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: InkWell(
              onTap: _pickMonthYear,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      '${_monthNames[_month - 1]} $_year',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
            onPressed: () => _changeMonth(1),
            tooltip: 'Bulan berikutnya',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildParameterChips() {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 40,
      child: ListView.builder(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: _paramOrder.length,
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final p = _paramOrder[index];
          final isSelected = _parameter == p;
          final color = ParameterColors.of(p);

          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () => setState(() => _parameter = p),
            child: Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6),
              padding:
                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                // ✅ Selected = warna parameter itu sendiri
                color: isSelected
                    ? color
                    : AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 8,
                    height: 8,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  Center(
                    child: Text(
                      _paramLabels[p] ?? p,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  List<Widget> _buildAllCharts() {
    final resp = _response!;
    final unit = _paramUnits[_parameter] ?? '';
    final label = _paramLabels[_parameter] ?? _parameter;

    final baseColor = ParameterColors.of(_parameter);

    return [
      _MonthlyBarChart(
        title: 'Tertinggi',
        subtitle: 'Nilai $label tertinggi dalam bulan ini',
        days: resp.days,
        parameter: _parameter,
        metric: 'max',
        unit: unit,
        color: baseColor,
      ),
      const SizedBox(height: 12),
      _MonthlyBarChart(
        title: 'Rata-rata',
        subtitle: 'Rata-rata nilai $label harian dalam bulan ini',
        days: resp.days,
        parameter: _parameter,
        metric: 'avg',
        unit: unit,
        color: baseColor,
      ),
      const SizedBox(height: 12),
      _MonthlyBarChart(
        title: 'Terendah',
        subtitle: 'Nilai $label terendah dari semua hari sejenis dalam bulan ini',
        days: resp.days,
        parameter: _parameter,
        metric: 'min',
        unit: unit,
        color: baseColor,
      ),
    ];
  }

  Widget _buildErrorState() {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 350, // ← samakan dengan tinggi chart di HistoryChart
      child: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 48, color: AppColors.textHint),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              _error,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12.5,
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                fontFamily: 'Inter',
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            ElevatedButton.icon(
              onPressed: _loadData,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 16),
              label: const Text('Coba Lagi'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

   Widget _buildEmptyState() {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 350, // ← samakan dengan tinggi chart di HistoryChart
      child: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.bar_chart_outlined,
                size: 48, color: AppColors.textHint),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              'Tidak ada data di ${_monthNames[_month - 1]} $_year',
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textHint),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// BAR CHART PER METRIK
// ============================================================
class _MonthlyBarChart extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final List<MonthlyDayData> days;
  final String parameter;
  final String metric;
  final String unit;
  final Color color;

  const _MonthlyBarChart({
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.days,
    required this.parameter,
    required this.metric,
    required this.unit,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final values = days
        .map((d) => d.values[parameter]?.byMetric(metric))
        .toList(growable: false);

    final maxVal = values
        .whereType<double>()
        .fold<double>(0.0, (a, b) => b > a ? b : a);
    final chartMax = maxVal > 0 ? maxVal * 1.15 : 1.0;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(10, 10, 10, 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.04),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.25)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 8,
                height: 8,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text(
                title,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  color: color,
                  fontFamily: 'Inter',
                ),
              ),
              if (unit.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(
                  '($unit)',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 10,
                    color: AppColors.textHint,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            subtitle,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 10.5,
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              fontFamily: 'Inter',
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          SizedBox(
            height: 140,
            child: LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, constraints) {
                final labelSpace = 30.0;
                final barAreaHeight = constraints.maxHeight - labelSpace;

                return Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                  children: List.generate(days.length, (i) {
                    final day = days[i];
                    final value = values[i];
                    final hasData = value != null;

                    final barHeight = hasData
                        ? (value / chartMax) * barAreaHeight
                        : 0.0;

                    return Expanded(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                          children: [
                            SizedBox(
                              height: 14,
                              child: FittedBox(
                                fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
                                child: Text(
                                  hasData
                                      ? _formatValue(value, parameter)
                                      : '—',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 9.5,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                    color: hasData
                                        ? AppColors.textPrimary
                                        : AppColors.textHint,
                                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 2),
                            Container(
                              height: hasData && barHeight > 4
                                  ? barHeight
                                  : 4,
                              width: double.infinity,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: hasData
                                    ? color
                                    : Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.3),
                                borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                                  top: Radius.circular(4),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            SizedBox(
                              height: 12,
                              child: FittedBox(
                                fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
                                child: Text(
                                  _dayShort(day.dayName),
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 9.5,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _formatValue(double v, String parameter) {
    if (parameter == 'aqi') return v.round().toString();
    if (parameter == 'humidity') return v.round().toString();
    if (parameter == 'co') return v.round().toString();
    return v.toStringAsFixed(1);
  }

  static String _dayShort(String dayName) {
    const map = {
      'Senin': 'Sen',
      'Selasa': 'Sel',
      'Rabu': 'Rab',
      'Kamis': 'Kam',
      'Jumat': 'Jum',
      'Sabtu': 'Sab',
      'Minggu': 'Min',
    };
    return map[dayName] ?? dayName;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MONTH/YEAR PICKER BOTTOM SHEET
// ============================================================
class _MonthYearPickerSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final int initialYear;
  final int initialMonth;

  const _MonthYearPickerSheet({
    required this.initialYear,
    required this.initialMonth,
  });

  @override
  State<_MonthYearPickerSheet> createState() => _MonthYearPickerSheetState();
}

class _MonthYearPickerSheetState extends State<_MonthYearPickerSheet> {
  late int _year;
  late int _month;

  static const List<String> _monthNames = [
    'Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
    'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember',
  ];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _year = widget.initialYear;
    _month = widget.initialMonth;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final minYear = now.year - 5;
    final maxYear = now.year;
    final years = List.generate(maxYear - minYear + 1, (i) => minYear + i);

    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(
        bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
      ),
      child: SafeArea(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 16),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 40,
                height: 4,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.grey.shade300,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Text(
                'Pilih Bulan & Tahun',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              DropdownButton<int>(
                value: _year,
                isExpanded: true,
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _year = v ?? _year),
                items: years
                    .map((y) => DropdownMenuItem(value: y, child: Text('$y')))
                    .toList(),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              GridView.count(
                crossAxisCount: 3,
                shrinkWrap: true,
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                childAspectRatio: 2.2,
                mainAxisSpacing: 8,
                crossAxisSpacing: 8,
                children: List.generate(12, (i) {
                  final m = i + 1;
                  final isSelected = m == _month;
                  final isFuture = (_year == now.year && m > now.month);

                  return GestureDetector(
                    onTap: isFuture
                        ? null
                        : () {
                            setState(() => _month = m);
                          },
                    child: Container(
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: isSelected
                            ? AppColors.primary
                            : (isFuture
                                ? Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.08)
                                : AppColors.surface),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                        border: Border.all(
                          color: isSelected
                              ? AppColors.primary
                              : AppColors.border,
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        _monthNames[i],
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12.5,
                          fontWeight:
                              isSelected ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
                          color: isSelected
                              ? Colors.white
                              : (isFuture
                                  ? AppColors.textDisabled
                                  : AppColors.textPrimary),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: OutlinedButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                      child: const Text('Batal'),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(
                        context,
                        DateTime(_year, _month),
                      ),
                      child: const Text('Terapkan'),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../data/models/room.dart';
import 'room_card.dart';

class BuildingSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final String buildingName;
  final List<Room> rooms;
  final Function(Room)? onRoomTap;
  
  const BuildingSection({
    super.key,
    required this.buildingName,
    required this.rooms,
    this.onRoomTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          
          // Building Header
// Building Header
Container(
  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
  decoration: BoxDecoration(
    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor.withOpacity(0.1),
    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
  ),
  child: IntrinsicHeight(
    child: Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch, 
      children: [
        // ✅ Ikon sekarang di tengah secara vertikal
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.center, // Ubah dari topLeft ke center
          child: const Icon(Icons.location_city, size: 20), // Padding dihapus
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        
        // Nama gedung (bisa wrap ke bawah)
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            buildingName,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        
        // Chip jumlah ruangan (tetap di tengah)
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.center, 
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 2),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Text(
              '${rooms.length} ruangan',
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: Colors.white,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  ),
),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          // Rooms List
          ListView.separated(
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            shrinkWrap: true,
            itemCount: rooms.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final room = rooms[index];
              return RoomCard(
                room: room,
                onTap: () {
                  if (onRoomTap != null) {
                    onRoomTap!(room);
                  }
                },
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

  Widget _buildMenuItem({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
  }) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon),
      title: Text(
        label,
        style: const TextStyle(
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
        ),
      ),
      onTap: onTap,
    );

lib/presentation/widgets/common/room_card.dart:

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../data/models/room.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/helpers.dart';

class RoomCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Room room;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const RoomCard({
    super.key,
    required this.room,
    this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final aqi = room.currentAQI;
    final aqiColor = Helpers.getAQIColor(aqi);
    final aqiLabel = Helpers.getAQILabel(aqi);
    final hasAnyData = room.hasAnyData;

    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 0),
      elevation: 2,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Header
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          room.name,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 18,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            color: Color(0xFF212529),
                          ),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 2),
                        Text(
                          room.buildingName,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            color: Color(0xFF6C757D),
                          ),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Container(
                    padding:
                        const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: aqiColor.withOpacity(0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      border: Border.all(color: aqiColor),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      aqiLabel,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: aqiColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // AQI Display — no_data & error jadi grey
              Container(
                width: double.infinity,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: aqiColor,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      aqi?.toString() ?? '—',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 64,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        color: Colors.white,
                        height: 0.9,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 20, vertical: 6),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        aqi == null ? 'TIDAK ADA DATA' : 'INDEKS KUALITAS UDARA',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          letterSpacing: 0.5,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 20),

              // Grid param dinamis — card yang null otomatis hilang
              GridView.count(
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                shrinkWrap: true,
                crossAxisCount: 3,
                mainAxisSpacing: 12,
                crossAxisSpacing: 12,
                childAspectRatio: 1.1,
                children: [
                  if (room.currentData.hasPM25)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'PM2.5',
                      value: room.currentData.pm25!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getPM25Status(room.currentData.pm25),
                      color: Helpers.getPM25Color(room.currentData.pm25),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasPM10)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'PM10',
                      value: room.currentData.pm10!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getPM10Status(room.currentData.pm10),
                      color: Helpers.getPM10Color(room.currentData.pm10),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasCO)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'CO',
                      value: room.currentData.co!.round().toString(),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getCOStatus(room.currentData.co),
                      color: Helpers.getCOColor(room.currentData.co),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasSO2)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'SO2',
                      value: room.currentData.so2!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getSO2Status(room.currentData.so2),
                      color: Helpers.getSO2Color(room.currentData.so2),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasO3)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'O3',
                      value: room.currentData.o3!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getO3Status(room.currentData.o3),
                      color: Helpers.getO3Color(room.currentData.o3),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasNO2)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'NO2',
                      value: room.currentData.no2!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getNO2Status(room.currentData.no2),
                      color: Helpers.getNO2Color(room.currentData.no2),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasTemperature)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'SUHU',
                      value: room.currentData.temperature!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: '°C',
                      status: Helpers.getTemperatureStatus(
                          room.currentData.temperature),
                      color: Helpers.getTemperatureColor(
                          room.currentData.temperature),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasHumidity)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'LEMBAB',
                      value: room.currentData.humidity!.round().toString(),
                      unit: '%',
                      status: Helpers.getHumidityStatus(
                          room.currentData.humidity),
                      color: Helpers.getHumidityColor(
                          room.currentData.humidity),
                    ),
                  _buildLastUpdateCell(room),
                ],
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 12),

              // Footer
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  if (room.isIot)
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFFE3F2FD),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Icon(Icons.sensors, size: 12, color: Colors.blue[700]),
                          const SizedBox(width: 4),
                          Text(
                            'Device IoT',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 10,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              color: Colors.blue[700],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    )
                  else
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFFF3E5F5),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Icon(Icons.auto_awesome,
                              size: 12, color: Colors.purple[700]),
                          const SizedBox(width: 4),
                          Text(
                            'Simulasi',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 10,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              color: Colors.purple[700],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  if (!hasAnyData && aqi == null)
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                      child: Text(
                        'Belum ada data dari perangkat',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          color: Colors.grey[600],
                          fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildParameterCell({
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required String unit,
    required String status,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(color: Colors.black12, blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 2)),
        ],
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.baseline,
              textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
              children: [
                Text(
                  value,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    color: Colors.white,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 2),
                Text(
                  unit,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 9,
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: Text(
                status.length > 10 ? '${status.substring(0, 10)}...' : status,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 9,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                maxLines: 1,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLastUpdateCell(Room room) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFF8F9FA),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFE9ECEF)),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        child: Builder(
          builder: (context) {
            final updateInfo =
                Helpers.formatCompactLastUpdate(room.currentData.updatedAt);
            final label = updateInfo['label'] ?? '';
            final time = updateInfo['time'] ?? '';
            final topLabel = label == 'HARI INI' ? 'UPDATE' : label;

            return Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  topLabel,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 10,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    color: Colors.grey[700],
                    letterSpacing: 0.3,
                  ),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  time,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    color: Color(0xFF212529),
                  ),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

lib/presentation/widgets/common/server_status_indicator.dart

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../core/services/server_status_service.dart';

class ServerStatusIndicator extends StatefulWidget {
  final Duration pollInterval;
  final double size;

  const ServerStatusIndicator({
    super.key,
    this.pollInterval = const Duration(seconds: 10),
    this.size = 10,
  });

  static Future<bool> checkNow() => ServerStatusService().checkNow();

  @override
  State<ServerStatusIndicator> createState() => _ServerStatusIndicatorState();
}

class _ServerStatusIndicatorState extends State<ServerStatusIndicator> {
  final ServerStatusService _service = ServerStatusService();
  Timer? _timer;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Check pertama kali instan
    _service.checkNow();
    // Polling berkala sebagai fallback (refresh button tetap jalan
    // lewat static helper).
    _timer = Timer.periodic(widget.pollInterval, (_) => _service.checkNow());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<bool?>(
      valueListenable: _service.status,
      builder: (context, value, _) {
        final Color color;
        if (value == null) {
          color = Colors.grey;
        } else if (value == true) {
          color = Colors.green;
        } else {
          color = Colors.red;
        }

        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6),
          child: Center(
            child: AnimatedContainer(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
              width: widget.size,
              height: widget.size,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: color.withOpacity(0.55),
                    blurRadius: 6,
                    spreadRadius: 1,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

function calculateISPU(conc, bps) {
  if (conc == null || isNaN(conc) || conc < 0) return 0;
  for (const bp of bps) {
    if (conc <= bp.concHigh) {
      const range = bp.concHigh - bp.concLow;
      if (range === 0) return bp.ispuHigh;
      const ispu = ((bp.ispuHigh - bp.ispuLow) / range) * (conc - bp.concLow) + bp.ispuLow;
      return Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(500, ispu)));
    }
  }
  return 500;
}

const calculateISPUFromPM25 = (pm25) => calculateISPU(pm25, PM25_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromPM10 = (pm10) => calculateISPU(pm10, PM10_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromCO   = (co)   => calculateISPU(co,   CO_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromSO2  = (so2)  => calculateISPU(so2,  SO2_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromO3   = (o3)   => calculateISPU(o3,   O3_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromNO2  = (no2)  => calculateISPU(no2,  NO2_BREAKPOINTS);
